-- Airflow UI from the supplied Insane_version_clean setup; existing farming callbacks retained.
-- V15 nearest-target priority + faster recovery pass.
-- V13 fast-next-cast pass: shorter reel confirmation and cast recovery windows.
-- V11 reliability pass: safer input release, cancellation-aware waits, and clearer input failures.
-- Based on V10; other farming systems are retained.
-- V7 quality pass: improved title dragging, independent wood range, bait countdown, and emergency stop-all control.
-- Unified Auto Fishing + Auto Mine/Harvest + Auto Wood Farmer
-- One GUI and one set of hotkeys; tree/wood targets are handled by the
-- shared harvesting loop, avoiding a duplicate AutoWoodGUI and duplicate loops.
-- Place in StarterPlayerScripts for your own Roblox experience.

    function catalog.FindPath(path, expectedClass)
        local ok, found = pcall(function()
            local parts = string.split(path, ".")
            local node = game:FindFirstChild(parts[1])
            for i = 2, #parts do
                node = node and node:FindFirstChild(parts[i])
                if not node then return nil end
            end
            if expectedClass and node and not node:IsA(expectedClass) then return nil end
            return node
        end)
        return ok and found or nil
    end
    function catalog.FindNamed(name, expectedClass)
        local base = "ReplicatedStorage.rbxts_include.node_modules.@rbxts.net.out"
        local exact = catalog.FindPath(base .. "._NetManaged." .. name, expectedClass)
        if exact then return exact end
        local out = catalog.FindPath(base)
        if not out then return nil end
        for _, child in ipairs(out:GetDescendants()) do
            if child.Name == name and child:IsA(expectedClass) then return child end
        end
        return nil
    end
    function catalog.FindLegacy(name)
        return catalog.FindPath("ReplicatedStorage.rbxts_include.node_modules.@rbxts.net.out.ULrMeokpcNr7uD." .. name, "RemoteFunction")
    end
    function catalog.RequestId(remoteName)
        local ok, id = pcall(function()
            local environment = type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv() or _G
            local recorder = environment.RebornCapture
            return recorder and recorder.RequestIds and recorder.RequestIds[remoteName]
        end)
        return ok and type(id) == "string" and id ~= "" and id or nil
    end
    catalog.VerifiedRequests = {
        OEzzgCCBzocxJw = "mining",
        ohy6O10QKzyGZE = "placement",
        raqRasAwcny1zh = "bait",
        RZQQNNDRUvfj1N = "hotbar",
    }
    function catalog.InvokeCaptured(remoteName, body)
        local action = catalog.VerifiedRequests[remoteName]
        if not action then return false, "No captured request format for this action" end
        local remote = catalog.FindLegacy(remoteName)
        if not remote then return false, action .. " remote unavailable", "unavailable" end
        local requestId = catalog.RequestId(remoteName)
        if not requestId then return false, "Perform " .. action .. " manually once with capture active first", "unavailable" end
        local ok, result = pcall(function()
            return remote:InvokeServer({
                TrNhTTgExIb1Bg = requestId,
                TlSR8Mzd9FLwaZ = body,
            })
        end)
        if not ok or result == false or type(result)=="table" and result.success==false then return false, result end
        return true, result
    end
    catalog.ModuleCache = {}
    catalog.ModuleErrors = {}
    function catalog.GameModule(path, export)
        if catalog.ModuleCache[path] then return catalog.ModuleCache[path][export] end
        local module = catalog.FindPath(path, "ModuleScript")
        if not module then catalog.ModuleErrors[path]="Module path not found";return nil end
        local ok, result = pcall(require, module)
        if (not ok or type(result) ~= "table") and type(getrenv) == "function" then
            local gotEnvironment, environment = pcall(getrenv)
            if gotEnvironment and type(environment) == "table" and type(environment.require) == "function" and environment.require ~= require then
                ok, result = pcall(environment.require, module)
            end
        end
        if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then catalog.ModuleErrors[path]="Module could not be loaded";return nil end
        catalog.ModuleErrors[path]=nil
        catalog.ModuleCache[path] = result
        return result[export]
    end
    function catalog.BaitRecorderValid(recorder)
        if not recorder then return false end
        if recorder.Version and recorder.Version<19 then return false end
        return not recorder.CanUseBaitBinding or recorder:CanUseBaitBinding()
    end
    function catalog.LiveRecorder(bindingName)
        local recorder=catalog.Recorder
        local binding=recorder and recorder[bindingName]
        local localValid=binding and binding.Remote and binding.Remote.Parent
            and (bindingName~="BaitBinding" or catalog.BaitRecorderValid(recorder))
            and (bindingName~="ChestBinding" or recorder.CanUseChestBinding and recorder:CanUseChestBinding())
        if bindingName~="BaitBinding" and localValid then return recorder end
        local ok,existing=pcall(function()
            local environment=type(getgenv)=="function" and getgenv() or _G
            return environment.RebornCapture
        end)
        local other=ok and existing and existing[bindingName]
        local otherValid=other and other.Remote and other.Remote.Parent
            and (bindingName~="BaitBinding" or catalog.BaitRecorderValid(existing))
            and (bindingName~="ChestBinding" or existing.CanUseChestBinding and existing:CanUseChestBinding())
        if otherValid and (not localValid or bindingName~="BaitBinding" or (other.ObservedAt or 0)>=(binding.ObservedAt or 0)) then return existing end
        if localValid then return recorder end
    end
    function catalog.PlacementClient()
        local requests = catalog.GameModule("ReplicatedStorage.TS.network.requests", "Requests")
        return requests and requests.Client
    end
    function catalog.PlacementAvailable()
        return catalog.PlacementClient() ~= nil or catalog.LiveRecorder("PlacementBinding") ~= nil or catalog.RequestId("ohy6O10QKzyGZE") ~= nil
    end
    function catalog.CallPlacement(body)
        local client = catalog.PlacementClient()
        if client then
            -- Use the game's documented request wrapper; it supplies its own session context.
            local ok, result = pcall(function() return client:Get("BLOCK_PLACE_REQUEST"):CallServer(body) end)
            if not ok or result == false or type(result) == "table" and result.success == false then return false, result end
            return true, result
        end
        local recorder = catalog.LiveRecorder("PlacementBinding")
        if recorder and recorder.Place then return recorder:Place(body) end
        return catalog.InvokeCaptured("ohy6O10QKzyGZE", body)
    end
    function catalog.GameEndpoint(name)
        local client = catalog.PlacementClient()
        if not client then return nil,catalog.ModuleErrors["ReplicatedStorage.TS.network.requests"] or "Requests.Client export unavailable" end
        local ok, endpoint = pcall(function() return client:Get(name) end)
        if not ok or not endpoint then return nil,"Request endpoint lookup failed" end
        return endpoint
    end
    function catalog.SendEndpoint(endpoint, body)
        local ok, result = pcall(function() return endpoint:CallServer(body) end)
        if not ok or result == false or type(result) == "table" and result.success == false then return false, result end
        return true, result
    end
    function catalog.HarvestEndpoint()
        return catalog.GameEndpoint("CLIENT_HARVEST_CROP_REQUEST")
    end
    function catalog.CallHarvest(target)
        local endpoint = catalog.HarvestEndpoint()
        if endpoint then return catalog.SendEndpoint(endpoint, {model = target, player = player}) end
        local recorder = catalog.LiveRecorder("HarvestBinding")
        local binding = recorder and recorder.HarvestBinding
        if binding and binding.Remote.Parent and recorder.Harvest then return recorder:Harvest(target) end
        if catalog.InputHarvest then return catalog.InputHarvest(target) end
        return false, "Harvest API unavailable; harvest one crop manually with capture active"
    end
    function catalog.AcceptFreshBaitBinding()
        local recorder=catalog.LiveRecorder("BaitBinding")
        local binding=recorder and recorder.BaitBinding
        if catalog.BaitNeedsRefresh and binding and binding~=catalog.BaitFailedBinding then
            catalog.BaitNeedsRefresh=false
            catalog.BaitFailedBinding=nil
        end
        return recorder
    end
    function catalog.CallBait(tool, prepared)
        catalog.BaitAttemptedBinding=nil
        -- The game's wrapper supplies the current session envelope for food use.
        -- CLIENT_EAT_FOOD and its tool body are present in the supplied remote captures.
        local failed=catalog.BaitNativeFailed
        if not failed or failed.Character~=player.Character or failed.Tool~=tool then
            local endpoint,unavailable=catalog.GameEndpoint("CLIENT_EAT_FOOD")
            if endpoint then
                catalog.BaitTransport="game food request"
                local ok,reason=catalog.SendEndpoint(endpoint,{tool=tool})
                catalog.BaitNativeStatus=ok and "Food request sent; awaiting consumption" or "Food request rejected or errored"
                if not ok then catalog.BaitNativeFailed={Character=player.Character,Tool=tool} end
                return ok,reason
            end
            catalog.BaitNativeStatus="Food API unavailable: "..tostring(unavailable or "endpoint not found")
        end
        local recorder=catalog.AcceptFreshBaitBinding()
        if catalog.BaitNeedsRefresh and catalog.ActivateBait then
            catalog.BaitTransport="normal input (refreshing request)"
            return catalog.ActivateBait(tool,prepared)
        end
        recorder=recorder or catalog.Recorder
        if not catalog.BaitRecorderValid(recorder) then recorder=nil end
        if not (recorder and recorder.BaitBinding and recorder.Bait) then
            local ok, existing = pcall(function()
                local environment = type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv() or _G
                return environment.RebornCapture
            end)
            if ok and existing and existing.BaitBinding and existing.Bait and catalog.BaitRecorderValid(existing) then recorder = existing end
        end
        if recorder and recorder.BaitBinding and recorder.Bait then
            local remote = recorder.BaitBinding.Remote
            if not remote or remote.Parent then
                catalog.BaitTransport="learned request "..tostring(remote and remote.Name or "unknown")
                catalog.BaitAttemptedBinding=recorder.BaitBinding
                local ok,result=recorder:Bait(tool)
                catalog.BaitLearnedStatus=ok and "Returned without rejection; consumption required" or "Request rejected or errored"
                return ok,result
            end
            -- A removed captured remote sends nothing; normal tool activation is still safe.
        end
        local ok, reason, state = catalog.InvokeCaptured("raqRasAwcny1zh", {tool = tool})
        if state == "unavailable" and catalog.ActivateBait then
            catalog.BaitTransport="normal tool input"
            return catalog.ActivateBait(tool, prepared)
        end
        return ok, reason
    end
    function catalog.ServerReplantEnabled()
        local recorder=catalog.LiveRecorder("AutoReplantBinding")
        if recorder and type(recorder.AutoReplantState)=="boolean" then return recorder.AutoReplantState end
        return catalog.ServerAutoReplant == true
    end
    function catalog.CallFruit(model,fruit)
        local recorder=catalog.LiveRecorder("FruitBinding")
        if recorder and recorder.Fruit then return recorder:Fruit(model,fruit) end
        return false,"Pick one fruit manually with capture active to learn its current call"
    end
    function catalog.SetAutoReplant(value)
        local recorder=catalog.LiveRecorder("AutoReplantBinding")
        if not recorder or not recorder.SetAutoReplant then return false,"Change AutoReplant manually with capture active","unavailable" end
        local ok,result=recorder:SetAutoReplant(value)
        if ok then catalog.ServerAutoReplant=value end
        return ok,result
    end
    function catalog.CallMine(target, category)
        local recorder=catalog.LiveRecorder(category == "wood" and "WoodHitBinding" or "MineHitBinding")
        if recorder and recorder.Hit then return recorder:Hit(target,category) end
        local endpoint = catalog.GameEndpoint("BLOCK_HIT_REQUEST")
        if endpoint then return catalog.SendEndpoint(endpoint, {block = target}) end
        local ok, reason, state = catalog.InvokeCaptured("OEzzgCCBzocxJw", {block = target})
        -- Only use normal tool input when no request was sent. A rejected call must not be duplicated.
        if state == "unavailable" and catalog.InputMine then return catalog.InputMine(target) end
        return ok, reason
    end
    function catalog.CallHotbar(layout)
        local client = catalog.PlacementClient()
        if client then
            local ok, result = pcall(function() return client:Get("CLIENT_HOTBAR_LAYOUT_UPDATE"):CallServer({layout = layout}) end)
            if not ok or result == false or type(result) == "table" and result.success == false then return false, result end
            return true, result
        end
        local recorder=catalog.LiveRecorder("HotbarBinding")
        if recorder and recorder.Hotbar then return recorder:Hotbar(layout) end
        return catalog.InvokeCaptured("RZQQNNDRUvfj1N", {layout = layout})
    end
    function catalog.WoodHotbarLayout()
        local recorder=catalog.LiveRecorder("HotbarBinding")
        local layout=recorder and recorder.HotbarLayout
        if type(layout)~="string" then return nil end
        for slot in string.gmatch(layout,"[^,]+") do
            local name=string.lower(slot)
            if string.find(name,"axe",1,true) and not string.find(name,"pickaxe",1,true) then return layout end
        end
    end
    function catalog.OwnedTools()
        local tools, seen = {}, {}
        local function add(tool)
            if typeof(tool) == "Instance" and tool:IsA("Tool") and not seen[tool] then
                seen[tool] = true
                table.insert(tools, tool)
            end
        end
        local character = player.Character
        local backpack = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        for _, container in ipairs({character, backpack}) do
            if container then for _, tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do add(tool) end end
        end
        local service = catalog.GameModule("ReplicatedStorage.TS.inventory.inventory-service", "InventoryService")
        if service then
            local ok, inventory = pcall(function() return service.getPlayerInventory(player) end)
            if ok and inventory then
                for _, tool in ipairs(inventory.allTools or {}) do add(tool) end
            end
        end
        return tools
    end
    function catalog.EquipTool(tool)
        local character = player.Character
        if not character or player:GetAttribute("ToolsLocked") == true then return false end
        local loading = player:FindFirstChild("SessionLoading")
        if loading and loading.Value then return false end
        if tool.Parent == character then return true end
        local service = catalog.GameModule("Players." .. player.Name .. ".PlayerScripts.TS.inventory.client-inventory-service", "ClientInventoryService")
        if service then
            local ok = pcall(function()
                local slot
                for index, item in pairs(service.hotbar or {}) do if item == tool then slot = index; break end end
                -- Slot zero is valid. Selecting an already selected slot would unequip it.
                if slot ~= nil and service.selectedHotbarIndex ~= slot then service:setSelectedHotbarIndex(slot)
                else service:equipTool(tool) end
            end)
            return ok and tool.Parent == character
        end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        return humanoid ~= nil and pcall(function() humanoid:EquipTool(tool) end)
    end
    function catalog.SeedNames(cropName)
        local meta = catalog.GameModule("ReplicatedStorage.TS.tool.tool-meta", "ToolMeta")
        local names = { cropName }
        if meta then
            for name, info in pairs(meta) do
                if type(info) == "table" and type(info.cropSeed) == "table" and info.cropSeed.cropName == cropName then
                    table.insert(names, type(info.name) == "string" and info.name or name)
                end
            end
        end
        return names
    end
    function catalog.Refresh()
        local found = 0
        for _, entry in ipairs(catalog.Entries) do
            entry.Instance = catalog.FindPath(entry.Path, entry.Class)
            -- A renamed grouping folder may still contain the exact named remote.
            if not entry.Instance and string.find(entry.Path, ".out._NetManaged.", 1, true) then
                entry.Instance = catalog.FindNamed(string.match(entry.Path, "([^.]+)$"), entry.Class)
            end
            if entry.Instance then found += 1 end
        end
        return found
    end
    return catalog
end)()
-- REMOTE CATALOG END

-- EMBEDDED CAPTURE BEGIN
-- Generated from tools/CaptureRemotes.lua by scripts/embed-capture.mjs.
function RemoteCatalog.StartCapture()
-- Reborn remote recorder: records calls; never replays or sends extra requests.
-- Requires host-provided hookmetamethod/getnamecallmethod (not Studio APIs).
-- F8 pause/resume | F9 print + save capture | F10 stop recording.
-- Perform ONE manual mining/harvest/plant/fishing action at a time.
if type(hookmetamethod) ~= "function" or type(getnamecallmethod) ~= "function" then
    warn("[Reborn capture] This host cannot intercept outgoing calls. In Studio, instrument your own server handlers or client call sites instead.")
    return
end
local environment = type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv() or _G
local previous = environment.RebornCapture
if previous and previous.Version == 26 and previous.Resume then
    previous:Resume()
    print("[Reborn capture] Existing recorder reused; " .. #previous.Records .. " calls retained. F9 exports.")
    return previous
end
if previous and previous.Stop then previous:Stop() end
local capture = { Version = 26, Active = true, Stopped = false, Records = {}, MaxCalls = 100, RequestIds = {} }
if previous then
    capture.Records = previous.Records or capture.Records
    capture.RequestIds = previous.RequestIds or capture.RequestIds
    capture.HarvestBinding = previous.HarvestBinding
    capture.BaitBinding = previous.BaitBinding
    capture.PlacementBinding = previous.PlacementBinding
    capture.WoodHitBinding = previous.WoodHitBinding
    capture.MineHitBinding = previous.MineHitBinding
    capture.AutoReplantBinding = previous.AutoReplantBinding
    capture.ChestBinding = previous.ChestBinding
    capture.ChestBindings = previous.ChestBindings or (previous.ChestBinding and {previous.ChestBinding} or {})
    capture.FruitBinding = previous.FruitBinding
    capture.HotbarBinding = previous.HotbarBinding
    capture.HotbarLayout = previous.HotbarLayout
    capture.AutoReplantState = previous.AutoReplantState
    capture.Active = #capture.Records < capture.MaxCalls
end
capture.ChestBindings=capture.ChestBindings or {}
local exactNames = {
    OEzzgCCBzocxJw = true, hFuV0Z1w601mXP = true,
    RZQQNNDRUvfj1N = true, ohy6O10QKzyGZE = true, raqRasAwcny1zh = true,
    T5XIZMc0DxnCFt = true, -- Most recent rotated block-hit endpoint.
    OAVO4xgiY252ym = true, -- Updated axe/block-hit endpoint.
    Gu0tNi2YAOdeYv = true, -- Updated hotbar-layout capture.
    Y6oBjV1u9qIAAS = true, -- Fruit-picking capture.
    nrGQVrxpfb3dKr = true, -- AutoReplant setting endpoint.
    qn1PInnZJmfDd1 = true, -- Supplied axe/block-hit capture.
    u71Y03otNIwj9g = true, -- Supplied updated placement endpoint.
    yrIxXd1d5bBQji = true,
    GDbJa5Ix6UZ8mg = true,
    wKCeyGAO4SqdMY = true,
    XPQExfDjDSUkYv = true, -- Supplied bait capture (rotating envelope).
    UctIHHAfsCxxzj = true,
    COtnYCOPUThadM = true, -- Supplied crop-harvest capture (new network group).
}
local function wanted(remote)
    if typeof(remote) ~= "Instance" then return false end
    if not remote:IsA("RemoteFunction") and not remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then return false end
    if exactNames[remote.Name] then return true end
    local name = string.lower(remote.Name)
    for _, part in ipairs({"fish", "harvest", "block_hit_request", "block_place_request", "hotbar_layout", "trim_tree", "replant", "bait"}) do
        if string.find(name, part, 1, true) then return true end
    end
    return false
end
local function instancePath(instance)
    if instance == game then return "game", true end
    local path, cursor = {}, instance
    while cursor and cursor ~= game do
        table.insert(path, 1, cursor.Name)
        cursor = cursor.Parent
    end
    if cursor ~= game then
        -- Metadata is evidence, not a guessed replacement for the actual Instance.
        local fields = {
            "Name = " .. string.format("%q", instance.Name),
            "ClassName = " .. string.format("%q", instance.ClassName),
        }
        local ok, debugId = pcall(function() return instance:GetDebugId(0) end)
        if ok then table.insert(fields, "DebugId = " .. string.format("%q", debugId)) end
        local positioned, position = pcall(function()
            if instance:IsA("BasePart") then return instance.Position end
            if instance:IsA("Model") then return instance:GetPivot().Position end
        end)
        if positioned and position then
            table.insert(fields, string.format("Position = Vector3.new(%.17g, %.17g, %.17g)", position.X, position.Y, position.Z))
        end
        return "DetachedInstance({" .. table.concat(fields, ", ") .. "})", false
    end
    local expression = "game"
    for _, name in ipairs(path) do
        expression ..= ":FindFirstChild(" .. string.format("%q", name) .. ")"
    end
    return expression, true
end
-- SERIALIZER BEGIN (also exercised by the local test suite)
local function serialize(value, seen, budget, depth)
    budget = budget or { count = 0, complete = true }
    seen = seen or {}
    depth = depth or 0
    budget.count += 1
    if depth > 20 or budget.count > 500 then
        budget.complete = false
        return "nil --[[capture limit]]", budget.complete
    end
    local kind = typeof(value)
    if kind == "nil" or kind == "boolean" then return tostring(value), budget.complete end
    if kind == "number" then
        if value ~= value then return "(0/0)", budget.complete end
        if value == math.huge then return "math.huge", budget.complete end
        if value == -math.huge then return "-math.huge", budget.complete end
        return string.format("%.17g", value), budget.complete
    end
    if kind == "string" then
        if #value > 4096 then budget.complete = false end
        return string.format("%q", string.sub(value, 1, 4096)), budget.complete
    end
    if kind == "Instance" then
        local path, complete = instancePath(value)
        if not complete then budget.complete = false end
        return path, budget.complete
    end
    if kind == "Vector3" then
        return string.format("Vector3.new(%.17g, %.17g, %.17g)", value.X, value.Y, value.Z), budget.complete
    end
    if kind == "Vector2" then
        return string.format("Vector2.new(%.17g, %.17g)", value.X, value.Y), budget.complete
    end
    if kind == "CFrame" then
        local components = { value:GetComponents() }
        for i, component in ipairs(components) do components[i] = string.format("%.17g", component) end
        return "CFrame.new(" .. table.concat(components, ", ") .. ")", budget.complete
    end
    if kind == "Color3" then
        return string.format("Color3.new(%.17g, %.17g, %.17g)", value.R, value.G, value.B), budget.complete
    end
    if kind == "EnumItem" then return tostring(value), budget.complete end
    if kind == "table" then
        if seen[value] then budget.complete = false; return "nil --[[cyclic table]]", false end
        seen[value] = true
        local parts = {}
        for key, item in next, value do
            if budget.count > 500 then budget.complete = false; break end
            local encodedKey = serialize(key, seen, budget, depth + 1)
            local encodedValue = serialize(item, seen, budget, depth + 1)
            table.insert(parts, "[" .. encodedKey .. "] = " .. encodedValue)
        end
        seen[value] = nil
        return "{" .. table.concat(parts, ", ") .. "}", budget.complete
    end
    budget.complete = false
    return "nil --[[unsupported: " .. kind .. "]]", false
end
-- SERIALIZER END
function capture:Dump()
    local lines = { "-- Reborn outgoing call capture. Reference only; do not execute this file." }
    for _, record in ipairs(self.Records) do table.insert(lines, record) end
    return table.concat(lines, "\n\n")
end
function capture:Export()
    local output = self:Dump()
    print(output)
    if type(writefile) == "function" and type(isfile) == "function" then
        local prefix = "Reborn-remotes-" .. tostring(os.time())
        for i = 1, 100 do
            local path = prefix .. "-" .. i .. ".lua"
            local ok, saved = pcall(function()
                if isfile(path) then return false end
                writefile(path, output)
                return true
            end)
            if ok and saved then print("[Reborn capture] Saved " .. path); return path end
            if not ok then break end
        end
    end
    warn("[Reborn capture] File export unavailable; copy the capture from the console.")
end
function capture:CopyAll()
    if #self.Records == 0 then
        return false, "Ingen kald optaget endnu. Udfør handlingerne manuelt først."
    end
    local output = self:Dump()
    local copy = type(setclipboard) == "function" and setclipboard
        or (type(toclipboard) == "function" and toclipboard)
    if copy then
        local ok = pcall(copy, output)
        if ok then return true, string.format("Alle %d optagne kald er kopieret. Send teksten her.", #self.Records) end
    end
    self:Export()
    return true, "Udklipsholder utilgængelig. Alle kald er eksporteret til fil/konsol."
end
function capture:Stop()
    self.Active, self.Stopped = false, true
    self.LearnWoodOnly = false
    self.LearnBaitOnly = false
    self.LearnChestOnly = false
    self.LearnChestMany = false
    -- Retain the single hotkey connection so this same recorder can be resumed.
    -- It ignores input while stopped; no extra hooks or listeners are installed.
    -- Leave our hook in pass-through mode; restoring it could overwrite another hook.
end
function capture:Resume()
    self.Stopped = false
    self.Active = #self.Records < self.MaxCalls
end
-- Learn only a concrete model-target request observed during manual harvesting.
local function cloneArguments(value, oldTarget, newTarget, seen, budget)
    if oldTarget and value == oldTarget then return newTarget end
    if typeof(value) ~= "table" then return value end
    seen, budget = seen or {}, budget or { count = 0 }
    if seen[value] then return seen[value] end
    budget.count += 1
    assert(budget.count <= 500, "request snapshot too large")
    local copy = {}
    seen[value] = copy
    for key, item in next, value do copy[key] = cloneArguments(item, oldTarget, newTarget, seen, budget) end
    return copy
end
local function learnHotbar(remote,method,args)
    if method~="InvokeServer" or type(args[1])~="table" then return false end
    if remote.Name~="Gu0tNi2YAOdeYv" and remote.Name~="RZQQNNDRUvfj1N" and not string.find(string.lower(remote.Name),"hotbar_layout",1,true) then return false end
    local packet=args[1]
    local key=type(packet.P1ZFEJgXWM4k62)=="table" and "P1ZFEJgXWM4k62"
        or (type(packet.wGquKmyB3mV2Cf)=="table" and "wGquKmyB3mV2Cf"
        or (type(packet.TlSR8Mzd9FLwaZ)=="table" and "TlSR8Mzd9FLwaZ" or nil))
    local body=key and packet[key] or packet
    if type(body.layout)~="string" then return false end
    capture.HotbarBinding={Remote=remote,Method=method,Args=cloneArguments(args),BodyKey=key}
    capture.HotbarLayout=body.layout
    return true
end
function capture:Hotbar(layout)
    local binding=self.HotbarBinding
    if not binding or not binding.Remote.Parent or type(layout)~="string" then return false,"Change hotbar manually once with capture active" end
    local ok,result=pcall(function()
        local args=cloneArguments(binding.Args)
        local body=binding.BodyKey and args[1][binding.BodyKey] or args[1]
        body.layout=layout
        return binding.Remote[binding.Method](binding.Remote,table.unpack(args,1,args.n))
    end)
    if not ok or result==false or type(result)=="table" and result.success==false then return false,result end
    self.HotbarLayout=layout
    return true,result
end
local function learnFruit(remote, method, args)
    if remote.Name~="Y6oBjV1u9qIAAS" or method~="InvokeServer" or type(args[1])~="table" then return false end
    local body=args[1].wGquKmyB3mV2Cf
    if type(body)~="table" or typeof(body.model)~="Instance" or typeof(body.fruit)~="Instance" or typeof(body.player)~="Instance" then return false end
    capture.FruitBinding={Remote=remote,Method=method,Args=cloneArguments(args),Model=body.model,Fruit=body.fruit}
    return true
end
function capture:Fruit(model,fruit)
    local binding=self.FruitBinding
    if not binding or not binding.Remote.Parent then return false,"Pick fruit manually once with capture active" end
    if typeof(model)~="Instance" or typeof(fruit)~="Instance" or not fruit:IsDescendantOf(model) then return false,"Fruit no longer belongs to this tree" end
    local ok,result=pcall(function()
        local args=cloneArguments(binding.Args,binding.Model,model)
        args=cloneArguments(args,binding.Fruit,fruit)
        return binding.Remote[binding.Method](binding.Remote,table.unpack(args,1,args.n))
    end)
    if not ok or result==false or type(result)=="table" and result.success==false then return false,result end
    return true,result
end
local function learnAutoReplant(remote, method, args)
    if remote.Name~="nrGQVrxpfb3dKr" or method~="InvokeServer" or type(args[1])~="table" then return false end
    local body=args[1].wGquKmyB3mV2Cf
    if type(body)~="table" or body.name~="AutoReplant" or type(body.value)~="boolean" then return false end
    capture.AutoReplantBinding={Remote=remote,Method=method,Args=cloneArguments(args)}
    return true,body.value
end
function capture:SetAutoReplant(value)
    local binding=self.AutoReplantBinding
    if type(value)~="boolean" or not binding or not binding.Remote.Parent then return false,"Change AutoReplant manually once with capture active" end
    local ok,result=pcall(function()
        local args=cloneArguments(binding.Args)
        args[1].wGquKmyB3mV2Cf.value=value
        return binding.Remote[binding.Method](binding.Remote,table.unpack(args,1,args.n))
    end)
    if not ok or result==false or type(result)=="table" and result.success==false then return false,result end
    self.AutoReplantState=value
    return true,result
end
local function learnBlockHit(remote, method, args)
    if method~="InvokeServer" or type(args[1])~="table" then return false end
    local known=remote.Name=="T5XIZMc0DxnCFt" or remote.Name=="OAVO4xgiY252ym" or remote.Name=="qn1PInnZJmfDd1" or remote.Name=="OEzzgCCBzocxJw"
        or string.find(string.lower(remote.Name),"block_hit_request",1,true)
    local packet=args[1]
    local body=packet
    -- Body/context field names rotate independently of the remote's name.
    for _,value in pairs(packet) do
        if type(value)=="table" and typeof(value.block)=="Instance" then body=value;break end
    end
    local block=body.block
    if typeof(block)~="Instance" or not (block:IsA("Model") or block:IsA("BasePart")) then return false end
    local name=string.lower(block.Name)
    local wood=false
    for _,word in ipairs({"tree","wood","log","oak","pine","birch","palm"}) do
        if string.find(name,word,1,true) then wood=true;break end
    end
    if not known then
        local group=remote.Parent
        if not group or not group.Parent or group.Parent.Name~="out" then return false end
        if not wood then
            local mineral=false
            for _,word in ipairs({"rock","stone","ore","iron","coal","gold","copper","mineral","crystal"}) do
                if string.find(name,word,1,true) then mineral=true;break end
            end
            if not mineral then return false end
        end
        local player=game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        local character=player and player.Character
        local tool=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        local toolName=tool and string.lower(tool.Name) or ""
        local pick=string.find(toolName,"pickaxe",1,true)~=nil
        local axe=not pick and string.find(toolName,"axe",1,true)~=nil
        if not (wood and axe or not wood and pick) then return false end
    end
    local key=wood and "WoodHitBinding" or "MineHitBinding"
    capture[key]={Remote=remote,Method=method,Args=cloneArguments(args),Target=block}
    return true
end
function capture:Hit(block, category)
    local binding=self[category=="wood" and "WoodHitBinding" or "MineHitBinding"]
    if not binding or not binding.Remote.Parent then return false,"Hit a matching resource manually with capture active" end
    local ok,result=pcall(function()
        local args=cloneArguments(binding.Args,binding.Target,block)
        return binding.Remote[binding.Method](binding.Remote,table.unpack(args,1,args.n))
    end)
    if not ok or result==false or type(result)=="table" and result.success==false then return false,result end
    return true,result
end
local function learnPlacement(remote, method, args)
    if method ~= "InvokeServer" or type(args[1]) ~= "table" then return false end
    if remote.Name ~= "u71Y03otNIwj9g" and remote.Name ~= "ohy6O10QKzyGZE"
        and not string.find(string.lower(remote.Name), "block_place_request", 1, true) then return false end
    local packet = args[1]
    local key = type(packet.wGquKmyB3mV2Cf) == "table" and "wGquKmyB3mV2Cf"
        or (type(packet.TlSR8Mzd9FLwaZ) == "table" and "TlSR8Mzd9FLwaZ" or nil)
    local body = key and packet[key] or packet
    if typeof(body.cframe) ~= "CFrame" or type(body.blockType) ~= "string" then return false end
    capture.PlacementBinding = {Remote=remote,Method=method,Args=cloneArguments(args),BodyKey=key}
    return true
end
function capture:Place(body)
    local binding = self.PlacementBinding
    if not binding or not binding.Remote.Parent then return false, "Place one block manually with capture active" end
    if typeof(body.cframe) ~= "CFrame" or type(body.blockType) ~= "string" then return false, "Invalid placement body" end
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        local args = cloneArguments(binding.Args)
        local packet = binding.BodyKey and args[1][binding.BodyKey] or args[1]
        packet.cframe, packet.blockType = body.cframe, body.blockType
        return binding.Remote[binding.Method](binding.Remote,table.unpack(args,1,args.n))
    end)
    if not ok or result == false or type(result) == "table" and result.success == false then return false, result end
    return true, result
end
local function learnBait(remote, method, args)
    if method ~= "InvokeServer" or type(args[1]) ~= "table" then return false end
    local known=remote.Name=="yrIxXd1d5bBQji" or remote.Name=="GDbJa5Ix6UZ8mg" or remote.Name=="wKCeyGAO4SqdMY" or remote.Name=="XPQExfDjDSUkYv" or remote.Name=="raqRasAwcny1zh"
        or string.find(string.lower(remote.Name),"bait",1,true)
    local group=remote.Parent
    if not known and (not group or not group.Parent or group.Parent.Name~="out") then return false end
    local packet=args[1]
    local body=packet
    for _,value in pairs(packet) do
        if type(value)=="table" and typeof(value.tool)=="Instance" then body=value;break end
    end
    local tool = body.tool
    if typeof(tool) ~= "Instance" or not tool:IsA("Tool") or string.lower(tool.Name) ~= "bait" then return false end
    local owner=game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    if not owner or not owner.Character then return false end
    capture.BaitBinding = {Remote=remote,Method=method,Args=cloneArguments(args),Target=tool,
        OwnerUserId=owner.UserId,OwnerCharacter=owner.Character,JobId=game.JobId,ObservedAt=os.clock()}
    return true
end
function capture:CanUseBaitBinding()
    local binding=self.BaitBinding
    local owner=game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    return binding~=nil and binding.Remote~=nil and binding.Remote.Parent~=nil and owner~=nil
        and binding.OwnerCharacter==owner.Character and owner.Character~=nil
        and binding.OwnerUserId==owner.UserId and binding.JobId==game.JobId
end
function capture:Bait(tool)
    local binding = self.BaitBinding
    if not self:CanUseBaitBinding() then return false, "Use bait once on this account and character to learn its current request" end
    if typeof(tool) ~= "Instance" or not tool:IsA("Tool") or string.lower(tool.Name) ~= "bait" then return false, "Bait tool unavailable" end
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        local args = cloneArguments(binding.Args, binding.Target, tool)
        return binding.Remote[binding.Method](binding.Remote, table.unpack(args,1,args.n))
    end)
    if not ok or result == false or type(result) == "table" and result.success == false then return false, result end
    return true, result
end
-- Learn observed item withdrawals; never infer items or rotating envelope keys.
local function learnChest(remote,method,args)
    if method~="InvokeServer" or type(args[1])~="table" then return false end
    local group=remote.Parent
    if remote.Name~="kAnpiAlDrqMuEh" and (not group or not group.Parent or group.Parent.Name~="out") then return false end
    local body=args[1]
    for _,value in pairs(body) do
        if type(value)=="table" and value.action=="withdraw" then body=value;break end
    end
    if body.action~="withdraw" or (type(body.item)~="string" or #body.item==0 or #body.item>128) or typeof(body.chest)~="Instance"
        or not (body.chest:IsA("Model") or body.chest:IsA("BasePart")) then return false end
    if type(body.slot)~="number" or body.slot<0 or body.slot%1~=0
        or type(body.amount)~="number" or body.amount<=0 or body.amount%1~=0 then return false end
    local owner=game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    if not owner or not owner.Character then return false end
    local binding={Remote=remote,Method=method,Args=cloneArguments(args),Chest=body.chest,
        Slot=body.slot,Amount=body.amount,Item=body.item,OwnerUserId=owner.UserId,OwnerCharacter=owner.Character,JobId=game.JobId,ObservedAt=os.clock()}
    capture.ChestBinding=binding
    local replaced=false
    for i,old in ipairs(capture.ChestBindings) do
        if old.Chest==binding.Chest and old.Slot==binding.Slot then capture.ChestBindings[i]=binding;replaced=true;break end
    end
    if not replaced then table.insert(capture.ChestBindings,binding) end
    return true
end
function capture:CanUseChestBinding(binding)
    local b=binding or self.ChestBinding
    local owner=game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    return b~=nil and b.Remote~=nil and b.Remote.Parent~=nil and b.Chest~=nil and b.Chest.Parent~=nil
        and owner~=nil and owner.Character~=nil and b.OwnerCharacter==owner.Character
        and b.OwnerUserId==owner.UserId and b.JobId==game.JobId
end
function capture:GetChestBindings()
    local result={}
    for _,binding in ipairs(self.ChestBindings) do
        if self:CanUseChestBinding(binding) then table.insert(result,binding) end
    end
    return result
end
-- Input/Output chests in the supplied clip have twelve slots. Include the
-- boundary index while the capture has not established zero/one-based indexing.
function capture:MakeChestTarget(binding,chest,slot)
    if not self:CanUseChestBinding(binding) then return nil end
    if typeof(chest)~="Instance" or not chest.Parent or not (chest:IsA("Model") or chest:IsA("BasePart")) or chest.Name~=binding.Chest.Name
        or string.lower(chest.Name)~="chestmediumindustrialio" then return nil end
    if type(slot)~="number" or slot<0 or slot>12 or slot%1~=0 then return nil end
    local target={}
    for key,value in pairs(binding) do target[key]=value end
    target.Args=cloneArguments(binding.Args,binding.Chest,chest)
    target.Chest=chest;target.Slot=slot;target.Generated=true
    return target
end
function capture:WithdrawCarrots(amount,binding)
    if not self:CanUseChestBinding(binding) then return false,"Learn an item withdrawal on this character first" end
    local b=binding or self.ChestBinding
    amount=amount==nil and b.Amount or amount
    if type(amount)~="number" or amount<=0 or amount%1~=0 or amount>1000000 then return false,"Choose a positive whole withdrawal amount" end
    local args=cloneArguments(b.Args)
    local body=args[1]
    if type(body)~="table" then return false,"Learn this chest withdrawal again" end
    if body.chest~=b.Chest then
        for _,value in pairs(body) do
            if type(value)=="table" and value.chest==b.Chest and value.action=="withdraw" then body=value;break end
        end
    end
    if body.chest~=b.Chest or body.action~="withdraw" or body.item~=(b.Item or "carrotHarvested") then return false,"Learn this chest withdrawal again" end
    -- Apply the selected slot and count to the cloned body; retain the learned session envelope.
    body.amount=amount
    body.slot=b.Slot
    local ok,result=pcall(function() return b.Remote[b.Method](b.Remote,table.unpack(args,1,args.n)) end)
    if not ok or result==false or type(result)=="table" and result.success==false then return false,result end
    return true,result
end
capture.WithdrawItems=capture.WithdrawCarrots
local function learnHarvest(remote, method, args)
    if args.n < 1 or type(args[1]) ~= "table" then return false end
    local packet = args[1]
    local body = type(packet.wGquKmyB3mV2Cf) == "table" and packet.wGquKmyB3mV2Cf
        or type(packet.a1tIHk1Kl9GiE5) == "table" and packet.a1tIHk1Kl9GiE5
        or (type(packet.TlSR8Mzd9FLwaZ) == "table" and packet.TlSR8Mzd9FLwaZ or packet)
    if type(packet.H3ZxjzDRyU5tvI)=="table" then body=packet.H3ZxjzDRyU5tvI end
    local target = body.model
    if typeof(target) ~= "Instance" or not (target:IsA("Model") or target:IsA("BasePart")) then return false end
    local name = string.lower(target.Name)
    local crop = false
    for _, word in ipairs({"wheat", "berry", "carrot", "potato", "corn", "tomato", "pumpkin", "melon", "cabbage", "lettuce", "flower"}) do
        if string.find(name, word, 1, true) then crop = true; break end
    end
    if not crop then return false end
    if not string.find(string.lower(remote.Name), "harvest", 1, true) and remote.Name ~= "hFuV0Z1w601mXP" and remote.Name ~= "COtnYCOPUThadM" and remote.Name ~= "UctIHHAfsCxxzj" then return false end
    capture.HarvestBinding = { Remote = remote, Method = method, Args = cloneArguments(args), Target = target }
    return true
end
function capture:Harvest(target)
    local binding = self.HarvestBinding
    if not binding or not binding.Remote.Parent then return false, "Harvest a crop manually once first" end
    if binding.Target:IsA("Model") ~= target:IsA("Model") then return false, "Crop target type differs from the learned request" end
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        local args = cloneArguments(binding.Args, binding.Target, target)
        return binding.Remote[binding.Method](binding.Remote, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
    end)
    if not ok or result == false or type(result) == "table" and result.success == false then return false, result end
    return true, result
end
local original
local function intercept(remote, ...)
    if capture.Stopped then return original(remote, ...) end
    local method = getnamecallmethod()
    if capture.LearnWoodOnly or capture.LearnBaitOnly or capture.LearnChestOnly then
        if method == "InvokeServer" then
            local args = table.pack(...)
            pcall(function()
                if typeof(remote)=="Instance" and remote:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                    if capture.LearnWoodOnly then
                        local previousWood=capture.WoodHitBinding
                        learnBlockHit(remote,method,args)
                        local learnedWood=capture.WoodHitBinding
                        if learnedWood and learnedWood~=previousWood and learnedWood.Remote and learnedWood.Remote.Parent then capture.LearnWoodOnly=false end
                    end
                    if capture.LearnBaitOnly and learnBait(remote,method,args) then capture.LearnBaitOnly=false end
                    if capture.LearnChestOnly and learnChest(remote,method,args) and not capture.LearnChestMany then capture.LearnChestOnly=false end
                    if not capture.LearnWoodOnly and not capture.LearnBaitOnly and not capture.LearnChestOnly then capture:Stop() end
                end
            end)
        end
        return original(remote,...)
    end
    local autoReplantValue
    if not capture.Stopped and (capture.Active or #capture.Records >= capture.MaxCalls)
        and (method == "InvokeServer" or method == "FireServer") then
        local args = table.pack(...)
        -- Recorder failures must never cancel or replace the original game call.
        pcall(function()
            local learned = false
            if typeof(remote) == "Instance" and (remote:IsA("RemoteFunction") or remote:IsA("RemoteEvent")) then
                learned = learnHotbar(remote, method, args) or learnFruit(remote, method, args)
                if not learned then learned,autoReplantValue=learnAutoReplant(remote, method, args) end
                learned = learned or learnChest(remote,method,args) or learnBlockHit(remote, method, args) or learnPlacement(remote, method, args) or learnBait(remote, method, args) or learnHarvest(remote, method, args)
            end
            if not learned and not wanted(remote) then return end
            -- Keep the observed per-remote request ID in memory for this session.
            -- Never substitute IDs between different remote names.
            if exactNames[remote.Name] and type(args[1]) == "table"
                and type(args[1].TrNhTTgExIb1Bg) == "string" then
                capture.RequestIds[remote.Name] = args[1].TrNhTTgExIb1Bg
            end
            -- Keep learning live context after the log fills, without adding more records.
            if not capture.Active then return end
            local budget = { count = 0, complete = true }
            local path, validPath = instancePath(remote)
            if not validPath then budget.complete = false end
            local encoded = {}
            for i = 1, args.n do encoded[i] = serialize(args[i], {}, budget) end
            local record = string.format("-- Call %d | %s | t=%.3f | %s\n%s:%s(%s)",
                #capture.Records + 1, remote.Name, os.clock(), budget.complete and "COMPLETE" or "INCOMPLETE",
                path, method, table.concat(encoded, ", "))
            if #record > 65536 then record = "-- INCOMPLETE: call too large to capture" end
            table.insert(capture.Records, record)
            print(record)
            if #capture.Records >= capture.MaxCalls then
                capture.Active = false
                warn("[Reborn capture] 100-call limit reached. Press F9 to export.")
            end
        end)
    end
    if autoReplantValue~=nil then
        -- Observing a setting request does not prove the server accepted it.
        -- Snapshot its value before the original call can yield or mutate the packet.
        -- Do not catch original errors, and retain every return (including nils).
        local returned=table.pack(original(remote,...))
        pcall(function()
            local response=returned[1]
            if response~=false and not (type(response)=="table" and response.success==false) then
                capture.AutoReplantState=autoReplantValue
            end
        end)
        return table.unpack(returned,1,returned.n)
    end
    return original(remote, ...)
end
local installed, result = pcall(function()
    return hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", type(newcclosure) == "function" and newcclosure(intercept) or intercept)
end)
if not installed or type(result) ~= "function" then
    warn("[Reborn capture] Could not install recorder: " .. tostring(result))
    return
end
original = result
environment.RebornCapture = capture
capture.Connection = game:GetService("UserInputService").InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
    if processed or capture.Stopped then return end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F8 then
        if #capture.Records < capture.MaxCalls then capture.Active = not capture.Active end
        print("[Reborn capture] Recording: " .. tostring(capture.Active))
    elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F9 then
        capture:Export()
    elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F10 then
        capture:Stop()
        print("[Reborn capture] Stopped. Run RebornCapture:Export() to export, or rerun the recorder to resume.")
    end
end)
print("[Reborn capture] Ready. Do ONE action manually. F8 pause, F9 export, F10 stop.")

return capture
end
-- EMBEDDED CAPTURE END

-- EMBEDDED FARM LOGIC BEGIN
-- Generated by scripts/embed-farm.mjs.
local FarmLogic = (function()
-- Shared targeting and confirmation rules; no server calls or input injection.
local FarmLogic = {}
local cropNames = {"wheat", "berry", "carrot", "potato", "corn", "tomato", "pumpkin", "melon", "cabbage", "lettuce", "flower", "crop", "bush"}
local woodNames = {"tree", "wood", "log", "oak", "pine", "birch", "palm"}
local mineNames = {"rock", "stone", "ore", "iron", "coal", "gold", "copper", "tin", "silver", "crystal", "gem", "mineral"}
local readiness = {"Mature", "IsMature", "FullyGrown", "Harvestable", "ReadyToHarvest"}
local function contains(name, patterns)
    for _, pattern in ipairs(patterns) do
        if string.find(name, pattern, 1, true) then return true end
    end
    return false
end
function FarmLogic.Category(name)
    name = string.lower(name)
    if contains(name, {"sapling", "seedling", "seed", "harvested"}) then return nil end
    if contains(name, cropNames) then return "crop" end
    if contains(name, woodNames) then return "wood" end
    if contains(name, mineNames) then return "mine" end
    return nil
end
function FarmLogic.Fruits(model)
    local fruits={}
    if FarmLogic.Category(model.Name)~="wood" then return fruits end
    for _,stage in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
        if string.match(stage.Name,"^stage%-%d+$") then
            local fruit=stage:FindFirstChild("Fruit")
            if fruit and FarmLogic.IsReady(fruit) then table.insert(fruits,fruit) end
        end
    end
    return fruits
end
function FarmLogic.CropMatches(name, selected)
    local wanted = string.lower(selected)
    if wanted == "berrybush" then wanted = "berry" end
    return FarmLogic.Category(name) == "crop" and string.find(string.lower(name), wanted, 1, true) ~= nil
end
FarmLogic.CropOptions={"wheat","carrot","potato","corn","tomato","pumpkin","melon","cabbage","lettuce","berryBush"}
function FarmLogic.NormalizeCrops(selection, fallback)
    local chosen={}
    if type(selection)=="table" then
        for key,value in pairs(selection) do
            local name=type(key)=="number" and value or value==true and key
            if type(name)=="string" and table.find(FarmLogic.CropOptions,name) then chosen[name]=true end
        end
    end
    if not next(chosen) then chosen[table.find(FarmLogic.CropOptions,fallback) and fallback or "wheat"]=true end
    return chosen
end
function FarmLogic.SelectedCrops(selection,fallback)
    local names={}
    for _,name in ipairs(FarmLogic.CropOptions) do
        if type(selection)=="table" and selection[name] then table.insert(names,name) end
    end
    if #names==0 then table.insert(names,fallback or "wheat") end
    return names
end
function FarmLogic.CropsMatch(name,selection,fallback)
    for _,crop in ipairs(FarmLogic.SelectedCrops(selection,fallback)) do
        if FarmLogic.CropMatches(name,crop) then return true end
    end
    return false
end
function FarmLogic.ReplantType(name,selection,fallback)
    for _,crop in ipairs(FarmLogic.SelectedCrops(selection,fallback)) do
        if FarmLogic.CropMatches(name,crop) then return crop end
    end
    return fallback
end
function FarmLogic.Range(category, config)
    if category == "wood" then return config.WoodRange end
    if category == "crop" then return config.CropRange end
    return config.MineRange
end
function FarmLogic.Snapshot(target)
    local values = {}
    for _, name in ipairs(readiness) do values[name] = target:GetAttribute(name) end
    return values
end
function FarmLogic.IsReady(target)
    for _, name in ipairs(readiness) do
        if target:GetAttribute(name) == false then return false end
    end
    return true -- Unknown growth schema: do not invent a numeric maturity threshold.
end
function FarmLogic.Confirmed(target, before, prompt)
    if not target.Parent then return true end
    for _, name in ipairs(readiness) do
        if before[name] == true and target:GetAttribute(name) == false then return true end
    end
    return prompt ~= nil and (not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled)
end
function FarmLogic.HarvestPrompt(target)
    for _, child in ipairs(target:GetDescendants()) do
        if child:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and child.Enabled then
            local text = string.lower(child.Name .. " " .. child.ActionText)
            if contains(text, {"harvest", "høst", "collect", "pick"}) then return child end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
function FarmLogic.HoldPrompt(prompt, active, waitFor, now)
    local began = pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldBegin() end)
    if not began then pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldEnd() end); return false end
    local held = pcall(function()
        local deadline = now() + prompt.HoldDuration
        while active() and now() < deadline do waitFor(0.05) end
    end)
    local released = pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldEnd() end)
    return held and released and active()
end
return FarmLogic
end)()
-- EMBEDDED FARM LOGIC END

-- EMBEDDED AUTO BUILD BEGIN
-- Generated by scripts/embed-build.mjs.
local AutoBuild=(function()
-- Blueprint planning and single-flight placement controller for the standalone hub.
local AutoBuild = {}
function AutoBuild.Plan(preset, width, length, height)
    width, length, height = math.floor(width), math.floor(length), math.floor(height)
    assert(width >= 1 and width <= 12 and length >= 1 and length <= 12 and height >= 1 and height <= 8, "Use sizes 1–12 and height 1–8")
    local cells = {}
    local function add(x,y,z,role)
        table.insert(cells, {x=x-math.floor(width/2),y=y,z=z+1,role=role or "main"})
        assert(#cells <= 300, "Plan exceeds 300 blocks; reduce its dimensions")
    end
    if preset == "Platform" then
        for z=0,length-1 do for x=0,width-1 do add(x,0,z) end end
    elseif preset == "Farm plots" then
        for z=0,length-1 do
            for x=0,width-1 do add(x,1,z,x%3==2 and "path" or "main") end
        end
    elseif preset == "Wall" then
        for y=1,height do for x=0,width-1 do add(x,y,0) end end
    elseif preset == "Stairs" then
        for z=0,length-1 do
            for y=1,z+1 do for x=0,width-1 do add(x,y,z) end end
        end
    else error("Unknown blueprint") end
    table.sort(cells,function(a,b)
        if a.y ~= b.y then return a.y < b.y end
        if a.z ~= b.z then return a.z < b.z end
        return a.x < b.x
    end)
    return cells
end
-- Plain-data blueprints are validated before use or persistence.
function AutoBuild.Validate(data)
    assert(type(data)=="table" and data.Version==1,"Unsupported blueprint version")
    assert(type(data.Name)=="string" and #data.Name>0 and #data.Name<=48,"Use a blueprint name of 1–48 bytes")
    assert(type(data.BlockSize)=="number" and data.BlockSize>=1 and data.BlockSize<=20,"Invalid blueprint block size")
    assert(type(data.Cells)=="table" and #data.Cells>=1 and #data.Cells<=300,"Blueprint must contain 1–300 blocks")
    local count=0
    for index in pairs(data.Cells) do
        assert(type(index)=="number" and index%1==0 and index>=1 and index<=#data.Cells,"Invalid blueprint cell list")
        count+=1
    end
    assert(count==#data.Cells,"Blueprint cell list contains gaps")
    local result={Version=1,Name=data.Name,BlockSize=data.BlockSize,Cells={}}
    local seen={}
    for index,cell in ipairs(data.Cells) do
        assert(type(cell)=="table" and type(cell.Material)=="string" and #cell.Material>0 and #cell.Material<=128,"Invalid block material")
        assert(type(cell.Components)=="table" and #cell.Components==12,"Invalid block transform")
        local c={}
        for i=1,12 do
            local n=cell.Components[i]
            assert(type(n)=="number" and n==n and math.abs(n)<=(i<=3 and 3000 or 1.001),"Invalid block coordinates")
            c[i]=n
        end
        -- Rotation rows must form an orthonormal, right-handed basis.
        for row=0,2 do
            local a=4+row*3
            assert(math.abs(c[a]^2+c[a+1]^2+c[a+2]^2-1)<.002,"Invalid block rotation")
            for other=row+1,2 do
                local b=4+other*3
                assert(math.abs(c[a]*c[b]+c[a+1]*c[b+1]+c[a+2]*c[b+2])<.002,"Invalid block rotation")
            end
        end
        local determinant=c[4]*(c[8]*c[12]-c[9]*c[11])-c[5]*(c[7]*c[12]-c[9]*c[10])+c[6]*(c[7]*c[11]-c[8]*c[10])
        assert(math.abs(determinant-1)<.002,"Invalid block rotation")
        local key=string.format("%.3f,%.3f,%.3f",c[1],c[2],c[3])
        assert(not seen[key],"Duplicate block position");seen[key]=true
        result.Cells[index]={Material=cell.Material,Components=c}
    end
    table.sort(result.Cells,function(a,b)
        local x,y=a.Components,b.Components
        if x[2]~=y[2] then return x[2]<y[2] end
        if x[3]~=y[3] then return x[3]<y[3] end
        return x[1]<y[1]
    end)
    return result
end
function AutoBuild.new(ctx)
    local self={Preset="Farm plots",Width=5,Length=6,Height=3,Rotation=0,Token=0,Running=false,Cells=nil,PreviewFolder=nil,OffsetX=0,OffsetY=0,OffsetZ=0,Library={},Slot=1,CaptureRange=30,Persistence={}}
    local inputs,buttons={},{}
    local space=ctx.Workspace or workspace
    local scheduler=ctx.Scheduler or task
    local now=ctx.Now or os.clock
    local function status(message)
        local p=self.Progress
        local counts=self.Running and p and p.State~="Finished" and string.format("\nRun: %d confirmed · %d skipped · %d failed",p.Placed,p.Skipped,p.Failed) or ""
        self.Status.Text=tostring(message)..counts
    end
    function self:ClearPreview()
        if self.PreviewFolder then self.PreviewFolder:Destroy();self.PreviewFolder=nil end
        self.Cells=nil;self.BuildReport=nil;self.LayerIndex=nil
        if buttons.Layer then buttons.Layer.Text="Build: All layers" end
        if self.MaterialSummary then self.MaterialSummary.Text="Preview or load a blueprint to see its material counts." end
    end
    function self:Stop()
        self.Token+=1;self.Running=false
        buttons.Start.Text=self.Cells and "Resume build" or "Start build"
        if self.Progress then self.Progress.State="Stopped" end
        local p=self.Progress
        status("Stopped. Remaining blocks were not sent."..(p and string.format(" %d confirmed, %d skipped, %d failed; %d planned.",p.Placed,p.Skipped,p.Failed,p.Total) or ""))
    end
    local function characterRoot()
        local char=ctx.Player.Character
        return char, char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    local function blockSize()
        local util=ctx.GameModule("ReplicatedStorage.TS.util.block-utils","BlockUtils")
        return util and type(util.BLOCK_SIZE)=="number" and util.BLOCK_SIZE or 3
    end
    local function occupied(cf,size)
        local util=ctx.GameModule("ReplicatedStorage.TS.util.block-utils","BlockUtils")
        if util and type(util.getBlocksFromLocation)=="function" then
            local ok,blocks=pcall(function() return util.getBlocksFromLocation(cf.Position,{},1,true) end)
            if ok and type(blocks)=="table" then return #blocks>0 end
        end
        local params=OverlapParams.new()
        params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        local char=ctx.Player.Character
        local ignored={}
        if char then table.insert(ignored,char) end
        if self.PreviewFolder then table.insert(ignored,self.PreviewFolder) end
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances=ignored
        for _,part in ipairs(space:GetPartBoundsInBox(cf,Vector3.new(size-.2,size-.2,size-.2),params)) do
            if part.CanCollide then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    function self:Layers()
        local heights,seen={},{}
        for _,cell in ipairs(self.Cells or {}) do
            local y=cell.LayerY
            if y==nil then
                local ok,value=pcall(function() return cell.CFrame.Position.Y end)
                if ok then y=value end
            end
            if type(y)~="number" or y~=y or math.abs(y)==math.huge then return nil end
            local key=math.round(y*1000)/1000
            if not seen[key] then seen[key]=true;table.insert(heights,key) end
        end
        table.sort(heights)
        return heights
    end
    function self:SelectedCells()
        if not self.LayerIndex then return self.Cells or {} end
        local heights=self:Layers()
        local height=heights and heights[self.LayerIndex]
        if height==nil then return {} end
        local selected={}
        for _,cell in ipairs(self.Cells or {}) do
            local y=cell.LayerY
            if y==nil then y=cell.CFrame.Position.Y end
            if math.round(y*1000)/1000==height then table.insert(selected,cell) end
        end
        return selected
    end
    function self:CycleLayer()
        if self.Running or self.WorkerToken then status("Wait for the build worker before changing layers.");return false end
        if not self.Cells then status("Preview a blueprint before selecting a layer.");return false end
        local heights=self:Layers()
        if not heights or #heights==0 then status("Layer information is unavailable; create a new preview.");return false end
        local index=(self.LayerIndex or 0)+1
        self.LayerIndex=index<=#heights and index or nil
        self.BuildReport=nil
        local selected=self:SelectedCells()
        local included={}
        for _,cell in ipairs(selected) do included[cell]=true end
        for _,cell in ipairs(self.Cells) do
            if cell.Preview then cell.Preview.Transparency=included[cell] and .65 or .94 end
        end
        buttons.Layer.Text=self.LayerIndex and ("Build: Layer "..self.LayerIndex.."/"..#heights.." · "..#selected.." blocks") or ("Build: All layers · "..#selected.." blocks")
        self:UpdateMaterials(selected)
        status("Selected "..#selected.." of "..#self.Cells.." blocks. Faint preview blocks are excluded. Save preview still saves the full layout.")
        return true
    end
    function self:Preview()
        if self.Running then status("Stop the build before changing its plan.");return false end
        self:ClearPreview()
        local char,root=characterRoot()
        if not root then status("Wait for your character to spawn.");return false end
        local ok,plan
        if self.SavedPlan then ok,plan=pcall(AutoBuild.Validate,self.SavedPlan)
        else ok,plan=pcall(AutoBuild.Plan,self.Preset,self.Width,self.Length,self.Height) end
        if not ok then status(plan);return false end
        local material=inputs.Material.Text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        local pathMaterial=inputs.Path.Text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        if not self.SavedPlan and (material=="" or self.Preset=="Farm plots" and pathMaterial=="") then status("Choose bed/build and path materials.");return false end
        local params=RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances={char}
        local ground=space:Raycast(root.Position,Vector3.new(0,-40,0),params)
        if not ground then status("Stand on solid ground before previewing.");return false end
        local size=blockSize()
        local look=root.CFrame.LookVector
        local yaw=math.round(math.atan2(-look.X,-look.Z)/(math.pi/2))*(math.pi/2)+math.rad(self.Rotation)
        local anchor=CFrame.new(math.round(root.Position.X/size)*size,math.round((ground.Position.Y-size/2)/size)*size,math.round(root.Position.Z/size)*size)*CFrame.Angles(0,yaw,0)
        anchor=anchor*CFrame.new(self.OffsetX*size,self.OffsetY*size,-self.OffsetZ*size)
        self.Anchor=anchor
        local definitions=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Blocks")
        if definitions and self.SavedPlan then
            for _,cell in ipairs(plan.Cells) do
                if not definitions:FindFirstChild(cell.Material) then status("Unknown saved block: "..cell.Material);return false end
            end
        elseif definitions then
            if not definitions:FindFirstChild(material) then status("Unknown build block: "..material);return false end
            if self.Preset=="Farm plots" and not definitions:FindFirstChild(pathMaterial) then status("Unknown path block: "..pathMaterial);return false end
        end
        if self.SavedPlan and math.abs(plan.BlockSize-size)>.001 then status("Saved blueprint uses a different block size.");return false end
        local folder=Instance.new("Folder");folder.Name="RebornBuildPreview";folder.Parent=space
        self.PreviewFolder=folder
        self.Cells={}
        for _,cell in ipairs(self.SavedPlan and plan.Cells or plan) do
            local cf=anchor*(self.SavedPlan and CFrame.new(table.unpack(cell.Components)) or CFrame.new(cell.x*size,cell.y*size,-cell.z*size))
            local part=Instance.new("Part")
            part.Name="PlannedBlock";part.Anchored=true;part.CanCollide=false;part.CanTouch=false;part.CanQuery=false
            part.Size=Vector3.new(size-.15,size-.15,size-.15);part.CFrame=cf
            part.Transparency=.65;part.Material=Enum.Material.ForceField
            part.Color=cell.role=="path" and Color3.fromRGB(210,180,100) or Color3.fromRGB(100,160,255)
            part.Parent=folder
            table.insert(self.Cells,{CFrame=cf,Material=self.SavedPlan and cell.Material or cell.role=="path" and pathMaterial or material,Preview=part,LayerY=self.SavedPlan and cell.Components[2] or cell.y*size})
        end
        self.BlockSize=size
        self:UpdateMaterials(self:SelectedCells())
        status(string.format("Preview: %s, %d blocks. Blue = build/beds; gold = paths.",self.Preset,#self.Cells))
        return true
    end
    function self:Move(dx,dy,dz)
        if self.Running then status("Stop the build before moving its blueprint.");return false end
        if not self.Cells and not self:Preview() then return false end
        local anchor=self.Anchor*CFrame.new(dx*self.BlockSize,dy*self.BlockSize,-dz*self.BlockSize)
        local delta=anchor*self.Anchor:Inverse()
        for _,cell in ipairs(self.Cells) do
            cell.CFrame=delta*cell.CFrame
            if cell.Preview then cell.Preview.CFrame=cell.CFrame end
        end
        self.Anchor=anchor;self.BuildReport=nil
        self:UpdateMaterials(self:SelectedCells())
        self.OffsetX+=dx;self.OffsetY+=dy;self.OffsetZ+=dz
        self.MoveLabel.Text=string.format("Offset: right %d / up %d / forward %d blocks",self.OffsetX,self.OffsetY,self.OffsetZ)
        status("Blueprint moved. Start builds the visible preview positions.")
        return true
    end
    function self:Start()
        if self.Running then return false end
        if self.WorkerToken then status("Wait for the previous build request to finish before resuming.");return false end
        if not self.Cells and not self:Preview() then return end
        local cells=self:SelectedCells()
        if #cells==0 then status("No blocks selected. Choose All layers or create a new preview.");return false end
        self.Token+=1
        local token=self.Token
        self.WorkerToken=token
        self.Progress={Placed=0,Skipped=0,Failed=0,Total=#cells,Index=0,State="Building"}
        self.Running=true;buttons.Start.Text="Building…"
        if self.MoveMenu then self.MoveMenu.Visible=false end
        local function active() return self.Running and self.Token==token and ctx.Alive() end
        scheduler.spawn(function()
            local placed,skipped,failed,streak=0,0,0,0
            local ok,err=pcall(function()
                for index,cell in ipairs(cells) do
                    if not active() then return end
                    self.Progress.Index=index
                    local existing=occupied(cell.CFrame,self.BlockSize)
                    if existing then
                        skipped+=1
                    else
                        local _,root=characterRoot()
                        local reach=ctx.GameModule("ReplicatedStorage.Shared.SharedConfig","CLIENT_REACH")
                        if type(reach)~="number" then reach=20 end
                        while active() and (not root or (root.Position-cell.CFrame.Position).Magnitude>reach) do
                            self.Progress.State="Waiting for reach"
                            status(string.format("Move within %d studs of block %d/%d, or press Stop.",reach,index,#cells))
                            scheduler.wait(.2);_,root=characterRoot()
                            if active() then existing=occupied(cell.CFrame,self.BlockSize) end
                            if existing then break end
                        end
                        if not active() then return end
                        if existing or occupied(cell.CFrame,self.BlockSize) then
                            skipped+=1
                        else
                            self.Progress.State="Building"
                            status(string.format("Building %d/%d: %s",index,#cells,cell.Material))
                            local sent,reason=ctx.Place({cframe=cell.CFrame,blockType=cell.Material})
                            if not active() then return end
                            local confirmed=false
                            if sent then
                                local deadline=now()+1
                                repeat
                                    if not active() then return end
                                    confirmed=occupied(cell.CFrame,self.BlockSize)
                                    if confirmed then break end
                                    scheduler.wait(.05)
                                until now()>=deadline
                            end
                            if confirmed then placed+=1;streak=0
                            else failed+=1;streak+=1 end
                            self.Progress.Placed=placed;self.Progress.Skipped=skipped;self.Progress.Failed=failed
                            if streak>=3 then
                                self.Progress.State="Paused after failures"
                                status("Build paused after 3 failures: "..tostring(reason or "no block appeared")..". Check materials, support and island access.")
                                return
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    self.Progress.Placed=placed;self.Progress.Skipped=skipped;self.Progress.Failed=failed
                    if cell.Preview then cell.Preview:Destroy();cell.Preview=nil end
                    scheduler.wait(.2)
                end
                if active() then self.Progress.State="Finished";status(string.format("Finished: %d confirmed, %d occupied/skipped, %d failed.",placed,skipped,failed)) end
            end)
            if self.WorkerToken==token then self.WorkerToken=nil end
            if self.Token==token then
                self.Running=false;buttons.Start.Text="Start build"
                if not ok then self.Progress.State="Error";status("Build stopped: "..tostring(err)) end
            end
        end)
    end
    function self:UpdateMaterials(cells)
        local counts,names={},{}
        for _,cell in ipairs(cells or {}) do
            if not counts[cell.Material] then counts[cell.Material]=0;table.insert(names,cell.Material) end
            counts[cell.Material]+=1
        end
        table.sort(names)
        local lines={"Planned materials (availability not checked):"}
        for index,name in ipairs(names) do
            if index>5 then table.insert(lines,"More material types: "..(#names-5));break end
            table.insert(lines,name.." ×"..counts[name])
        end
        if self.MaterialSummary then self.MaterialSummary.Text=table.concat(lines,"\n") end
        return counts
    end
    function self:StoreBlueprint(data,slot)
        if self.Running or self.WorkerToken then status("Wait for the build worker to finish before changing saved slots.");return false end
        if slot==nil then slot=self.Slot end
        if type(slot)~="number" or slot%1~=0 or slot<1 or slot>5 then status("Choose a slot from 1 to 5.");return false end
        local ok,copy=pcall(AutoBuild.Validate,data)
        if not ok then status(copy);return false end
        self.Library[slot]=copy
        local persisted=false
        if ctx.WriteBlueprint then
            local ran,result=pcall(ctx.WriteBlueprint,slot,copy)
            persisted=ran and result==true
        end
        self.Persistence[slot]=persisted and "File + session" or "Session only"
        self:RefreshLibrary(false)
        status("Saved slot "..slot..": "..copy.Name.." ("..#copy.Cells.." blocks). "..(persisted and "Saved to file." or "Session only; file saving is unavailable or failed."))
        return true
    end
    function self:InspectSlot(slot)
        local data=self.Library[slot]
        if data then return data,self.Persistence[slot] or "Session" end
        local function unavailable(reason) self.Persistence[slot]=reason;return nil,reason end
        if not ctx.ReadBlueprint then return unavailable("No file access") end
        local ran,result=pcall(ctx.ReadBlueprint,slot)
        if not ran then return unavailable("Unavailable file") end
        if result==nil then return unavailable("No saved data") end
        local valid,copy=pcall(AutoBuild.Validate,result)
        if not valid then return unavailable("Invalid file") end
        self.Library[slot]=copy;self.Persistence[slot]="Loaded from file"
        return copy,"Loaded from file"
    end
    function self:RefreshLibrary(readFiles)
        local lines={"SAVED SLOTS"}
        for slot=1,5 do
            local data,source
            if readFiles then data,source=self:InspectSlot(slot)
            else data=self.Library[slot];source=self.Persistence[slot] or "Not inspected" end
            table.insert(lines,(slot==self.Slot and "→ " or "")..slot..": "..(data and (data.Name.." · "..#data.Cells.." blocks · "..source) or source))
        end
        if self.LibrarySummary then self.LibrarySummary.Text=table.concat(lines,"\n") end
        local selected=self.Library[self.Slot]
        buttons.Slot.Text="Slot "..self.Slot.." / 5"..(selected and (": "..selected.Name) or "")
        return table.concat(lines,"\n")
    end
    function self:CheckMaterials()
        if self.Running or self.WorkerToken then status("Finish or stop the current build request before checking materials.");return false end
        if not self.Cells then status("Preview at the intended position before checking materials.");return false end
        local cells=self:SelectedCells()
        if #cells==0 then status("No blocks selected. Choose All layers or refresh the preview.");return false end
        local pending,skipped,reachable={},0,0
        local _,root=characterRoot()
        local reach=ctx.GameModule("ReplicatedStorage.Shared.SharedConfig","CLIENT_REACH")
        if type(reach)~="number" then reach=20 end
        local ok,err=pcall(function()
            for _,cell in ipairs(cells) do
                if occupied(cell.CFrame,self.BlockSize) then skipped+=1
                else
                    pending[cell.Material]=(pending[cell.Material] or 0)+1
                    if root and (root.Position-cell.CFrame.Position).Magnitude<=reach then reachable+=1 end
                end
            end
        end)
        if not ok then status("Could not check world occupancy: "..tostring(err));return false end
        local names,total={},0
        for material,amount in pairs(pending) do table.insert(names,material);total+=amount end
        table.sort(names)
        local lines={"BUILD CHECK · snapshot",#cells.." selected of "..#self.Cells.." planned · "..skipped.." occupied · "..total.." remaining",reachable.." remaining cells currently in reach"}
        for _,material in ipairs(names) do
            local count
            if ctx.ItemCount then local ran,value=pcall(ctx.ItemCount,material);if ran then count=value end end
            local valid=type(count)=="number" and count>=0 and count%1==0 and count<=9007199254740991
            table.insert(lines,material..": need "..pending[material]..(valid and (", owned "..count..", short "..math.max(0,pending[material]-count)) or ", owned quantity unknown"))
        end
