        table.insert(lines,"Occupied cells may contain a different block. Materials, support and permissions remain server-controlled.")
        self.BuildReport=table.concat(lines,"\n")
        self.MaterialSummary.Text=self.BuildReport
        status("Build check ready. No blocks were placed. Refresh after inventory or world changes.")
        return {Remaining=total,Occupied=skipped,Reachable=reachable,Materials=pending,Selected=#cells,Excluded=#self.Cells-#cells}
    end
    function self:RenameSlot(name)
        if self.Running or self.WorkerToken then status("Wait for the build worker before renaming a slot.");return false end
        local data=self:InspectSlot(self.Slot)
        if not data then status("Load or save a valid selected slot before renaming it.");return false end
        local copy=AutoBuild.Validate(data)
        copy.Name=type(name)=="string" and name:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
        return self:StoreBlueprint(copy)
    end
    function self:CopySlot(target)
        if self.Running or self.WorkerToken then status("Wait for the build worker before copying a slot.");return false end
        if type(target)~="number" or target%1~=0 or target<1 or target>5 then status("Choose a copy target from 1 to 5.");return false end
        if target==self.Slot then status("Choose a copy target different from the source slot.");return false end
        local data=self:InspectSlot(self.Slot)
        if not data then status("Select a valid saved source slot before copying it.");return false end
        return self:StoreBlueprint(data,target)
    end
    function self:ReplaceMaterial(from,to)
        if self.Running or self.WorkerToken then status("Wait for the build worker before replacing materials.");return false end
        if not self.SavedPlan then status("Load a saved blueprint before replacing its materials.");return false end
        from=type(from)=="string" and from:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
        to=type(to)=="string" and to:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
        if from=="" or to=="" or #from>128 or #to>128 or from==to then status("Enter different source and replacement block names.");return false end
        local definitions=ctx.BlockDefinitions and ctx.BlockDefinitions()
        if not definitions or not definitions:FindFirstChild(to) then status("Replacement block is unknown or definitions are unavailable.");return false end
        local copy=AutoBuild.Validate(self.SavedPlan)
        local changed=0
        for _,cell in ipairs(copy.Cells) do
            if cell.Material==from then cell.Material=to;changed+=1 end
        end
        if changed==0 then status("No blocks named "..from.." in the loaded blueprint.");return false end
        self.MaterialUndo={Plan=AutoBuild.Validate(self.SavedPlan),Dirty=self.PlanDirty}
        self.SavedPlan=copy;self.PlanDirty=true;self.BuildReport=nil
        if self.Cells then
            for _,cell in ipairs(self.Cells) do if cell.Material==from then cell.Material=to end end
        end
        self.Preset="Saved: "..copy.Name.." *";buttons.Preset.Text=self.Preset.."  ▼"
        self:UpdateMaterials(self.Cells and self:SelectedCells() or copy.Cells)
        status("Changed "..changed.." blocks in the working blueprint. Saved slots are unchanged; Preview then Save preview to keep this edit.")
        return true,changed
    end
    function self:UndoMaterial()
        if self.Running or self.WorkerToken then status("Wait for the build worker before undoing material edits.");return false end
        if not self.MaterialUndo then status("No material edit to undo.");return false end
        self.SavedPlan=self.MaterialUndo.Plan;self.PlanDirty=self.MaterialUndo.Dirty;self.MaterialUndo=nil;self.BuildReport=nil
        if self.Cells then
            for index,cell in ipairs(self.Cells) do cell.Material=self.SavedPlan.Cells[index].Material end
        end
        self.Preset="Saved: "..self.SavedPlan.Name..(self.PlanDirty and " *" or "");buttons.Preset.Text=self.Preset.."  ▼"
        self:UpdateMaterials(self.Cells and self:SelectedCells() or self.SavedPlan.Cells)
        status("Last material edit undone. No saved slot or placed block was changed.")
        return true
    end
    function self:SavePreview()
        if self.Running then status("Stop the build before saving a blueprint.");return false end
        if not self.Cells or not self.Anchor then status("Preview a build before saving it.");return false end
        local data={Version=1,Name=inputs.Name.Text,BlockSize=self.BlockSize,Cells={}}
        for _,cell in ipairs(self.Cells) do
            table.insert(data.Cells,{Material=cell.Material,Components={self.Anchor:ToObjectSpace(cell.CFrame):GetComponents()}})
        end
        local saved=self:StoreBlueprint(data)
        if saved and self.SavedPlan then
            self.SavedPlan=self.Library[self.Slot];self.PlanDirty=false;self.MaterialUndo=nil
            self.Preset="Saved: "..self.SavedPlan.Name;buttons.Preset.Text=self.Preset.."  ▼"
        end
        return saved
    end
    function self:LoadBlueprint()
        if self.Running then status("Stop the build before loading a blueprint.");return false end
        local data=self.Library[self.Slot]
        if not data and ctx.ReadBlueprint then
            local ran,result=pcall(ctx.ReadBlueprint,self.Slot)
            if ran then data=result end
        end
        local ok,copy=pcall(AutoBuild.Validate,data)
        if not ok then status("Cannot load slot "..self.Slot..": "..tostring(copy));return false end
        self:ClearPreview()
        if not self.Library[self.Slot] then self.Persistence[self.Slot]="Loaded from file" end
        self.Library[self.Slot]=copy;self.SavedPlan=copy;self.PlanDirty=false;self.MaterialUndo=nil
        self:RefreshLibrary(false)
        self.Preset="Saved: "..copy.Name;buttons.Preset.Text=self.Preset.."  ▼"
        inputs.Name.Text=copy.Name
        self.OffsetX,self.OffsetY,self.OffsetZ=0,0,0
        self.Rotation=0;buttons.Rotate.Text="Rotate: 0°"
        self:UpdateMaterials(copy.Cells)
        status("Loaded "..copy.Name..". Preview at your current position, adjust offset/rotation, then Start. Materials and permissions are still required.")
        return true
    end
    function self:CaptureIsland()
        if self.Running then status("Stop the build before saving island blocks.");return false end
        local _,root=characterRoot()
        local blocks=ctx.IslandBlocks and ctx.IslandBlocks()
        if not root or not blocks then status("Stand on your own island to save nearby blocks.");return false end
        local definitions=ctx.BlockDefinitions and ctx.BlockDefinitions()
        if not definitions then status("Block definitions are unavailable; island capture was not saved.");return false end
        local selected,minY={},math.huge
        for _,block in ipairs(blocks:GetChildren()) do
            if definitions:FindFirstChild(block.Name) and (block:IsA("BasePart") or block:IsA("Model")) then
                local ok,cf=pcall(function() return block:IsA("BasePart") and block.CFrame or block:GetPivot() end)
                if not ok then status("A block transform is unreadable; nothing was saved.");return false end
                if (cf.Position-root.Position).Magnitude<=self.CaptureRange then
                    table.insert(selected,{CFrame=cf,Material=block.Name});minY=math.min(minY,cf.Position.Y)
                    if #selected>300 then status("Area exceeds 300 blocks. Reduce capture range or choose a smaller island section; nothing was saved.");return false end
                end
            end
        end
        if #selected==0 then status("No supported placed blocks within "..self.CaptureRange.." studs.");return false end
        local size=blockSize()
        local origin=CFrame.new(math.round(root.Position.X/size)*size,minY,math.round(root.Position.Z/size)*size)
        local data={Version=1,Name=inputs.Name.Text,BlockSize=size,Cells={}}
        for _,cell in ipairs(selected) do
            table.insert(data.Cells,{Material=cell.Material,Components={origin:ToObjectSpace(cell.CFrame):GetComponents()}})
        end
        return self:StoreBlueprint(data)
    end
    function self:Destroy() self:Stop();self:ClearPreview() end
    local function layoutY(y) return y>=446 and y+44 or y end
    local function label(text,y,height)
        return ctx.Label({Parent=ctx.Parent,Text=text,TextSize=12,Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,height or 24),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,layoutY(y))})
    end
    local function button(name,text,x,y,width,callback)
        local b=ctx.Button({Parent=ctx.Parent,Text=text,TextSize=12,Size=UDim2.new(width or 1,-12,0,32),Position=UDim2.new(x or 0,6,0,layoutY(y))})
        buttons[name]=b
        ctx.Track(b.Activated:Connect(callback));return b
    end
    local function invalidate()
        if not self.Running then self:ClearPreview();status("Settings changed. Preview the new plan.") end
    end
    label("Build farm beds and paths, platforms, walls or solid stairs.",4,36)
    local presets={"Farm plots","Platform","Wall","Stairs"}
    local presetMenu=Instance.new("Frame")
    presetMenu.Name="BlueprintOptions";presetMenu.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,144);presetMenu.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,84)
    presetMenu.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(29,39,48);presetMenu.BorderSizePixel=0;presetMenu.ZIndex=35;presetMenu.Visible=false;presetMenu.Parent=ctx.Parent
    button("Preset","Farm plots  ▼",0,48,.5,function()
        if not self.Running then presetMenu.Visible=not presetMenu.Visible;if self.MoveMenu then self.MoveMenu.Visible=false end end
    end)
    local moveMenu=Instance.new("Frame")
    moveMenu.Name="MoveBlueprint";moveMenu.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,184);moveMenu.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,84)
    moveMenu.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(29,39,48);moveMenu.BorderSizePixel=0;moveMenu.ZIndex=35;moveMenu.Visible=false;moveMenu.Parent=ctx.Parent
    button("Movement","Move blueprint  ▼",.5,48,.5,function()
        presetMenu.Visible=false
        moveMenu.Visible=not moveMenu.Visible
    end)
    local function moveButton(key,text,x,y,dx,dy,dz)
        local item=ctx.Button({Parent=moveMenu,Text=text,Size=UDim2.new(.5,-6,0,32),Position=UDim2.new(x,3,0,y)})
        item.ZIndex=36;buttons[key]=item
        ctx.Track(item.Activated:Connect(function() self:Move(dx,dy,dz) end))
    end
    moveButton("MoveLeft","← Left",0,4,-1,0,0)
    moveButton("MoveRight","Right →",.5,4,1,0,0)
    moveButton("MoveForward","↑ Forward",0,40,0,0,1)
    moveButton("MoveBack","Back ↓",.5,40,0,0,-1)
    moveButton("MoveUp","Up +1",0,76,0,1,0)
    moveButton("MoveDown","Down −1",.5,76,0,-1,0)
    local resetOffset=ctx.Button({Parent=moveMenu,Text="Reset blueprint offset",Size=UDim2.new(1,-6,0,32),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(3,112)})
    resetOffset.ZIndex=36;buttons.ResetOffset=resetOffset
    ctx.Track(resetOffset.Activated:Connect(function() self:Move(-self.OffsetX,-self.OffsetY,-self.OffsetZ) end))
    self.MoveLabel=ctx.Label({Parent=moveMenu,Text="Moves 1 block per click, relative to preview.",TextSize=12,Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,30),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,148)})

    self.MoveMenu=moveMenu
    for index,preset in ipairs(presets) do
        local option=ctx.Button({Parent=presetMenu,Text=preset,Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,34),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(2,(index-1)*36)})
        option.ZIndex=36
        ctx.Track(option.Activated:Connect(function()
            if self.Running then return end
            self.SavedPlan=nil;self.PlanDirty=false;self.MaterialUndo=nil
            self.Preset=preset;buttons.Preset.Text=preset.."  ▼";presetMenu.Visible=false;invalidate()
        end))
    end
    local function input(key,title,value,y,numeric)
        label(title,y)
        local field=Instance.new("TextBox");field.Name=key;field.Text=tostring(value);field.PlaceholderText=title
        field.ClearTextOnFocus=false;field.TextSize=14;field.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(226,232,240)
        field.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(29,39,48);field.BorderSizePixel=0;field.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,30);field.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,y+24);field.Parent=ctx.Parent
        inputs[key]=field
        local committed=field.Text
        ctx.Track(field.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            if self.Running or self.SavedPlan then
                field.Text=committed
                status(self.SavedPlan and "Saved layouts keep their materials and shape. Choose a built-in blueprint to edit them." or "Stop the build before changing its plan.")
                return
            end
            if numeric then
                local limit=key=="Height" and 8 or 12
                self[key]=math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(field.Text) or self[key]),1,limit);field.Text=tostring(self[key])
            end
            committed=field.Text
            invalidate()
        end))
    end
    input("Material","Bed/build block name (for example grass, soil or stone)","grass",90)
    input("Path","Farm path block name","stone",152)
    input("Width","Width (blocks)",self.Width,214,true)
    input("Length","Length / stair steps (blocks)",self.Length,276,true)
    input("Height","Wall height (blocks)",self.Height,338,true)
    button("Rotate","Rotate: 0°",0,406,1,function()
        if self.Running then return end
        self.Rotation=(self.Rotation+90)%360;buttons.Rotate.Text="Rotate: "..self.Rotation.."°";invalidate()
    end)
    buttons.Layer=ctx.Button({Parent=ctx.Parent,Text="Build: All layers",TextSize=12,Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,32),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,446)})
    ctx.Track(buttons.Layer.Activated:Connect(function() self:CycleLayer() end))
    button("Preview","Preview",0,446,.5,function() self:Preview() end)
    button("Start","Start build",.5,446,.5,function() self:Start() end)
    button("Stop","Stop build",0,486,.5,function() self:Stop() end)

    materialView.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,100);materialView.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,layoutY(616))
    materialView.BackgroundTransparency=1;materialView.BorderSizePixel=0;materialView.ScrollBarThickness=5
    materialView.CanvasSize=UDim2.new();materialView.AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y;materialView.Parent=ctx.Parent
    self.MaterialSummary=ctx.Label({Parent=materialView,Text="Preview or load a blueprint to see its material counts.",TextSize=12,
        Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,24),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(2,0)})
    self.MaterialSummary.TextWrapped=true;self.MaterialSummary.AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y;self.MaterialSummary.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.None
    label("SAVED BLUEPRINTS · own-island block layouts, up to 300 blocks",724,34)
    local name=Instance.new("TextBox");name.Name="BlueprintName";name.Text="My island build";name.PlaceholderText="Blueprint name (1–48 bytes)"
    name.ClearTextOnFocus=false;name.TextSize=14;name.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(226,232,240);name.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(29,39,48)
    name.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,32);name.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,layoutY(764));name.Parent=ctx.Parent;inputs.Name=name
    button("Slot","Blueprint slot: 1 / 5",0,804,1,function()
        if self.Running then status("Stop the build before changing its blueprint slot.");return end
        self.Slot=self.Slot%5+1
        self:InspectSlot(self.Slot);self:RefreshLibrary(false)
    end)
    button("Save","Save preview",0,844,.5,function() self:SavePreview() end)
    button("Capture","Save island area",.5,844,.5,function() self:CaptureIsland() end)
    button("Load","Load selected slot",0,884,1,function() self:LoadBlueprint() end)
    button("CaptureRange","Save radius: 30 studs",0,924,1,function()
        if self.Running then return end
        self.CaptureRange=self.CaptureRange>=120 and 15 or self.CaptureRange*2
        buttons.CaptureRange.Text="Save radius: "..self.CaptureRange.." studs"
    end)
    local hint=label("Save preview stores its shape and materials. Save island area stores nearby placed blocks on your own island. Saving overwrites the selected slot. Load never starts building. Chest contents, inventories and scripts are not saved.",966,110)
    hint.TextWrapped=true

        if not self.BuildReport then status("Run Check remaining materials first.");return end
        if ctx.CopyReport then ctx.CopyReport(self.BuildReport) else status("Report copying is unavailable.") end
    end)

    self.LibrarySummary=label("Saved slots have not been inspected. Press Refresh saved slots to read them.",1208,156)
    self.LibrarySummary.TextWrapped=true

    end)

    copyHint.TextWrapped=true

        inputs[key]=field

    button("UndoMaterial","Undo last edit",.5,1618,.5,function() self:UndoMaterial() end)

    replaceHint.TextWrapped=true
    self.Buttons=buttons;self.Inputs=inputs
    return self
end
return AutoBuild
end)()

-- Config

local CONFIG = {
	ToggleKey = Enum.KeyCode.F6,          -- fishing ON/OFF
	HarvestKey = Enum.KeyCode.F7,         -- mine / harvest ON/OFF
	RequireTool = true,                   -- only cast while a Tool (rod) is equipped
	CastTimeout = 2.0,                  -- typical bobber response is quick; allow a small network margin
	CastHold = 0.35,
    CastDistance = 12,
    BiteTimeout = 45, -- reset the cast if a bobber never tugs
    ReelTimeout = 1.0,                    -- shorter confirmation window after reeling
	FishingPollInterval = 0.01,           -- check cast/bobber state more frequently
	ReelSettleDelay = 0.025,              -- brief time for the server to register a reel
	ActionCooldown = 0.04,                -- minimal guard against duplicate rapid clicks
	MaxFailedCasts = 5,                   -- pause and retry after this many casts with no bobber
	RecentCount = 5,                      -- how many recent catches to show

	-- Auto Mine settings
	MineRange = 90,                       -- studs from character
	MineDelay = 0.10,                     -- faster sequential actions while leaving server time to register drops
	MineBatchSize = 1,                    -- 1 at a time so drops actually register
	MineScanInterval = 0.20,              -- rescan a little more often when targets are absent

	-- Auto Plant settings
	PlantEnabled = true,                  -- plant after harvesting crops
	PlantType = "wheat",                  -- selected crop to plant
	HarvestCrop = "wheat",                 -- crop type to harvest
	WoodRange = 90,
    CropRange = 60,
    HarvestWalk = true,
    FarmTravelMode = "Walk",
    WoodTravelMode = "Normal",
	AutoReplant = true,
    FruitEnabled = false,
	PlantDelay = 0.15,                    -- delay between plant attempts
	PlantRadius = 12,                     -- how far around player to plant
	PlantSpacing = 3,                     -- grid spacing between plants
}

-- Optional persistence: supported only where the host exposes file APIs.
-- Failures are intentionally non-fatal so the UI still works in standard Roblox.
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local SETTINGS_FILE = "IslandsFarmingHub_" .. tostring(player.UserId) .. "_settings.json"
-- Declare these before persistence helpers so the helpers capture the intended locals.
local woodToolPreference = "Auto best axe"
local mineToolPreference = "Auto best pickaxe"
local function savePreferences()
    if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return false end
    local data = {
        WoodTravelMode = CONFIG.WoodTravelMode, FarmTravelMode = CONFIG.FarmTravelMode, HarvestWalk = CONFIG.HarvestWalk, HarvestCrops = CONFIG.HarvestCrops, HarvestCrop = CONFIG.HarvestCrop, PlantType = CONFIG.PlantType,
        FruitEnabled = CONFIG.FruitEnabled, AutoReplant = CONFIG.AutoReplant, MineRange = CONFIG.MineRange,
        WoodRange = CONFIG.WoodRange, CropRange = CONFIG.CropRange, MineDelay = CONFIG.MineDelay,
        MineScanInterval = CONFIG.MineScanInterval, PlantDelay = CONFIG.PlantDelay,
        WoodToolPreference = woodToolPreference, MineToolPreference = mineToolPreference,
    }
    local ok = pcall(function() writefile(SETTINGS_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(data)) end)
    return ok
end
local function loadPreferences()
    if type(readfile) ~= "function" or type(isfile) ~= "function" then return false end
    local ok, data = pcall(function()
        if not isfile(SETTINGS_FILE) then return nil end
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(SETTINGS_FILE))
    end)
    if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" then return false end
    if type(data.HarvestCrop) == "string" then CONFIG.HarvestCrop = data.HarvestCrop end
    if data.WoodTravelMode=="Normal" or data.WoodTravelMode=="Walk" or data.WoodTravelMode=="Tween" or data.WoodTravelMode=="Teleport" then CONFIG.WoodTravelMode=data.WoodTravelMode end
    if data.FarmTravelMode=="Walk" or data.FarmTravelMode=="Tween" or data.FarmTravelMode=="Teleport" then CONFIG.FarmTravelMode=data.FarmTravelMode end
    if type(data.HarvestWalk)=="boolean" then CONFIG.HarvestWalk=data.HarvestWalk end
    if type(data.HarvestCrops) == "table" then CONFIG.HarvestCrops = data.HarvestCrops end
    if type(data.PlantType) == "string" then CONFIG.PlantType = data.PlantType end
    if type(data.AutoReplant) == "boolean" then CONFIG.AutoReplant = data.AutoReplant end
    if type(data.FruitEnabled) == "boolean" then CONFIG.FruitEnabled = data.FruitEnabled end
    if type(data.MineRange) == "number" then CONFIG.MineRange = math.clamp(data.MineRange, 15, 150) end
    if type(data.CropRange) == "number" then CONFIG.CropRange = math.clamp(data.CropRange, 10, 120) end
    if type(data.WoodRange) == "number" then CONFIG.WoodRange = math.clamp(data.WoodRange, 15, 150) end
    if type(data.MineDelay) == "number" then CONFIG.MineDelay = math.clamp(data.MineDelay, 0.08, 1) end
    if type(data.MineScanInterval) == "number" then CONFIG.MineScanInterval = math.clamp(data.MineScanInterval, 0.15, 2) end
    if type(data.PlantDelay) == "number" then CONFIG.PlantDelay = math.clamp(data.PlantDelay, 0.08, 1) end
    if type(data.WoodToolPreference) == "string" then woodToolPreference = data.WoodToolPreference == "Wood axe" and "Wooden axe" or data.WoodToolPreference end
    if type(data.MineToolPreference) == "string" then mineToolPreference = data.MineToolPreference == "Wood pickaxe" and "Wooden pickaxe" or data.MineToolPreference end
    return true
end
local preferencesLoaded = loadPreferences()
CONFIG.HarvestCrops = FarmLogic.NormalizeCrops(CONFIG.HarvestCrops, CONFIG.HarvestCrop)

-- Remove previous GUI (this also stops the previous script's loops)
local old = playerGui:FindFirstChild("AutoFishingGUI")
if old then
	old:Destroy()
end

local COLORS = {
	bg = Color3.fromRGB(17, 23, 29),
	panel = Color3.fromRGB(29, 39, 48),
	stroke = Color3.fromRGB(56, 73, 83),
	text = Color3.fromRGB(226, 232, 240),
	muted = Color3.fromRGB(170, 186, 194),
	on = Color3.fromRGB(70, 190, 145),
	off = Color3.fromRGB(42, 55, 65),
	gold = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 60),
	good = Color3.fromRGB(80, 230, 130),
	bad = Color3.fromRGB(240, 80, 80),
	warn = Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 50),
	harvest = Color3.fromRGB(122, 162, 247),
	accent = Color3.fromRGB(91, 204, 177),
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Connection bookkeeping
--------------------------------------------------------------------
local connections = {}
local function track(conn)
	table.insert(connections, conn)
	return conn
end

--------------------------------------------------------------------
-- GUI helpers
--------------------------------------------------------------------
local function corner(parent, radius)
	local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
	c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius)
	c.Parent = parent
	return c
end

local function label(props)
	local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	l.Font = props.Font or Enum.Font.Gotham
	l.TextSize = math.max(props.TextSize or 13, 12)
	l.TextColor3 = props.Color or COLORS.text
	l.TextXAlignment = props.Align or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	l.Text = props.Text or ""
	l.Size = props.Size
	l.Position = props.Position or UDim2.new()
	l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	l.Parent = props.Parent
	return l
end

local function button(props)
	local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
	b.Size = props.Size or UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 32)
	b.Position = props.Position or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	b.BackgroundColor3 = props.Bg or COLORS.panel
	b.TextColor3 = props.Color or COLORS.text
	b.Text = props.Text
	b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	b.TextSize = math.max(props.TextSize or 13, 13)
	b.BorderSizePixel = 0
	b.AutoButtonColor = true
	b.Parent = props.Parent
	corner(b, props.Radius or 8)
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color = COLORS.stroke
    stroke.Thickness = 1
    stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    stroke.Parent = b
    track(b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() stroke.Color = COLORS.accent end))
    track(b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() stroke.Color = COLORS.stroke end))
    if string.find(props.Text or "", "▼", 1, true) then
        b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 12)
        padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 12)
        padding.Parent = b
    end
	return b
end

local function panel(parent, size, position)
	local p = Instance.new("Frame")
	p.Size = size
	p.Position = position
	p.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.panel
	p.BorderSizePixel = 0
	p.Parent = parent
	corner(p, 8)

	local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
	pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6)
	pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
	pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
	pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
	pad.Parent = p

	local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	layout.Parent = p

	return p
end

-- NAVY. Upstream ships a plum palette; these are the same roles in blue. AccentDark is
-- the text that sits ON the accent (primary buttons, the toggle knob), so it stays the
-- darkest colour here -- swapping it for a blue would leave that text unreadable.

-- downloading a table from a third-party GitHub repo and running it -- a dependency that
-- can execute anything it likes in the executor, on every launch, and the README promises
-- no dependencies and no phone-home. The script already ships the ten icons it uses in
-- LucideIcons (030_services.lua); the resolver is handed in from there, because this part
-- loads first and cannot see a later part's locals.

local hub = Airflow:Window({
    Name = "AutoFishingGUI",
    Parent = playerGui,
    MinSize = Vector2.new(640, 420),
    Title = "Islands",
    Subtitle = "build-layers-48",
    Icon = "rbxassetid://6031090998",
    Keybind = Enum.KeyCode.RightControl,
    Loading = { Enabled = false },
    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(720, 470),
    OpenButton = { Title = "Islands Farming Hub", Draggable = false },
})
local gui = hub.Gui
local frame = hub.Root
local airflowTabs = {}
local pages = {}
local cropDropdown, cropDropdownOpen
local activeTab = "Fishing"

RemoteCatalog.MenuClosers={}
function RemoteCatalog.CloseMenus()
    for _,close in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.MenuClosers) do close() end
end
local selectTabOriginal=hub.SelectTab
hub.SelectTab=function(self,...)
    RemoteCatalog.CloseMenus()
    return selectTabOriginal(self,...)
end
local toggleOriginal=hub.Toggle
hub.Toggle=function(self,...)
    RemoteCatalog.CloseMenus()
    return toggleOriginal(self,...)
end

local function createPage(name)
    local tab = hub:Tab({ Name = name })
    tab._label.TextSize = 14
    airflowTabs[name] = tab
    -- Keep the existing farming controls and their callbacks in the Airflow page.
    -- Its stock list/empty state are replaced by our scrollable content.
    tab.List:Destroy()
    for _, child in ipairs(tab._page:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Frame") then child:Destroy() end
    end
    local page = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    page.Name = name .. "Page"
    page.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 58)
    page.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, -90)
    page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    page.BorderSizePixel = 0
    page.ScrollBarThickness = 6
    page.VerticalScrollBarInset = Enum.ScrollBarInset.ScrollBar
    page.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.WhenScrollable
    page.ScrollBarImageColor3 = COLORS.accent
    page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
    page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    page.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    page.Parent = tab._page
    table.insert(hub._controls, page) -- Scrolling/clicking controls must not drag the window.
    -- Airflow's intro reveals its list's children. Use the content as that list.
    tab.List = page
    pages[name] = page
    return page
end

local fishingPage = createPage("Fishing")
local harvestPage = createPage("Harvest")
RemoteCatalog.ChestPage = createPage("Chests")
local woodPage = createPage("Wood Farm")
local minePage = createPage("Mining")
local buildPage = createPage("Auto Build")
local movementPage = createPage("Movement")
RemoteCatalog.TeleportPage = createPage("Teleport")
local statsPage = createPage("Stats")
local settingsPage = createPage("Settings")
local remotesPage = createPage("Remotes")
local buildController = AutoBuild.new({
    Player = player, Parent = buildPage, Label = label, Button = button, Track = track,
    Alive = function() return gui.Parent ~= nil end,
    GameModule = RemoteCatalog.GameModule,
    Place = RemoteCatalog.CallPlacement,
    ItemCount = function(item) return RemoteCatalog.CarrotCount and RemoteCatalog.CarrotCount(item) end,
    CopyReport = function(report) return RemoteCatalog.CopyBuildReport(report) end,
    IslandBlocks = function() return RemoteCatalog.BuildIslandBlocks and RemoteCatalog.BuildIslandBlocks() end,
    BlockDefinitions = function() return ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Blocks") end,
    ReadBlueprint = function(slot)
        if type(readfile)~="function" then return nil end
        local encoded=readfile("RebornBlueprint_"..tostring(player.UserId).."_"..slot..".json")
        if type(encoded)~="string" or #encoded>1000000 then error("Invalid blueprint file size") end
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(encoded)
    end,
    WriteBlueprint = function(slot,data)
        if type(writefile)~="function" then return false end
        writefile("RebornBlueprint_"..tostring(player.UserId).."_"..slot..".json",HttpService:JSONEncode(data))
        return true
    end,
})

local function showTab(name)
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestSelectMenu then RemoteCatalog.ChestSelectMenu.Visible=false end
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestAmountMenu then RemoteCatalog.ChestAmountMenu.Visible=false end
    if RemoteCatalog.LayoutChestControls then RemoteCatalog.LayoutChestControls() end
    activeTab = name
    if RemoteCatalog.SetCropDropdown then RemoteCatalog.SetCropDropdown(false)
    else cropDropdownOpen=false;if cropDropdown then cropDropdown.Visible=false end end
    for _, child in ipairs(woodPage:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == "AxeDropdown" then child.Visible = false end
    end
    for _, child in ipairs(minePage:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == "PickaxeDropdown" or child.Name == "ToolDropdown" then child.Visible = false end
    end
    for _,refresh in pairs(RemoteCatalog.ToolMenuRefresh or {}) do refresh() end
    hub:SelectTab(airflowTabs[name])
end

local status = label({
    Parent = hub.Content,
    Text = "Idle",
    Color = COLORS.muted,
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 20),
    Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 1, -26),
    TextSize = 11,
})

local function setStatus(text, color)
    status.Text = tostring(text)
    status.TextColor3 = color or COLORS.muted
end
function RemoteCatalog.CopyBuildReport(report)
    local copy=setclipboard or toclipboard
    if type(copy)=="function" and pcall(copy,report) then setStatus("Build check copied",COLORS.good)
    else print(report);setStatus("Build check printed to console",COLORS.warn) end
end

local function pageHeading(parent, text, y)
    return label({
        Parent = parent, Text = text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        TextSize = 11, Color = COLORS.muted,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, y),
    })
end

    Safe =     { poll = 0.05, castTimeout = 4.0, reelTimeout = 2.0, settle = 0.10, cooldown = 0.25 },
    Balanced = { poll = 0.01, castTimeout = 2.0, reelTimeout = 0.9, settle = 0.025, cooldown = 0.04 },
    Fast =     { poll = 0.005, castTimeout = 1.5, reelTimeout = 0.7, settle = 0.015, cooldown = 0.025 },
    Test =     { poll = 0.01, castTimeout = 2.0, reelTimeout = 0.9, settle = 0.025, cooldown = 0.04 },
}

    Text = "Fixed at 12 studs for nearby water. Aim distance does not change the game's cast limits.",
    TextSize = 10, Color = COLORS.muted, Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 40), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 305)})
castAimHelp.TextWrapped = true

    Parent = statsOverlay, Text = "FISHING STATS", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,

    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 5),

local draggingOverlay = false
local overlayDragInput = nil
local overlayDragStart = nil
local overlayStartPosition = nil
statsOverlayTitle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        draggingOverlay = true
        overlayDragStart = input.Position
        overlayStartPosition = statsOverlay.Position
        overlayDragInput = input
        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                draggingOverlay = false
                overlayDragInput = nil
            end
        end)
    end
end)
track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if draggingOverlay and overlayDragStart and overlayStartPosition then
        local isMouseMove = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        local isTouchMove = overlayDragInput and input == overlayDragInput
        if isMouseMove or isTouchMove then
            local delta = input.Position - overlayDragStart
            statsOverlay.Position = UDim2.new(
                overlayStartPosition.X.Scale, overlayStartPosition.X.Offset + delta.X,
                overlayStartPosition.Y.Scale, overlayStartPosition.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
        end
    end
end))

local function applyFishingMode(modeName)
    modeName="Fast"
    CONFIG.CastDistance=12
    local profile = FISHING_MODES[modeName]
    if not profile then return end
    activeFishingMode = modeName
    CONFIG.FishingPollInterval = profile.poll
    CONFIG.CastTimeout = profile.castTimeout
    CONFIG.ReelTimeout = profile.reelTimeout
    CONFIG.ReelSettleDelay = profile.settle
    CONFIG.ActionCooldown = profile.cooldown
    for name, modeButton in pairs(modeButtons) do
        modeButton.BackgroundColor3 = name == modeName and COLORS.accent or COLORS.panel
    end
    modeHeading.Text = "FISHING MODE  ·  " .. string.upper(modeName)
    if modeName == "Safe" then
        modeInfo.Text = "Safe: slower polling and longer waits; best for high latency or checking reliability."
    elseif modeName == "Balanced" then
        modeInfo.Text = "Balanced: responsive timing with extra catch-confirmation time for everyday use."
    elseif modeName == "Fast" then
        modeInfo.Text = "Fast: shortest waits and quickest retry; may count a slow server response as a miss."
    else
        modeInfo.Text = "Test: conservative confirmation timing for comparing catch rate against Fast mode. Reset Stats between runs."
    end
end

local baitBusy, fishingInputBusy = false, false
local baitEnabled = false
local enabled = false
local setEnabled -- used by confirmed bait startup
local harvestEnabled = false -- crop farm
local woodEnabled = false
local mineEnabled = false
local baitCycleId = 0
local baitToggle = button({
    Parent = fishingPage, Text = "AUTO BAIT OFF  [60s]", TextSize = 12, Bg = COLORS.off,
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 34), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 90),
})
local baitInfo = label({
    Parent = fishingPage, Text = "Enable to apply bait. Confirmed consumption starts Fishing once; refreshes every 60s.",
    Color = COLORS.muted, TextSize = 10, Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 30),
    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 128),
})
baitInfo.TextWrapped = true

RemoteCatalog.BaitNativeLabel=label({Parent=fishingPage,Text="Food API: not checked yet",TextSize=11,Color=COLORS.muted,
    Size=UDim2.new(1,-16,0,48),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(8,356)})
RemoteCatalog.BaitNativeLabel.TextWrapped=true
RemoteCatalog.BaitDiagnosticButton=button({Parent=fishingPage,Text="Copy bait diagnostics",TextSize=12,
    Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,32),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,410)})
function RemoteCatalog.BaitDiagnosticReport()
    local bait
    local ok,tools=pcall(RemoteCatalog.OwnedTools)
    if ok then for _,tool in ipairs(tools) do if string.lower(tool.Name)=="bait" then bait=tool;break end end end
    local recorder=RemoteCatalog.LiveRecorder("BaitBinding")
    local function property(name)
        if not bait then return "no tool" end
        local ok,value=pcall(function() return bait[name] end)
        return ok and tostring(value) or "unavailable"
    end
    return table.concat({"Reborn build-layers-48",
        "Food API: "..tostring(RemoteCatalog.BaitNativeStatus or "not checked"),
        "Last transport: "..tostring(RemoteCatalog.BaitTransport or "none"),
        "Last result: "..tostring(RemoteCatalog.BaitLastResult or "none"),
        "Bait quantity: "..tostring(bait and (RemoteCatalog.BaitCount(bait) or "unreadable") or "no tool"),
        "Bait equipped: "..tostring(bait~=nil and bait.Parent==player.Character),
        "Current bait binding: "..tostring(recorder~=nil),
        "Binding refresh needed: "..tostring(RemoteCatalog.BaitNeedsRefresh==true),
        "Last learned request: "..tostring(RemoteCatalog.BaitLearnedStatus or "not tried"),
        "Next activation: "..(RemoteCatalog.BaitTryMouseHandler and "food mouse handler" or RemoteCatalog.BaitTrySignal and "food signal/handler" or RemoteCatalog.BaitTryToolActivation and "Tool:Activate" or "mouse click"),
        "Food mouse handler: "..tostring(RemoteCatalog.BaitFoodMouseStatus or "not inspected"),
        "Bait learner: "..tostring(RemoteCatalog.Recorder and RemoteCatalog.Recorder.LearnBaitOnly==true),
        "Tool.Enabled: "..property("Enabled"),
        "Tool.ManualActivationOnly: "..property("ManualActivationOnly"),
        "Activation signal support: "..tostring(RemoteCatalog.BaitSignalAvailable()),
        "Capture hook support: "..tostring(type(hookmetamethod)=="function" and type(getnamecallmethod)=="function"),
        "Auto Bait: "..tostring(baitEnabled),"Fishing: "..tostring(enabled)},"\n")
end
track(RemoteCatalog.BaitDiagnosticButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    local report=RemoteCatalog.BaitDiagnosticReport()
    local copy=setclipboard or toclipboard
    if type(copy)=="function" and pcall(copy,report) then setStatus("Bait diagnostics copied",COLORS.good)
    else print(report);setStatus("Bait diagnostics printed to the console",COLORS.warn) end
end))

-- BAIT TOOL INPUT BEGIN
RemoteCatalog.BaitCount=function(tool)
    if not tool then return nil end
    local keys={"amount","quantity","stacksize","count","stack"}
    local values={}
    local ok,attributes=pcall(function() return tool:GetAttributes() end)
    if ok then
        for name,value in pairs(attributes) do
            local number=(type(value)=="number" or type(value)=="string") and tonumber(value)
            if number and number>=0 then values[string.lower(name)]=number end
        end
        for _,key in ipairs(keys) do if values[key]~=nil then return values[key] end end
    end
    local childrenOK,children=pcall(function() return tool:GetChildren() end)
    if childrenOK then
        for _,child in ipairs(children) do
            if (child:IsA("IntValue") or child:IsA("NumberValue")) and child.Value>=0 then values[string.lower(child.Name)]=child.Value end
        end
        for _,key in ipairs(keys) do if values[key]~=nil then return values[key] end end
    end
end
RemoteCatalog.WithBaitTool = function(tool, callback)
    if baitBusy or fishingInputBusy then return false, "Waiting for current rod input" end
    if RemoteCatalog.BobberActive and RemoteCatalog.BobberActive() then return false, "Waiting for the current cast to finish" end
    local character = player.Character
    if not character then return false, "Wait for your character" end
    local previous = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    local token = baitCycleId
    local function active() return baitEnabled and baitCycleId == token and gui.Parent ~= nil and player.Character == character end
    if not active() then return false, "Auto Bait stopped" end
    baitBusy = true
    local sent, detail = false, "Auto Bait stopped"
    local ok, failure = pcall(function()
        if not RemoteCatalog.EquipTool(tool) then error("Could not equip bait") end
        task.wait(0.1)
        if not active() then return end
        if tool.Parent ~= character or character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") ~= tool then error("Bait tool not equipped") end
        local before=RemoteCatalog.BaitCount(tool)
        sent, detail = callback()
        if sent then
            local consumed=false
            local elapsed=0
            -- Food activation can complete asynchronously. Keep bait equipped while it finishes.
            repeat
                if not active() then sent=false;detail="Auto Bait stopped";return end
                if character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")~=tool and tool.Parent~=nil then
                    sent=false;detail="Bait tool changed before consumption";return
                end
                local after=RemoteCatalog.BaitCount(tool)
                consumed=before~=nil and (after~=nil and after<before or before==1 and tool.Parent==nil)
                if consumed then break end
                task.wait(.05);elapsed+=.05
            until elapsed>=2
            sent=consumed
            detail=consumed and "bait consumption confirmed" or before==nil and "Bait quantity unreadable on this account; consumption cannot be verified."
                or ("Bait use unconfirmed; quantity stayed at "..tostring(before)..".")
        end
    end)
    -- Restore only if bait is still selected; preserve manual selection and respawns.
    if previous and previous ~= tool and player.Character == character then
        local current = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        if current == tool or current == nil then pcall(RemoteCatalog.EquipTool, previous) end
    end
    baitBusy = false
    if not ok then return false, failure end
    return sent and active(), detail
end
-- BAIT NORMAL CLICK BEGIN
RemoteCatalog.ClickBaitTool=function(tool)
    local character=player.Character
    local camera=workspace and workspace.CurrentCamera
    local root
    pcall(function() root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end)
    if not camera or not root then return nil,"World input unavailable" end
    local focused,text=pcall(function() return UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() end)
    if focused and text then return false,"Close chat before applying bait" end
    local viewport=camera.ViewportSize
    local candidates={}
    local projected,visible=camera:WorldToScreenPoint(root.Position+root.CFrame.LookVector*12)
    if visible and projected.Z>0 then table.insert(candidates,{X=projected.X,Y=projected.Y}) end
    for _,fraction in ipairs({.8,.65,.5,.35}) do table.insert(candidates,{X=viewport.X*fraction,Y=viewport.Y*.5}) end
    local point
    for _,candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
        local blocked=false
        if hub.Root.Visible then
            local p,size=hub.Root.AbsolutePosition,hub.Root.AbsoluteSize
            blocked=candidate.X>=p.X and candidate.X<=p.X+size.X and candidate.Y>=p.Y and candidate.Y<=p.Y+size.Y
        end
        local checked,objects=pcall(function() return playerGui:GetGuiObjectsAtPosition(candidate.X,candidate.Y) end)
        if checked then
            for _,object in ipairs(objects) do if object:IsA("GuiButton") or object:IsA("TextBox") then blocked=true;break end end
        end
        if not blocked then point=candidate;break end
    end
    if not point then return false,"Move the hub to leave clear game space for bait input" end
    local token=baitCycleId
    local function active()
        return baitEnabled and baitCycleId==token and gui.Parent~=nil and player.Character==character
            and tool.Parent==character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")==tool
    end
    if not active() then return false,"Auto Bait stopped or tool changed" end
    local host=type(mousemoveabs)=="function" and type(mouse1press)=="function" and type(mouse1release)=="function"
    local moved=pcall(function()
        if host then mousemoveabs(point.X,point.Y) else VirtualInputManager:SendMouseMoveEvent(point.X,point.Y,game) end
    end)
    if not moved then return nil,"Mouse movement unavailable" end
    task.wait(.03)
    if not active() then return false,"Auto Bait stopped or tool changed" end
    local function input(down)
        if host then if down then mouse1press() else mouse1release() end
        else VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(point.X,point.Y,0,down,game,0) end
    end
    RemoteCatalog.BaitMouseInput=true
    local pressed=pcall(input,true)
    local held=pcall(function() if pressed then task.wait(.1) end end)
    local released=pcall(input,false)
    pcall(task.wait,.03) -- Let GUI activation from the release finish before accepting toggles.
    RemoteCatalog.BaitMouseInput=false
    if not pressed or not held or not released then return false,"Bait mouse input failed" end
    return active(),host and "host mouse bait click" or "virtual mouse bait click"
end
-- BAIT NORMAL CLICK END
RemoteCatalog.BaitSignalAvailable=function()
    return type(firesignal)=="function" and not RemoteCatalog.BaitDirectSignalFailed
        or type(getconnections)=="function" and type(getfenv)=="function"
end
RemoteCatalog.FireBaitSignal=function(tool,pressed,directRelease)
    if pressed and (tool.Parent~=player.Character or tool.Enabled==false) then return false,"Bait is not ready to activate" end
    local event=pressed and tool.Activated or tool.Deactivated
    if type(firesignal)=="function" and (not RemoteCatalog.BaitDirectSignalFailed or directRelease) then
        local ok,result=pcall(firesignal,event)
        if pressed and not ok then RemoteCatalog.BaitDirectSignalFailed=true end
        return ok,result
    end
    RemoteCatalog.BaitTransport="Tool.Activated food handler"
    return RemoteCatalog.FireBaitFoodEvent(tool,event,pressed)
end
RemoteCatalog.BaitFoodConnections=function(tool,event)
    if type(getconnections)~="function" or type(getfenv)~="function" then return {},"Food handler inspection unavailable" end
    local found,food=pcall(function() return tool:FindFirstChild("food") end)
    if not found or not food then return {},"Bait food script unavailable" end
    local ok,list=pcall(getconnections,event)
    if not ok or type(list)~="table" then return {},"Food activation callbacks unavailable" end
    local matched={}
    for _,connection in ipairs(list) do
        local valid,callback=pcall(function()
            if connection.Enabled==false or type(connection.Function)~="function" then return end
            if getfenv(connection.Function).script==food then return connection.Function end
        end)
        if valid and callback then table.insert(matched,{Connection=connection,Callback=callback}) end
    end
    return matched,#matched>0 and "Enabled food handlers found" or "No enabled food activation handler found"
end
RemoteCatalog.FireBaitFoodEvent=function(tool,event,pressed)
    if pressed and (tool.Parent~=player.Character or tool.Enabled==false) then return false,"Bait is not ready to activate" end
    local list,reason=RemoteCatalog.BaitFoodConnections(tool,event)
    local sent=0
    for _,handler in ipairs(list) do
        if pressed and tool.Parent~=player.Character then break end
        local fired=pcall(function()
            if type(handler.Connection.Fire)=="function" then handler.Connection:Fire() else handler.Callback() end
        end)
        if not fired then return false,"Food activation handler failed" end
        sent+=1
    end
    return sent>0 or not pressed,reason
end
RemoteCatalog.BaitFoodMouse=function()
    local ok,mouse=pcall(function() return player:GetMouse() end)
    if ok and mouse then return mouse end
end
RemoteCatalog.BaitFoodMouseAvailable=function(tool)
    local mouse=RemoteCatalog.BaitFoodMouse()
    if not mouse then RemoteCatalog.BaitFoodMouseStatus="Player mouse unavailable";return false end
    local list,reason=RemoteCatalog.BaitFoodConnections(tool,mouse.Button1Down)
    RemoteCatalog.BaitFoodMouseStatus=reason
    return #list>0
end
RemoteCatalog.ActivateBait = function(tool, prepared)
    local function activate()
        if RemoteCatalog.BaitTryMouseHandler~=false and RemoteCatalog.BaitFoodMouseAvailable(tool) then
            local mouse=RemoteCatalog.BaitFoodMouse()
            RemoteCatalog.BaitTransport="Mouse.Button1Down food handler"
            local pressed,reason=RemoteCatalog.FireBaitFoodEvent(tool,mouse.Button1Down,true)
            local held=pcall(task.wait,.15)
            local released=RemoteCatalog.FireBaitFoodEvent(tool,mouse.Button1Up,false)
            return pressed and held and released,pressed and "bait food mouse handler attempted" or reason
        end
        if RemoteCatalog.BaitTrySignal~=false and RemoteCatalog.BaitSignalAvailable() then
            RemoteCatalog.BaitTransport="Tool.Activated signal"
            local direct=type(firesignal)=="function" and not RemoteCatalog.BaitDirectSignalFailed
            local pressed,reason=RemoteCatalog.FireBaitSignal(tool,true)
            local held=pcall(task.wait,.15)
            local released=RemoteCatalog.FireBaitSignal(tool,false,direct)
            return pressed and held and released,pressed and "bait activation signal attempted" or reason
        end
        if not RemoteCatalog.BaitTryToolActivation then
            local clicked,reason=RemoteCatalog.ClickBaitTool(tool)
            if clicked~=nil then
                RemoteCatalog.BaitTransport=reason
                return clicked,reason
            end
        end
        RemoteCatalog.BaitTransport="Tool:Activate"
        -- Use the engine activation lifecycle, including its release. A signal alone
        -- does not update the Tool's internal activation state.
        local pressed,reason=pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
        local held=pcall(task.wait,.15)
        local released=pcall(function() tool:Deactivate() end)
        return pressed and held and released,pressed and "tool activation attempted" or tostring(reason)
    end
    if prepared then return activate() end
    return RemoteCatalog.WithBaitTool(tool, activate)
end
-- BAIT TOOL INPUT END

local function applyBait()
    local character = player.Character
    local baitTool = character and character:FindFirstChild("bait")
    if not baitTool then
        local backpack = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        baitTool = backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("bait")
    end
    if not baitTool then
        for _,owned in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.OwnedTools()) do
            if string.lower(owned.Name)=="bait" then baitTool=owned;break end
        end
    end
    if baitTool and RemoteCatalog.BaitInputCharacter~=character then
        RemoteCatalog.BaitInputCharacter=character
        RemoteCatalog.BaitTryMouseHandler=nil
        RemoteCatalog.BaitDirectSignalFailed=nil
        local checked,manual=pcall(function() return baitTool.ManualActivationOnly end)
        -- These tools explicitly require Tool:Activate. Prefer it to a synthetic signal.
        RemoteCatalog.BaitTrySignal=not (checked and manual==true) and RemoteCatalog.BaitSignalAvailable()
        RemoteCatalog.BaitTryToolActivation=not RemoteCatalog.BaitTrySignal
    end
    -- Inventory rebuilds can replace the Tool without replacing the character.
    -- Keep fallback progress so each replacement cannot restart a failed signal path.
    RemoteCatalog.BaitInputTool=baitTool
    local count=baitTool and RemoteCatalog.BaitCount(baitTool)
    local recorder=RemoteCatalog.AcceptFreshBaitBinding()
    local binding=recorder and recorder.BaitBinding
    local sample=RemoteCatalog.BaitSample
    local consumed=sample and type(sample.Count)=="number" and sample.Tool and
        (baitTool==sample.Tool and type(count)=="number" and count<sample.Count or sample.Count==1 and sample.Tool.Parent==nil)
    if consumed and type(sample.At)=="number" and sample.Character==character
        and binding and binding~=sample.Binding and binding.Target==sample.Tool
        and (os.clock()-sample.At<=15 or sample.Manual and type(binding.ObservedAt)=="number"
            and binding.ObservedAt>=sample.At and os.clock()-binding.ObservedAt<=15) then
        RemoteCatalog.BaitNeedsRefresh=false
        RemoteCatalog.BaitFailedBinding=nil
        RemoteCatalog.BaitPending=false
        RemoteCatalog.BaitOutOfStock=not baitTool or count==0
        RemoteCatalog.BaitSample={Character=character,Tool=baitTool,Count=count,Binding=binding,At=os.clock()}
        local observed=type(binding.ObservedAt)=="number" and binding.ObservedAt or os.clock()
        RemoteCatalog.BaitRefreshDelay=math.clamp(math.floor(60-(os.clock()-observed)),1,60)
        baitInfo.Text="Manual bait consumption confirmed; ready to fish."
        baitInfo.TextColor3=COLORS.good
        return true,"bait consumption confirmed"
    end
    if not baitTool then
        RemoteCatalog.BaitOutOfStock=true
        RemoteCatalog.BaitPending=false
        baitInfo.Text = "No bait in inventory. Fishing continues without bait; checking again every 5s."
        baitInfo.TextColor3 = COLORS.warn
        setStatus(enabled and "No bait — fishing continues without it" or "No bait in inventory; Auto Bait will check again in 5s",COLORS.warn)
        return false, "bait tool not found"
    end
    RemoteCatalog.BaitOutOfStock=false
    RemoteCatalog.BaitSample={Character=character,Tool=baitTool,Count=count,Binding=binding,At=os.clock()}
    if count==0 then
        RemoteCatalog.BaitOutOfStock=true;RemoteCatalog.BaitPending=false
        baitInfo.Text="No bait remaining. Fishing continues; checking again in 5s."
        return false,"bait tool not found"
    end
    if RemoteCatalog.PrepareBaitLearning then RemoteCatalog.PrepareBaitLearning() end
    RemoteCatalog.BaitAttemptedBinding=nil
    local ok, result = RemoteCatalog.WithBaitTool(baitTool, function() return RemoteCatalog.CallBait(baitTool, true) end)
    if not ok then
        if result~="Waiting for current rod input" and result~="Waiting for the current cast to finish" and result~="Auto Bait stopped" then
            -- Normal input can capture a NEW request even when consumption is late.
            -- Reject only the exact learned template that was actually replayed.
            if RemoteCatalog.BaitAttemptedBinding then
                RemoteCatalog.BaitFailedBinding=RemoteCatalog.BaitAttemptedBinding
            end
            RemoteCatalog.BaitNeedsRefresh=true
            if RemoteCatalog.BaitTransport=="game food request" then
                RemoteCatalog.BaitNativeFailed={Character=character,Tool=baitTool}
                if RemoteCatalog.BaitNativeStatus=="Food request sent; awaiting consumption" then
                    RemoteCatalog.BaitNativeStatus="Food request sent but consumption was not observed"
                end
            end
            -- A successful mouse API call can still produce no game input on this host.
            -- Try one other normal activation method on the next scheduled attempt.
            local transport=RemoteCatalog.BaitTransport or ""
            -- Give an observed food mouse handler a turn after normal mouse input fails.
            RemoteCatalog.BaitTryMouseHandler=(transport=="host mouse bait click" or transport=="virtual mouse bait click")
                and RemoteCatalog.BaitFoodMouseAvailable and RemoteCatalog.BaitFoodMouseAvailable(baitTool) or false
            local signal=transport=="Tool.Activated signal" or transport=="Tool.Activated food handler"
            if transport=="Tool.Activated signal" then RemoteCatalog.BaitDirectSignalFailed=true end
            local handlers=transport=="Tool.Activated signal" and type(getconnections)=="function" and type(getfenv)=="function"
            RemoteCatalog.BaitTrySignal=handlers or not signal and transport~="Tool:Activate" and RemoteCatalog.BaitSignalAvailable()
            RemoteCatalog.BaitTryToolActivation=signal or transport~="Tool:Activate" and not RemoteCatalog.BaitTrySignal
        end
        baitInfo.Text = "Bait waiting: " .. tostring(result) .. (enabled and " Fishing continues." or " Fishing is OFF.") .. " ["..tostring(RemoteCatalog.BaitTransport or "no request").."]"
        baitInfo.TextColor3 = COLORS.warn
        if result~="Waiting for current rod input" and result~="Waiting for the current cast to finish" then
            setStatus("Auto Bait failed: " .. tostring(result))
        end
    else
        RemoteCatalog.BaitNeedsRefresh=false
        RemoteCatalog.BaitFailedBinding=nil
        local learned=RemoteCatalog.LiveRecorder("BaitBinding")
        RemoteCatalog.BaitSample={Character=character,Tool=baitTool,Count=RemoteCatalog.BaitCount(baitTool),Binding=learned and learned.BaitBinding,At=os.clock()}
        baitInfo.Text = "Bait consumption confirmed. Refreshes after 60 seconds."
        baitInfo.TextColor3 = COLORS.good
    end
    RemoteCatalog.BaitLastResult=tostring(result)
    return ok, result
end

local function setBaitEnabled(state)
    if baitEnabled == state then
        if not state and RemoteCatalog.StopBaitLearning then RemoteCatalog.StopBaitLearning() end
        return
    end
    RemoteCatalog.BaitPending=state
    if state then
        RemoteCatalog.BaitInputCharacter=nil
        RemoteCatalog.BaitTryMouseHandler=nil
        RemoteCatalog.BaitNativeFailed=nil
        RemoteCatalog.BaitDirectSignalFailed=nil
        RemoteCatalog.BaitTrySignal=RemoteCatalog.BaitSignalAvailable()
        RemoteCatalog.BaitTryToolActivation=not RemoteCatalog.BaitTrySignal
    end
    RemoteCatalog.StartFishingOnBait=state
    baitEnabled = state
    baitCycleId += 1
    local myCycle = baitCycleId
    baitToggle.Text = baitEnabled and "AUTO BAIT ON · CHECKING" or "AUTO BAIT OFF  [60s]"
    baitToggle.BackgroundColor3 = baitEnabled and COLORS.on or COLORS.off
    if not baitEnabled then
        if RemoteCatalog.StopBaitLearning then RemoteCatalog.StopBaitLearning() end
        setStatus("Auto Bait stopped")
        return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while baitEnabled and baitCycleId == myCycle and gui.Parent do
            RemoteCatalog.BaitPending=true
            local succeeded,applied,detail=pcall(applyBait)
            if not baitEnabled or baitCycleId ~= myCycle or not gui.Parent then return end
            if not succeeded then
                detail="Unexpected bait error: "..tostring(applied)
                applied=false
                baitBusy=false
                RemoteCatalog.BaitPending=false
                RemoteCatalog.BaitLastResult=detail
                baitInfo.Text=detail.." Retrying; Fishing stays under your control."
                baitInfo.TextColor3=COLORS.warn
                setStatus(detail,COLORS.warn)
            end
            local waiting=not applied and (detail=="Waiting for current rod input" or detail=="Waiting for the current cast to finish")
            RemoteCatalog.BaitPending=waiting
            if waiting then
                baitInfo.Text="Bait queued: finishing the current cast first."
                baitToggle.Text="AUTO BAIT ON · QUEUED"
                task.wait(.1)
                continue
            end
            if applied and detail=="bait consumption confirmed" and RemoteCatalog.StartFishingOnBait then
                RemoteCatalog.StartFishingOnBait=false
                if not enabled then setEnabled(true) end
            end
            if applied then setStatus(detail == "tool activation attempted" and "Bait tool activated; next attempt in 60s" or "Auto Bait applied; refresh in 60s", COLORS.good) end
            baitToggle.BackgroundColor3 = applied and COLORS.on or COLORS.off
            local delay = applied and (RemoteCatalog.BaitRefreshDelay or 60) or 5
            RemoteCatalog.BaitRefreshDelay=nil
            for remaining = delay, 1, -1 do
                if not baitEnabled or baitCycleId ~= myCycle or not gui.Parent then return end
                baitToggle.Text = string.format(applied and "AUTO BAIT ON  [%ds]" or RemoteCatalog.BaitOutOfStock and "AUTO BAIT ON · NO BAIT  [%ds]" or "AUTO BAIT ON · RETRY  [%ds]", remaining)
                task.wait(1)
            end
        end
    end)
end

RemoteCatalog.LearnBaitButton=button({Parent=fishingPage,Text="Learn bait manually (pause fishing & bait)",TextSize=12,
    Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,32),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,452)})
track(RemoteCatalog.LearnBaitButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    if baitBusy then setStatus("Wait for the current bait attempt to finish",COLORS.warn);return end
    setBaitEnabled(false)
    setEnabled(false)
    local recorder=RemoteCatalog.LiveRecorder("BaitBinding")
    RemoteCatalog.BaitSample=nil
    for _,tool in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.OwnedTools()) do
        if string.lower(tool.Name)=="bait" then
            RemoteCatalog.BaitSample={Character=player.Character,Tool=tool,Count=RemoteCatalog.BaitCount(tool),
                Binding=recorder and recorder.BaitBinding,At=os.clock(),Manual=true}
            break
        end
    end
    RemoteCatalog.BaitFailedBinding=recorder and recorder.BaitBinding
    RemoteCatalog.BaitNeedsRefresh=true
    RemoteCatalog.PrepareBaitLearning()
    local capture=RemoteCatalog.Recorder
    if capture and not capture.Stopped and (capture.Active or capture.LearnBaitOnly) then
        baitInfo.Text="Hide the hub, use bait once manually, then immediately turn Auto Bait ON. Learning sends no requests."
        baitInfo.TextColor3=COLORS.good
        setStatus("Manual bait learning ready. Hide the hub with Right Ctrl and use bait once.",COLORS.good)
    else
        setStatus("Manual bait capture is unavailable on this client",COLORS.warn)
    end
end))

    end
end

RemoteCatalog.ChestControlPositions={}
for _,child in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.ChestPage:GetChildren()) do
    if child:IsA("GuiObject") and child~=RemoteCatalog.ChestSelectMenu and child~=RemoteCatalog.ChestAmountMenu and child~=RemoteCatalog.ChestItemMenu then
        RemoteCatalog.ChestControlPositions[child]=child.Position
    end
end
function RemoteCatalog.LayoutChestControls()
    local selectOffset=RemoteCatalog.ChestSelectMenu.Visible and RemoteCatalog.ChestSelectMenu.Size.Y.Offset+8 or 0
    local amountOffset=RemoteCatalog.ChestAmountMenu.Visible and RemoteCatalog.ChestAmountMenu.Size.Y.Offset+8 or 0
    local itemOffset=RemoteCatalog.ChestItemMenu.Visible and RemoteCatalog.ChestItemMenu.Size.Y.Offset+8 or 0
    for child,base in pairs(RemoteCatalog.ChestControlPositions) do
        local y=base.Y.Offset
        if y>=328 then y+=selectOffset end
        if base.Y.Offset>=642 then y+=amountOffset end
        if base.Y.Offset>=260 then y+=itemOffset end
        child.Position=UDim2.new(base.X.Scale,base.X.Offset,base.Y.Scale,y)
    end
    RemoteCatalog.ChestAmountMenu.Position=UDim2.new(.5,3,0,628+selectOffset+itemOffset)
    RemoteCatalog.ChestSelectMenu.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,326+itemOffset)
end

    RemoteCatalog.ChestSelectMenu.Visible=false;RemoteCatalog.ChestAmountMenu.Visible=false;RemoteCatalog.ChestItemMenu.Visible=false
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestItemSearch then pcall(function() RemoteCatalog.ChestItemSearch:ReleaseFocus() end) end
    RemoteCatalog.LayoutChestControls()
end)

RemoteCatalog.TravelSelectors={}
RemoteCatalog.TravelKeys={}
function RemoteCatalog.TravelSelector(page,y)
    local key=page==woodPage and "WoodTravelMode" or "FarmTravelMode"
    local title=page==woodPage and "Mode: " or "Travel: "
    local modes=page==woodPage and {"Normal","Walk","Tween","Teleport"} or {"Walk","Tween","Teleport"}
    local selector=button({Parent=page,Text=title..CONFIG[key].."  ▾",TextSize=12,Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,32),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,y)})
    table.insert(RemoteCatalog.TravelSelectors,selector)
    RemoteCatalog.TravelKeys[selector]=key
    local options={}
    for i,mode in ipairs(modes) do
        local option=button({Parent=page,Text=mode,TextSize=12,Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,30),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,y+34+(i-1)*31)})
        option.Visible=false;option.ZIndex=20
        table.insert(options,option)
        track(option.Activated:Connect(function()
            CONFIG[key]=mode
            RemoteCatalog.Navigation.Stop()
            for _,item in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.TravelSelectors) do
                if RemoteCatalog.TravelKeys[item]==key then item.Text=title..mode.."  ▾" end
            end
            for _,item in ipairs(options) do item.Visible=false end
            savePreferences()
        end))
    end
    local function close()
        for _,item in ipairs(options) do item.Visible=false end
        selector.Text=title..CONFIG[key].."  ▾"
    end
    table.insert(RemoteCatalog.MenuClosers,close)
    track(selector.Activated:Connect(function()
        local show=not options[1].Visible
        RemoteCatalog.CloseMenus()
        for _,item in ipairs(options) do item.Visible=show end
        selector.Text=title..CONFIG[key]..(show and "  ▴" or "  ▾")
    end))
end

local function makeRow(parent, labelText, order, valueColor)
    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    row.LayoutOrder = order
    row.Parent = parent

    label({
        Parent = row, Text = labelText, TextSize = 13, Color = COLORS.muted,
        Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 1, 0),
    })
    return label({
        Parent = row, Text = "0", TextSize = 13, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        Color = valueColor or COLORS.text, Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
        Size = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.55, 0),
    })
end

for index,mode in ipairs({"fishing","crop","wood","mine"}) do

RemoteCatalog.GoalHint=label({Parent=statsPage,Text="Goals start Unlimited. Choose a limit, then start the mode. Each run starts at zero. Harvest goals count confirmed targets, not stack quantities.",TextSize=12,Color=COLORS.muted,

function RemoteCatalog.CycleModeGoal(mode)
    local goal=RemoteCatalog.ModeGoals[mode]
    RemoteCatalog.StopGoalMode(mode)
    local limits={0,10,25,50,100}
    local index=table.find(limits,goal.Limit or 0) or 1
    local limit=limits[index%#limits+1]
    goal.Limit=limit>0 and limit or nil;goal.Progress=0;goal.Reached=false
    RemoteCatalog.UpdateModeGoals()
    setStatus(RemoteCatalog.GoalNames[mode].." goal updated. Start that mode when ready.",COLORS.muted)
end
function RemoteCatalog.UpdateModeGoals()
    for mode,goal in pairs(RemoteCatalog.ModeGoals) do
        goal.Button.Text=RemoteCatalog.GoalNames[mode]..(goal.Limit and (": "..goal.Progress.."/"..goal.Limit..(goal.Reached and " ✓" or "")) or " goal: Unlimited")
        goal.Button.BackgroundColor3=goal.Reached and COLORS.on or COLORS.panel
        if goal.PageButton then
            goal.PageButton.Text=goal.Button.Text
            goal.PageButton.BackgroundColor3=goal.Button.BackgroundColor3
        end
    end
end
function RemoteCatalog.ResetModeGoal(mode)
    local goal=RemoteCatalog.ModeGoals[mode]
    goal.Progress=0;goal.Reached=false
    RemoteCatalog.UpdateModeGoals()
end
function RemoteCatalog.ProgressModeGoal(mode)
    local goal=RemoteCatalog.ModeGoals[mode]
    local active=mode=="fishing" and enabled or mode=="crop" and harvestEnabled or mode=="wood" and woodEnabled or mode=="mine" and mineEnabled
    if goal and active and not goal.Reached then goal.Progress+=1 end
end
function RemoteCatalog.CompleteModeGoal(mode)
    local goal=RemoteCatalog.ModeGoals[mode]
    if not goal or not goal.Limit or goal.Reached or goal.Progress<goal.Limit then return end
    goal.Reached=true
    RemoteCatalog.StopGoalMode(mode)
    RemoteCatalog.UpdateModeGoals()
    setStatus(RemoteCatalog.GoalNames[mode].." goal reached: "..goal.Progress..". That mode is stopped.",COLORS.good)
end
function RemoteCatalog.UpdateDashboard()
    if not gui.Parent then return end
    RemoteCatalog.UpdateModeGoals()
    local deadline=RemoteCatalog.StopTimerDeadline
    if deadline and os.clock()>=deadline then
        RemoteCatalog.StopTimerDeadline=nil
        RemoteCatalog.StopAllAutomation()
        setStatus("Session timer finished. All automation stopped.",COLORS.warn)
    end
    local remaining=RemoteCatalog.StopTimerDeadline and math.max(0,math.ceil(RemoteCatalog.StopTimerDeadline-os.clock()))
    RemoteCatalog.StopTimerButton.Text=remaining and "Cancel stop timer" or "Start stop timer"
    RemoteCatalog.StopTimerButton.BackgroundColor3=remaining and COLORS.on or COLORS.off
    RemoteCatalog.StopTimerHint.Text=remaining and ("Stops all automation in "..math.floor(remaining/60)..":"..string.format("%02d",remaining%60)..". Cancel to keep playing.")
        or "Timer is OFF. Starting it does not enable any mode. Expiry uses Stop All."
    local function state(value) return value and "ON" or "OFF" end
    local chest=RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoEnabled and "collecting" or RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoWorker and "stopping" or RemoteCatalog.ChestBusy and "manual request" or "idle"
    RemoteCatalog.DashboardStatus.Text="Fishing "..state(enabled).." · Bait "..state(baitEnabled)..
        "\nHarvest "..state(harvestEnabled).." · Wood "..state(woodEnabled).." · Mining "..state(mineEnabled)..
        "\nChests: "..chest.." · Build "..(buildController.Running and "ON" or buildController.WorkerToken and "stopping" or "OFF")..
        "\nConfirmed chest items this run: "..tostring(RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoTotal or 0)
end

-- ANTI AFK BEGIN
RemoteCatalog.AntiAFKClass=(function()
-- Idle-event driven; no input loop and no startup listener.
local AntiAFK={}
function AntiAFK.new(player,virtualUser,camera,changed)
    return setmetatable({Player=player,VirtualUser=virtualUser,Camera=camera,Changed=changed,Enabled=false}, {__index=AntiAFK})
end
function AntiAFK:SetEnabled(state)
    state=state==true and not self.Destroyed
    if self.Enabled==state then return self.Enabled end
    self.Enabled=state
    if self.Connection then self.Connection:Disconnect();self.Connection=nil end
    if state then
        local ok,connection=pcall(function()
            return self.Player.Idled:Connect(function()
                if not self.Enabled or self.Destroyed then return end
                local sent,failure=pcall(function()
                    local user=self.VirtualUser()
                    local camera=self.Camera()
                    if not camera then error("Camera unavailable") end
                    user:CaptureController()
                    user:ClickButton2(Vector2.zero,camera.CFrame)
                end)
                if not sent then
                    self:SetEnabled(false)
                    if self.Changed then self.Changed(false,"input unavailable") end
                elseif self.Changed then self.Changed(true,"idle response sent") end
            end)
        end)
        if not ok then
            self.Enabled=false
            if self.Changed then self.Changed(false,"idle event unavailable") end
            return false
        end
        self.Connection=connection
    end
    if self.Changed then self.Changed(self.Enabled) end
    return self.Enabled
end
function AntiAFK:Destroy()
    self:SetEnabled(false)
    self.Destroyed=true
end
return AntiAFK

end)()
RemoteCatalog.AntiAFKButton=button({Parent=settingsPage,Text="ANTI AFK OFF",TextSize=12,Bg=COLORS.off,
    Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,32),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,52)})
RemoteCatalog.AntiAFK=RemoteCatalog.AntiAFKClass.new(player,function() return game:GetService("VirtualUser") end,
    function() return workspace.CurrentCamera end,function(on,reason)
        RemoteCatalog.AntiAFKButton.Text=(on and "ANTI AFK ON" or "ANTI AFK OFF")..(reason and " · "..reason or "")
        RemoteCatalog.AntiAFKButton.BackgroundColor3=on and COLORS.on or COLORS.off
    end)
track(RemoteCatalog.AntiAFKButton.Activated:Connect(function() RemoteCatalog.AntiAFK:SetEnabled(not RemoteCatalog.AntiAFK.Enabled) end))
RemoteCatalog.AntiAFK:SetEnabled(true)
-- ANTI AFK END

local function resizeHub(factor)
    local size = frame.Size
    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
        math.clamp(math.round(size.X.Offset * factor), 640, 1080),
        math.clamp(math.round(size.Y.Offset * factor), 420, 705)
    )
    hub:_fitToScreen(true)
    hub:_clampToScreen()
end
track(smallerUI.Activated:Connect(function() resizeHub(0.9) end))
track(largerUI.Activated:Connect(function() resizeHub(1.1) end))
