    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(720, 470)
    frame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    hub:_fitToScreen(true)
    hub:_clampToScreen()
end))

-- MOVEMENT UI BEGIN
local movementController = (function()
    local self = {WalkEnabled=false,JumpEnabled=false,Speed=20,JumpPower=60,JumpHeight=10}
    local originals = setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
    local function humanoid()
        local char = player.Character
        return char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    end
    local function override(hum,property,value)
        local original=originals[hum]
        if not original then original={};originals[hum]=original end
        if original[property]==nil then original[property]=hum[property] end
        hum[property]=value
    end
    local function restore(hum,property)
        local original=originals[hum]
        if original and original[property]~=nil then
            hum[property]=original[property]
            original[property]=nil
        end
    end
    local info = label({Parent=movementPage,Text="Adjust walking and jumping. Changes apply only while enabled; Reset restores your character's original values.",
        TextSize=12,Color=COLORS.muted,Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,48),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,4)})
    info.TextWrapped=true
    local walk = button({Parent=movementPage,Text="Custom walk: OFF",Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,36),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,66)})
    local speed = button({Parent=movementPage,Text="Walk speed: 20  ▾",Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,34),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,110)})
    local jump = button({Parent=movementPage,Text="Custom jump: OFF",Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,36),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,162)})
    local height = button({Parent=movementPage,Text="Jump strength: Medium  ▾",Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,34),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,206)})
    local reset = button({Parent=movementPage,Text="Reset movement",Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,34),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,258)})
    self.Status=label({Parent=movementPage,Text="Movement uses the game's character settings; the server may override changes.",
        TextSize=12,Color=COLORS.muted,Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,42),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,302)})
    self.Status.TextWrapped=true
    function self:Apply(feature)
        walk.Text=self.WalkEnabled and "Custom walk: ON" or "Custom walk: OFF"
        jump.Text=self.JumpEnabled and "Custom jump: ON" or "Custom jump: OFF"
        walk.BackgroundColor3=self.WalkEnabled and COLORS.on or COLORS.off
        jump.BackgroundColor3=self.JumpEnabled and COLORS.on or COLORS.off
        local hum=humanoid()
        if not hum then self.Status.Text="Wait for your character to spawn.";return end
        if feature~="jump" then
            if self.WalkEnabled then override(hum,"WalkSpeed",self.Speed)
            else restore(hum,"WalkSpeed") end
        end
        if feature~="walk" then
            local property=hum.UseJumpPower and "JumpPower" or "JumpHeight"
            local other=hum.UseJumpPower and "JumpHeight" or "JumpPower"
            restore(hum,other)
            if self.JumpEnabled then override(hum,property,self[property])
            else restore(hum,property) end
        end
        self.Status.Text="Movement updated. Reset restores only settings changed here."
    end
    function self:Reset()
        self.WalkEnabled,self.JumpEnabled=false,false
        local hum=humanoid()
        if hum then
            restore(hum,"WalkSpeed");restore(hum,"JumpPower");restore(hum,"JumpHeight")
        end
        walk.Text="Custom walk: OFF";jump.Text="Custom jump: OFF"
        walk.BackgroundColor3=COLORS.off;jump.BackgroundColor3=COLORS.off
        self.Status.Text="Original movement restored."
    end
    track(walk.Activated:Connect(function() self.WalkEnabled=not self.WalkEnabled;self:Apply("walk") end))
    track(jump.Activated:Connect(function() self.JumpEnabled=not self.JumpEnabled;self:Apply("jump") end))
    track(reset.Activated:Connect(function() self:Reset() end))
    local function dropdown(anchor, options, y, select)
        local menu=Instance.new("Frame");menu.BackgroundColor3=COLORS.panel;menu.BorderSizePixel=0
        menu.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,#options*36);menu.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,y);menu.ZIndex=30;menu.Visible=false;menu.Parent=movementPage;corner(menu,8)
        table.insert(self.Menus,menu)
        table.insert(RemoteCatalog.MenuClosers,function() menu.Visible=false end)
        for i, option in ipairs(options) do
            local choice=button({Parent=menu,Text=tostring(option),Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,34),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(2,(i-1)*36)})
            choice.ZIndex=31
            track(choice.Activated:Connect(function() select(i);menu.Visible=false end))
        end
        track(anchor.Activated:Connect(function()
            local open=not menu.Visible
            RemoteCatalog.CloseMenus()
            menu.Visible=open
        end))
    end
    self.Menus={}
    local speeds={16,20,24,32}
    dropdown(speed,speeds,146,function(i) self.Speed=speeds[i];speed.Text="Walk speed: "..self.Speed.."  ▾";if self.WalkEnabled then self:Apply("walk") end end)
    local powers={50,60,75};local heights={7.2,10,14};local names={"Normal","Medium","High"}
    dropdown(height,names,242,function(i)
        self.JumpPower,self.JumpHeight=powers[i],heights[i];height.Text="Jump strength: "..names[i].."  ▾"
        if self.JumpEnabled then self:Apply("jump") end
    end)
    track(player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        if self.WalkEnabled or self.JumpEnabled then
            task.spawn(function()
                local hum=char:WaitForChild("Humanoid",5)
                if hum and player.Character==char and gui.Parent and (self.WalkEnabled or self.JumpEnabled) then self:Apply() end
            end)
        end
    end))
    self.WalkButton,self.JumpButton,self.ResetButton=walk,jump,reset
    return self
end)()
-- MOVEMENT UI END

function RemoteCatalog.UpdateCaptureUI()
    local recorder = RemoteCatalog.Recorder
    if not recorder then
        RemoteCatalog.CaptureStatus.Text = RemoteCatalog.CaptureAttempted
            and "Capture unavailable: this host does not provide working hook APIs."
            or "Recorder OFF. Farming uses the game API; capture is optional."
        RemoteCatalog.CaptureToggle.Text = RemoteCatalog.CaptureAttempted and "Retry capture setup" or "Start capture (optional)"
        return
    end
    local state = recorder.LearnChestOnly and "Learning chests; press Stop learning when finished" or recorder.LearnBaitOnly and "Waiting for one bait use (no call log)" or recorder.LearnWoodOnly and "Learning axe request; normal input stays available (no call log)" or (recorder.Stopped and "Stopped" or (recorder.Active and "Recording" or "Paused"))
    local learned = {}
    for _, name in ipairs({"OEzzgCCBzocxJw", "ohy6O10QKzyGZE", "raqRasAwcny1zh", "RZQQNNDRUvfj1N"}) do
        if RemoteCatalog.RequestId(name) then table.insert(learned, RemoteCatalog.VerifiedRequests[name]) end
    end
    if recorder.HarvestBinding then table.insert(learned, "crop harvest") end
    if recorder.BaitBinding then table.insert(learned, "bait (current remote)") end
    if recorder.PlacementBinding then table.insert(learned, "placement (current remote)") end
    if recorder.WoodHitBinding then table.insert(learned, "axe / wood hit") end
    if recorder.MineHitBinding then table.insert(learned, "mining hit") end
    if recorder.AutoReplantBinding then table.insert(learned, "game AutoReplant") end
    if recorder.FruitBinding then table.insert(learned, "fruit picking") end
    if recorder.ChestBinding then table.insert(learned, "chest items") end
    if recorder.HotbarBinding then table.insert(learned, "hotbar (current remote)") end
    local mining = #learned > 0 and ("Ready: " .. table.concat(learned, ", ")) or "Perform actions manually once to learn their request IDs"
    RemoteCatalog.CaptureStatus.Text = RemoteCatalog.CopyNotice or string.format("%s: %d / %d calls. %s.", state, #recorder.Records, recorder.MaxCalls, mining)
    RemoteCatalog.CaptureToggle.Text = recorder.Active and "Pause capture (F8)" or "Resume capture (F8)"
end
function RemoteCatalog.EnableCapture()
    RemoteCatalog.CaptureAttempted = true
    local ok, recorder = pcall(RemoteCatalog.StartCapture)
    RemoteCatalog.Recorder = ok and recorder or nil
    RemoteCatalog.UpdateCaptureUI()
end
function RemoteCatalog.PrepareBaitLearning()
    RemoteCatalog.AcceptFreshBaitBinding()
    if not RemoteCatalog.BaitNeedsRefresh and RemoteCatalog.LiveRecorder("BaitBinding") then return end
    local recorder=RemoteCatalog.Recorder
    if recorder and not recorder.Stopped and (recorder.Active or recorder.LearnBaitOnly) then return end
    local ok,result=pcall(RemoteCatalog.StartCapture)
    if ok and result then
        RemoteCatalog.Recorder=result
        result.LearnBaitOnly=true
        result.Active=false
        RemoteCatalog.UpdateCaptureUI()
    end
end
function RemoteCatalog.StopBaitLearning()
    local recorder=RemoteCatalog.Recorder
    if recorder and recorder.LearnBaitOnly then
        recorder.LearnBaitOnly=false
        if not recorder.LearnWoodOnly and not recorder.LearnChestOnly then recorder:Stop() end
    end
end
function RemoteCatalog.PrepareWoodLearning()
    if RemoteCatalog.LiveRecorder("WoodHitBinding") or RemoteCatalog.GameEndpoint("BLOCK_HIT_REQUEST") then return end
    local recorder=RemoteCatalog.Recorder
    if recorder and not recorder.Stopped and recorder.Active then return end
    local ok,result=pcall(RemoteCatalog.StartCapture)
    if ok and result then
        RemoteCatalog.Recorder=result
        result.LearnWoodOnly=true
        result.Active=false
        RemoteCatalog.UpdateCaptureUI()
    end
end
function RemoteCatalog.StopWoodLearning()
    local recorder=RemoteCatalog.Recorder
    if recorder and recorder.LearnWoodOnly then recorder.LearnWoodOnly=false;if not recorder.LearnBaitOnly and not recorder.LearnChestOnly then recorder:Stop() end end
end
track(RemoteCatalog.CaptureToggle.Activated:Connect(function()
    RemoteCatalog.CopyNotice = nil
    if RemoteCatalog.Recorder then RemoteCatalog.Recorder.LearnWoodOnly=false;RemoteCatalog.Recorder.LearnBaitOnly=false;RemoteCatalog.Recorder.LearnChestOnly=false end
    local recorder = RemoteCatalog.Recorder
    if not recorder then
        RemoteCatalog.EnableCapture()
    elseif recorder.Active then
        recorder.Active = false
    else
        recorder:Resume()
    end
    RemoteCatalog.UpdateCaptureUI()
end))
track(RemoteCatalog.CaptureExport.Activated:Connect(function()
    if RemoteCatalog.Recorder then
        local ok, result, message = pcall(function() return RemoteCatalog.Recorder:CopyAll() end)
        -- Keep the result visible until the next action/status refresh.
        if ok then
            RemoteCatalog.CopyNotice = message
        else
            RemoteCatalog.CopyNotice = "Kopiering fejlede; brug F9 til eksport."
        end
        RemoteCatalog.UpdateCaptureUI()
    end
end))
track(RemoteCatalog.CaptureStop.Activated:Connect(function()
    if RemoteCatalog.Recorder then RemoteCatalog.Recorder:Stop() end
    RemoteCatalog.UpdateCaptureUI()
end))
RemoteCatalog.UpdateCaptureUI()

        TextSize = 11, Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 28), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 306 + (i - 1) * 28) })
end
function RemoteCatalog.RefreshUI()
    local found = RemoteCatalog.Refresh()
    RemoteCatalog.Summary.Text = string.format("Remotes found: %d / %d", found, #RemoteCatalog.Entries)
    for _, entry in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.Entries) do
        entry.Label.Text = (entry.Instance and "OK  " or "Missing  ") .. string.match(entry.Path, "([^.]+)$") .. "  [" .. entry.Class .. "]"
        entry.Label.TextColor3 = entry.Instance and COLORS.good or COLORS.muted
    end
end
track(RemoteCatalog.RefreshButton.Activated:Connect(RemoteCatalog.RefreshUI))
RemoteCatalog.RefreshUI()

--------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Stats
--------------------------------------------------------------------
local stats

local function newStats()
	return {
		casts = 0,
		bites = 0,
		catches = 0,
		missed = 0,
		streak = 0,
		bestStreak = 0,
		harvests = 0,
        resourceCounts = {wood=0,mine=0,crop=0},
        resourceAttempts = {wood=0,mine=0,crop=0},
        resourceLast = {},
		lastMineName = "–",
		activeTime = 0,
		onSince = nil,
		perFish = {},   -- [name] = count
		recent = {},    -- { {name=, at=}, ... } newest first
	}
end

stats = newStats()

local function getActiveTime()
	local t = stats.activeTime
	if stats.onSince then
		t += os.clock() - stats.onSince
	end
	return t
end

local function formatTime(seconds)
	seconds = math.floor(seconds)
	return string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d",
		math.floor(seconds / 3600),
		math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60),
		seconds % 60)
end

local function formatAgo(seconds)
	seconds = math.floor(seconds)
	if seconds < 60 then
		return seconds .. "s ago"
	elseif seconds < 3600 then
		return math.floor(seconds / 60) .. "m ago"
	end
	return math.floor(seconds / 3600) .. "h ago"
end

local function getTopFish()
	local bestName, bestCount = nil, 0
	for name, count in pairs(stats.perFish) do
		if count > bestCount then
			bestName, bestCount = name, count
		end
	end
	if bestName then
		return bestName .. " ×" .. bestCount
	end
	return "–"
end

function RemoteCatalog.SessionReport()
    local text={"Reborn build-layers-48", "Active time: "..formatTime(getActiveTime()),
        "Fishing: "..stats.catches.." catches, "..stats.casts.." casts, "..stats.bites.." bites, "..stats.missed.." missed",
        "Resources: harvest "..stats.resourceCounts.crop..", wood "..stats.resourceCounts.wood..", mining "..stats.resourceCounts.mine,
        "Chest run: "..tostring(RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoTotal or 0).." confirmed items",
        "Modes: Fishing "..(enabled and "ON" or "OFF")..", Bait "..(baitEnabled and "ON" or "OFF")..", Harvest "..(harvestEnabled and "ON" or "OFF")..", Wood "..(woodEnabled and "ON" or "OFF")..", Mining "..(mineEnabled and "ON" or "OFF")}
    local build=buildController.Progress
    if build then table.insert(text,"Build run: "..build.State.." · "..build.Placed.." confirmed · "..build.Skipped.." skipped · "..build.Failed.." failed · "..build.Total.." planned") end
    for _,mode in ipairs({"fishing","crop","wood","mine"}) do
        local goal=RemoteCatalog.ModeGoals[mode]
        table.insert(text,RemoteCatalog.GoalNames[mode].." goal: "..goal.Progress.."/"..tostring(goal.Limit or "unlimited")..(goal.Reached and " reached" or ""))
    end
    local fish={}
    for name in pairs(stats.perFish) do table.insert(fish,name) end
    table.sort(fish)
    for index,name in ipairs(fish) do
        if index>10 then table.insert(text,"More fish types: "..(#fish-10));break end
        table.insert(text,"Fish "..name..": "..stats.perFish[name])
    end
    return table.concat(text,"\n")
end
function RemoteCatalog.CopySessionReport()
    local report=RemoteCatalog.SessionReport()
    local copy=setclipboard or toclipboard
    if type(copy)=="function" and pcall(copy,report) then setStatus("Session report copied",COLORS.good)
    else print(report);setStatus("Session report printed to console",COLORS.warn) end
    return report
end
local function updateBoard()
    RemoteCatalog.UpdateDashboard()
    if RemoteCatalog.UpdateChestSelectionUI then RemoteCatalog.UpdateChestSelectionUI() end
    if RemoteCatalog.UpdateTeleportLabels then RemoteCatalog.UpdateTeleportLabels() end
    RemoteCatalog.BaitNativeLabel.Text=RemoteCatalog.BaitNativeStatus or "Food API: not checked yet"
    RemoteCatalog.UpdateCaptureUI()
	local t = getActiveTime()

	rows.harvests.Text = tostring(stats.harvests)
	rows.mineRate.Text = t >= 5
		and string.format("%.0f", stats.harvests / (t / 60))
		or "–"
	rows.lastMine.Text = stats.lastMineName or "–"
	rows.catches.Text = tostring(stats.catches)
	rows.missed.Text = tostring(stats.missed)
	rows.casts.Text = tostring(stats.casts)
	rows.bites.Text = tostring(stats.bites)
	rows.rate.Text = stats.bites > 0
		and string.format("%d%%", math.floor(stats.catches / stats.bites * 100 + 0.5))
		or "–"
    local rateText = stats.bites > 0 and string.format("%d%%", math.floor(stats.catches / stats.bites * 100 + 0.5)) or "–"
    local hourly = t >= 30 and string.format("%.0f", stats.catches / (t / 3600)) or "–"
    local baitState = "OFF"
    if baitEnabled then
        local countdown = baitToggle.Text:match("%[(.-)%]") or "ON"
        baitState = string.find(baitToggle.Text, "RETRY", 1, true) and ("Retry in " .. countdown) or countdown
    end
    overlayValues.catches.Text = tostring(stats.catches)
    overlayValues.hourly.Text = hourly
    overlayValues.attempts.Text = stats.casts .. " / " .. stats.bites
    overlayValues.success.Text = rateText .. " / " .. stats.missed
    overlayValues.session.Text = formatTime(t) .. " / " .. string.upper(activeFishingMode)
    overlayValues.harvests.Text = tostring(stats.harvests)
    overlayValues.bait.Text = baitState
    overlayValues.bait.TextColor3 = not baitEnabled and COLORS.muted
        or (string.find(baitToggle.Text, "RETRY", 1, true) and COLORS.warn or COLORS.good)
    local selected=hub.CurrentTab and hub.CurrentTab.Name
    local category=selected=="Wood Farm" and "wood" or selected=="Mining" and "mine" or selected=="Harvest" and "crop"
    if not category and selected~="Fishing" then category=woodEnabled and "wood" or mineEnabled and "mine" or harvestEnabled and "crop" end
    if category then
        local title=category=="wood" and "WOOD" or category=="mine" and "MINING" or "CROP"
        local count=stats.resourceCounts[category]
        local on=category=="wood" and woodEnabled or category=="mine" and mineEnabled or category=="crop" and harvestEnabled
        statsOverlayTitle.Text=title.." STATS"
        overlayTitles.catches.Text=category=="wood" and "WOOD HARVESTS" or category=="mine" and "BLOCKS MINED" or "CROPS PICKED"
        overlayTitles.hourly.Text="PER HOUR"
        overlayValues.catches.Text=tostring(count)
        overlayValues.hourly.Text=t>=30 and string.format("%.0f",count/(t/3600)) or "–"
        overlayLabels.attempts.Text="Actions / collected"
        overlayValues.attempts.Text=stats.resourceAttempts[category].." / "..count
        overlayLabels.success.Text="Range / state"
        overlayValues.success.Text=FarmLogic.Range(category,CONFIG).." studs / "..(on and "ON" or "OFF")
        overlayValues.session.Text=formatTime(t).." / "..title
        overlayLabels.harvests.Text="Last collected"
        overlayValues.harvests.Text=stats.resourceLast[category] or "–"
        overlayLabels.bait.Text=category=="crop" and "Crop" or "Tool"
        overlayValues.bait.Text=category=="wood" and woodToolPreference or category=="mine" and mineToolPreference or (#FarmLogic.SelectedCrops(CONFIG.HarvestCrops,CONFIG.HarvestCrop)>1 and tostring(#FarmLogic.SelectedCrops(CONFIG.HarvestCrops,CONFIG.HarvestCrop)).." selected" or CONFIG.HarvestCrop)
        overlayValues.bait.TextColor3=COLORS.text
    else
        statsOverlayTitle.Text="FISHING STATS"
        overlayTitles.catches.Text="FISH CAUGHT";overlayTitles.hourly.Text="FISH / HOUR"
        overlayLabels.attempts.Text="Casts / bites";overlayLabels.success.Text="Catch rate / misses"
        overlayLabels.harvests.Text="Harvests";overlayLabels.bait.Text="Bait"
    end
	rows.streak.Text = stats.streak .. " / " .. stats.bestStreak
	rows.perHour.Text = t >= 30
		and string.format("%.0f", stats.catches / (t / 3600))
		or "–"
	rows.time.Text = formatTime(t)

	local now = os.clock()
	for i, slot in ipairs(recentLabels) do
		local entry = stats.recent[i]
		if entry then
			slot.name.Text = "🐟 " .. entry.name
			slot.name.TextColor3 = COLORS.text
			slot.time.Text = formatAgo(now - entry.at)
		else
			slot.name.Text = i == 1 and "Nothing yet" or ""
			slot.name.TextColor3 = COLORS.muted
			slot.time.Text = ""
		end
	end
end

local function flash(lbl, color)
	lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	lbl.TextSize = 15
	TweenService:Create(lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
		TextColor3 = color,
		TextSize = 12,
	}):Play()
end

local function registerCatch(fishName)
	fishName = fishName or "Fish"

	stats.catches += 1
	stats.streak += 1
	stats.perFish[fishName] = (stats.perFish[fishName] or 0) + 1

	table.insert(stats.recent, 1, { name = fishName, at = os.clock() })
	if #stats.recent > CONFIG.RecentCount then
		table.remove(stats.recent)
	end

	if stats.streak > stats.bestStreak then
		stats.bestStreak = stats.streak
		if stats.bestStreak > 1 then
			flash(rows.streak, COLORS.gold)
		end
	end

	flash(rows.catches, COLORS.good)
    RemoteCatalog.ProgressModeGoal("fishing")
	updateBoard()
end

local function registerMiss()
	stats.missed += 1
	stats.streak = 0
	flash(rows.missed, COLORS.bad)
	updateBoard()
end

local function registerHarvest(name, category)
    category=category or FarmLogic.Category(name or "")
    if category and stats.resourceCounts[category]~=nil then
        stats.resourceCounts[category]+=1;stats.resourceLast[category]=name
        RemoteCatalog.ProgressModeGoal(category)
    end
	stats.harvests += 1
	if name then
		stats.lastMineName = name
	end
	if stats.harvests % 10 == 0 then
		flash(rows.harvests, COLORS.harvest)
	end
	updateBoard()
end

local function refreshModeStatus()
    if modeStatus then
        modeStatus.Text = string.format("Fishing: %s  |  Bait: %s  |  Crops: %s  |  Wood: %s  |  Mining: %s",
            enabled and "ON" or "OFF", baitEnabled and "ON" or "OFF",
            harvestEnabled and "ON" or "OFF", woodEnabled and "ON" or "OFF", mineEnabled and "ON" or "OFF")
        modeStatus.TextColor3 = (enabled or harvestEnabled or woodEnabled or mineEnabled or baitEnabled) and COLORS.good or COLORS.muted
    end
end

--------------------------------------------------------------------
-- New-item detection (used to name the fish you caught)
-- Rods moving between Backpack and Character are the same instance,
-- so only genuinely new instances count.
--------------------------------------------------------------------
local knownItems = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local lastNewItem = nil -- { name = string, at = number }

local function markKnown(container)
	for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
		knownItems[child] = true
	end
end

local function onItemAdded(child)
	if not knownItems[child] then
		knownItems[child] = true
		lastNewItem = { name = child.Name, at = os.clock() }
	end
end

local function hookBackpack(backpack)
	markKnown(backpack)
	track(backpack.ChildAdded:Connect(onItemAdded))
end

local currentBackpack = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
if currentBackpack then
	hookBackpack(currentBackpack)
end

track(player.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
	if child:IsA("Backpack") then
		hookBackpack(child)
	end
end))

if player.Character then
	markKnown(player.Character)
end
track(player.CharacterAdded:Connect(markKnown))

--------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Fishing helpers
--------------------------------------------------------------------
local cycleId = 0
local harvestCycleId = 0
local lastAction = 0

local function running(myCycle)
	return enabled and cycleId == myCycle and gui.Parent ~= nil
end

local function harvestRunning(myCycle)
	return harvestEnabled and harvestCycleId == myCycle and gui.Parent ~= nil
end

-- Note: VirtualInputManager is normally blocked in a standard LocalScript.
local function hasRodEquipped()
    local character = player.Character
    local tool = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    return tool ~= nil and (string.find(string.lower(tool.Name), "fishing", 1, true) ~= nil
        or string.find(string.lower(tool.Name), "rod", 1, true) ~= nil)
end
RemoteCatalog.EquipFishingRod=function(myCycle)
    if baitBusy or fishingInputBusy or not running(myCycle) then return false,"Waiting for bait/input to finish" end
    local character=player.Character
    if not character then return false,"Waiting for your character" end
    if hasRodEquipped() then return true end
    for _,owned in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.OwnedTools()) do
        local name=string.lower(owned.Name)
        if string.find(name,"fishing",1,true) or string.find(name,"rod",1,true) then
            if not running(myCycle) or player.Character~=character then return false,"Fishing stopped" end
            local ok,equipped=pcall(RemoteCatalog.EquipTool,owned)
            if not ok or not equipped then return false,"Could not equip your fishing rod" end
            task.wait(.1)
            if not running(myCycle) or player.Character~=character then return false,"Fishing stopped" end
            return hasRodEquipped(),"Fishing rod selected; waiting for equip confirmation"
        end
    end
    return false,"No fishing rod found in your inventory"
end
local lastFishingAim
local function clickOnce(action, myCycle)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if baitBusy or not camera or not hasRodEquipped() or not running(myCycle) then return false end
    local character = player.Character
    local tool=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    local function activeInput()
        if baitBusy or not running(myCycle) or player.Character~=character
            or not character or character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")~=tool then return false end
        local ok,focused=pcall(function() return UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() end)
        return not (ok and focused)
    end
    if not activeInput() then return false end
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local x, y = camera.ViewportSize.X * 0.7, camera.ViewportSize.Y * 0.6
    if action == "reel" and lastFishingAim then
        x, y = lastFishingAim.X, lastFishingAim.Y
    elseif root then
        local aim = root.Position + root.CFrame.LookVector * 12 - Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
        local point, visible = camera:WorldToScreenPoint(aim)
        if visible then x, y = point.X, point.Y end
    end
    local function overPanel(px, py)
        if not hub.Root.Visible then return false end
        local position, size = hub.Root.AbsolutePosition, hub.Root.AbsoluteSize
        return px >= position.X and px <= position.X + size.X and py >= position.Y and py <= position.Y + size.Y
    end
    if action ~= "reel" and overPanel(x, y) then x, y = camera.ViewportSize.X * 0.8, camera.ViewportSize.Y * 0.6 end
    if overPanel(x, y) then
        setStatus("Move the hub away from the water aiming area.", COLORS.warn)
        return false
    end
    local function clearInputPoint()
        if overPanel(x,y) then return false end
        local ok,objects=pcall(function() return playerGui:GetGuiObjectsAtPosition(x,y) end)
        if ok then
            for _,object in ipairs(objects) do
                if object:IsA("GuiButton") or object:IsA("TextBox") then return false end
            end
        end
        return true
    end
    if not clearInputPoint() then
        setStatus("Move on-screen controls away from the fishing aim.",COLORS.warn)
        return false
    end
    -- Update the Mouse ray first; button events alone leave the aim over the UI.
    -- The user or another tool may have moved the cursor since the cast.
    -- Restore the saved aim for every reel as well as every new cast.
    local moved = pcall(function() VirtualInputManager:SendMouseMoveEvent(x, y, game) end)
    if not moved then return false end
    task.wait(action == "reel" and .01 or .03)
    if not activeInput() or not clearInputPoint() then return false end
    lastFishingAim = {X=x,Y=y}
    fishingInputBusy = true
    RemoteCatalog.FishingMouseInput=true
    local pressed = pcall(function() VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(x, y, 0, true, game, 0) end)
    local held = pcall(function()
        if not pressed then return end
        local deadline = os.clock() + (action == "cast" and CONFIG.CastHold or 0.01)
        while activeInput() and os.clock() < deadline do task.wait(0.01) end
    end)
    -- Release independently of both input errors and Stop during the charge.
    local released = pcall(function() VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(x, y, 0, false, game, 0) end)
    fishingInputBusy = false
    RemoteCatalog.FishingMouseInput=false
    return pressed and held and released and activeInput()
end
local function getBobber()
    local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("FishingBobbers")
    if not folder then return nil end
    local exact = folder:FindFirstChild(player.Name .. "Bobber")
    if exact and (exact:IsA("BasePart") or exact:IsA("Model")) then return exact end
    for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
        local owner = child:GetAttribute("OwnerUserId") or child:GetAttribute("PlayerUserId")
        if owner == player.UserId and (child:IsA("BasePart") or child:IsA("Model")) then return child end
    end
    return nil
end

RemoteCatalog.BobberActive = function() return getBobber() ~= nil end

local function waitForTug(bobber, myCycle)
	local tugged = false
    local deadline = os.clock() + CONFIG.BiteTimeout

	local function checkTug()
		local value = bobber:GetAttribute("Tug")
		if value and value ~= false then
			tugged = true
		end
	end

	checkTug()
	local conn = bobber:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Tug"):Connect(checkTug)

	while running(myCycle) and bobber.Parent and not tugged and os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait(CONFIG.FishingPollInterval)
	end

	conn:Disconnect()
	return tugged and running(myCycle), not tugged and bobber.Parent ~= nil and os.clock() >= deadline
end

-- Polls until predicate() is true, the cycle stops, or timeout. Returns predicate result.
local function waitUntil(myCycle, timeout, predicate)
	local deadline = os.clock() + timeout
	while running(myCycle) and os.clock() < deadline do
		if predicate() then
			return true
		end
		task.wait(CONFIG.FishingPollInterval)
	end
	-- A stopped/restarted cycle must not report a stale success after cancellation.
	return running(myCycle) and predicate()
end

-- FISHING RECOVERY BEGIN
RemoteCatalog.RecoverFishingCast=function(myCycle,bobber)
    if not running(myCycle) or baitBusy or fishingInputBusy then return false end
    if player:GetAttribute("ToolsLocked")==true then return false end
    local loading=player:FindFirstChild("SessionLoading")
    if loading and loading.Value then return false end
    local focusedOK,focused=pcall(function() return UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() end)
    if focusedOK and focused then return false end
    local character=player.Character
    local rod=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if not character or not hasRodEquipped() then
        RemoteCatalog.EquipFishingRod(myCycle)
        return false
    end
    local function active()
        return running(myCycle) and player.Character==character and not baitBusy
    end
    local current=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if current~=rod then return false end
    setStatus("Resetting stuck cast: putting the rod away...",COLORS.warn)
    local service=RemoteCatalog.GameModule("Players."..player.Name..".PlayerScripts.TS.inventory.client-inventory-service","ClientInventoryService")
    if not active() or character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")~=rod then return false end
    local toggled=false
    if service then
        local slot=service.selectedHotbarIndex
        if slot~=nil and service.hotbar and service.hotbar[slot]==rod then
            toggled=pcall(function() service:setSelectedHotbarIndex(slot) end)
        end
    end
    if not toggled then
        local humanoid=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if humanoid then pcall(function() humanoid:UnequipTools() end) end
    end
    -- Never destroy/ignore a server bobber or claim a catch to unblock bait.
    local cleared=waitUntil(myCycle,2,function()
        return not active() or not bobber.Parent or getBobber()~=bobber
    end)
    if not active() then return false end
    cleared=cleared and (not bobber.Parent or getBobber()~=bobber)
    local selected=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if not selected and (not cleared or not RemoteCatalog.BaitPending) then
        pcall(RemoteCatalog.EquipTool,rod)
    end
    if cleared then
        setStatus("Stuck cast cleared; continuing fishing and queued bait.",COLORS.good)
    else
        setStatus("Cast reset not confirmed; Fishing stays ON and will retry.",COLORS.warn)
    end
    return cleared
end
-- FISHING RECOVERY END

--------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Auto Mine (Cobalt remotes for Islands)
-- Works with stone, iron, coal, ores, rocks, trees, etc.
--------------------------------------------------------------------
-- These paths come from the Cobalt snippets you provided.
-- They can break after game updates; re-spy if needed.

-- Plant a crop at a CFrame (from Cobalt plant capture)
local function firePlant(cf, blockType)
    local character = player.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then
        local names = RemoteCatalog.SeedNames(blockType or CONFIG.PlantType)
        for _, tool in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.OwnedTools()) do
            if table.find(names, tool.Name) then
                if not RemoteCatalog.EquipTool(tool) then return false, "Could not equip seed tool" end
                break
            end
        end
    end
    return RemoteCatalog.CallPlacement({ cframe = cf, blockType = blockType or CONFIG.PlantType })
end

-- Plant a grid of crops around the player
local function plantAroundPlayer(blockType,token)
    if not RemoteCatalog.PlacementAvailable() then
        setStatus("Place one block manually with capture active first.", COLORS.warn)
        return 0
    end
	local char = player.Character
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hrp then
		return 0
	end
	blockType = blockType or CONFIG.PlantType
	local origin = hrp.Position
	local planted = 0
	local r = CONFIG.PlantRadius
	local step = CONFIG.PlantSpacing

	for x = -r, r, step do
		for z = -r, r, step do
            if not gui.Parent or player.Character~=char or RemoteCatalog.PlantGeneration~=token then return planted end
			local pos = Vector3.new(origin.X + x, origin.Y, origin.Z + z)
			-- Keep upright rotation like the Cobalt sample
			local cf = CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0)
			if firePlant(cf, blockType) then
				planted += 1
			end
			task.wait(CONFIG.PlantDelay)
		end
	end
	return planted
end

-- Hotbar layout remote (from Cobalt)
-- Example: "woodPickaxe,,sapling,hoe,berryHarvested,ironOre,saplingOrange,yellowFlowerSeeds"
local function setHotbarLayout(layoutString)
    return RemoteCatalog.CallHotbar(layoutString)
end

-- Shared tool support from the Auto Wood Farmer.
-- Prefer an axe when available; otherwise fall back to a pickaxe.
local function equipBestResourceTool(category)
    local character = player.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return false end

    local preference = category == "wood" and woodToolPreference or mineToolPreference
    local preferenceName=string.lower(preference)
    local wantAxe = category == "wood" or string.find(preferenceName,"axe",1,true) and not string.find(preferenceName,"pickaxe",1,true)
    local best, bestScore = nil, -math.huge
    local fallback, fallbackScore = nil, -math.huge
    for _, tool in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.OwnedTools()) do
        local name = string.lower(tool.Name)
        local isPick = string.find(name, "pickaxe", 1, true) ~= nil
        local isAxe = string.find(name, "axe", 1, true) ~= nil and not isPick
        if (wantAxe and isAxe) or (not wantAxe and isPick) then
            local score = 1
            if string.find(name, "mythic", 1, true) then score = 100
            elseif string.find(name, "diamond", 1, true) then score = 90
            elseif string.find(name, "gold", 1, true) then score = 80
            elseif string.find(name, "steel", 1, true) then score = 70
            elseif string.find(name, "iron", 1, true) then score = 60
            elseif string.find(name, "stone", 1, true) then score = 40
            elseif string.find(name, "wood", 1, true) then score = 20 end
            if score > fallbackScore then fallback, fallbackScore = tool, score end
            if not string.find(string.lower(preference), "auto best", 1, true) then
                local wanted = string.lower(preference):gsub(" axe", ""):gsub(" pickaxe", ""):gsub("^wooden$", "wood")
                if not string.find(name, wanted, 1, true) then
                    score = -math.huge -- Prefer the requested material; recover to an owned tool if absent.
                else
                    score = score + 1000
                end
            end
            if score > bestScore then best, bestScore = tool, score end
        end
    end
    if not best and fallback then
        best=fallback;bestScore=fallbackScore
        if category == "wood" then
            woodToolPreference="Auto best axe";woodToolIndex=1
            if woodToolSelect then woodToolSelect.Text="Axe: Auto best axe  ▼" end
            if woodStatus then woodStatus.Text=preference.." unavailable. Using "..fallback.Name.."." end
        else
            mineToolPreference=wantAxe and "Auto best axe" or "Auto best pickaxe";mineToolIndex=table.find(mineToolOptions or {},mineToolPreference) or 1
            if mineToolSelect then mineToolSelect.Text="Tool: "..mineToolPreference.."  ▼" end
            if mineInfo then mineInfo.Text=preference.." unavailable. Using "..fallback.Name.."." end
        end
        if type(savePreferences)=="function" then savePreferences() end
    end
    if best and bestScore > -math.huge then
        local ok = RemoteCatalog.EquipTool(best)
        local err = "inventory tool selection unavailable"
        if not ok then
            if setStatus then setStatus("Tool equip failed: " .. tostring(err), COLORS.bad) end
            return false
        end
        return true
    end
    if setStatus then
        setStatus(wantAxe and "No axe found. Put an axe in your inventory." or "No pickaxe found. Put a pickaxe in your inventory.", COLORS.warn)
    end
    return false
end

local function equipPickaxe()
    return equipBestResourceTool(woodEnabled and "wood" or "mine")
end

local function getPlayerIsland()
    local islands = workspace:FindFirstChild("Islands")
    if not islands then return nil end
    local owned = islands:FindFirstChild(tostring(player.UserId) .. "-island")
    if owned then return owned end
    for _, island in ipairs(islands:GetChildren()) do
        if island:GetAttribute("OwnerUserId") == player.UserId then return island end
    end
    return nil
end

function RemoteCatalog.BuildIslandBlocks()
    local island=getPlayerIsland()
    return island and island:FindFirstChild("Blocks")
end
local function getPartPosition(obj)
	if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
		return obj.Position
	elseif obj:IsA("Model") then
        -- Model pivots may be far from their rendered rocks/crops.
        local boxed, bounds = pcall(function() return obj:GetBoundingBox() end)
        if boxed and bounds then return bounds.Position end
		local ok, pivot = pcall(function()
			return obj:GetPivot().Position
		end)
		if ok and pivot then
			return pivot
		end
	end
	local part = obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
	if part then
		return part.Position
	end
	return nil
end

local function targetCategory(name) return FarmLogic.Category(name) end
local function modeAllows(name)
    local category = targetCategory(name)
    if category == "crop" then return harvestEnabled and FarmLogic.CropsMatch(name, CONFIG.HarvestCrops, CONFIG.HarvestCrop) end
    if category == "wood" then return woodEnabled end
    return category == "mine" and mineEnabled
end
local FarmState = { Cooldowns = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }), FruitTrees = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) }
local function getMineTargets()
    local targets, seen = {}, {}
    local char = player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return targets end
    local function consider(obj, allowCrops)
        if seen[obj] then return end
        seen[obj] = true
        local category = targetCategory(obj.Name)
        if not category or not modeAllows(obj.Name) then return end
        if category == "crop" and not allowCrops then return end
        if category == "wood" and allowCrops and harvestEnabled and CONFIG.FruitEnabled then
            if FarmState.FruitTrees[obj] or string.find(string.lower(obj.Name),"treeorange",1,true) or #FarmLogic.Fruits(obj)>0 then
                FarmState.FruitTrees[obj]=true;return
            end
        end
        if category == "crop" and not FarmLogic.IsReady(obj) then return end
        if (FarmState.Cooldowns[obj] or 0) > os.clock() then return end
        local position = getPartPosition(obj)
        if not position then return end
        local distance = (position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
        if distance > FarmLogic.Range(category, CONFIG) then return end
        table.insert(targets, {kind = category == "crop" and "model" or "block",
            instance = obj, name = obj.Name, distance = distance, category = category})
    end
    local function scan(folder, allowCrops)
        if folder then for _, obj in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do consider(obj, allowCrops) end end
    end
    local function scanResources(folder, depth)
        if not folder or depth < 0 then return end
        for _, obj in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            local category = targetCategory(obj.Name)
            local resource = category == "wood" or category == "mine"
            local containsResources = false
            if obj:IsA("Model") then
                for _, child in ipairs(obj:GetChildren()) do
                    local childCategory = targetCategory(child.Name)
                    if child:IsA("Model") and (childCategory == "wood" or childCategory == "mine") then containsResources = true; break end
                end
            end
            if resource and not containsResources and (obj:IsA("Model") or obj:IsA("BasePart")) then
                consider(obj, false)
            elseif not obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                scanResources(obj, depth - 1)
            end
        end
    end
    local prefabs = workspace:FindFirstChild("spawnPrefabs")
    if woodEnabled or mineEnabled then scanResources(prefabs, 5) end
    local owned = getPlayerIsland()
    local islands = workspace:FindFirstChild("Islands")
    if islands then
        for _, island in ipairs(islands:GetChildren()) do
            if island == owned or woodEnabled or mineEnabled then
                scan(island:FindFirstChild("Blocks"), island == owned)
                scan(island:FindFirstChild("Prefabs"), island == owned)
            end
        end
    end
    if harvestEnabled and CONFIG.FruitEnabled and owned then
        local blocks=owned:FindFirstChild("Blocks")
        if blocks then for _,tree in ipairs(blocks:GetChildren()) do
            for _,fruit in ipairs(FarmLogic.Fruits(tree)) do
                local position=getPartPosition(fruit)
                if position and not seen[fruit] and (FarmState.Cooldowns[fruit] or 0)<=os.clock() then
                    local distance=(position-hrp.Position).Magnitude
                    if distance<=CONFIG.CropRange then
                        seen[fruit]=true;FarmState.FruitTrees[tree]=true
                        table.insert(targets,{kind="fruit",instance=fruit,model=tree,name=tree.Name.." fruit",distance=distance,category="crop"})
                    end
                end
            end
        end end
    end
    table.sort(targets, function(a, b) return a.distance < b.distance end)
    return targets
end

-- RESOURCE AIM BEGIN
local function getInteractionPoint(target, origin, visiblePoint, maxDistance)
    local best, distance
    local function candidate(point)
        local magnitude=(point-origin).Magnitude
        if maxDistance and magnitude>maxDistance then return end
        if visiblePoint and not visiblePoint(point) then return end
        if not distance or magnitude<distance then best,distance=point,magnitude end
    end
    local function consider(part)
        if not part:IsA("BasePart") then return end
        local ok,closest=pcall(function() return part:GetClosestPointOnSurface(origin) end)
        if ok and closest then candidate(closest) end
        candidate(part.Position)
        if part.CFrame and part.Size then
            candidate(part.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0,-part.Size.Y*.25,0)))
            candidate(part.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0,part.Size.Y*.25,0)))
        end
    end
    if target:IsA("BasePart") then consider(target) end
    -- Some tree roots are invisible Parts with the rendered mesh beneath them.
    for _,part in ipairs(target:GetDescendants()) do consider(part) end
    if visiblePoint then return best end
    return best or getPartPosition(target)
end
function RemoteCatalog.ResourcePosition(target)
    local char=player.Character
    local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    return root and getInteractionPoint(target,root.Position) or getPartPosition(target)
end
-- RESOURCE AIM END

-- FARM NAVIGATION BEGIN
RemoteCatalog.Navigation=(function()
-- Farm travel is idle until an active worker asks to approach a target.
local Navigation={Generation=0}
local function halt(humanoid,root)
    if not humanoid then return end
    -- Clear the outstanding MoveTo destination as well as current movement.
    if root then pcall(function() humanoid:MoveTo(root.Position) end) end
    pcall(function() humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero) end)
end
function Navigation.Stop(owner)
    if owner~=nil and Navigation.Owner~=owner then return false end
    Navigation.Generation+=1
    if Navigation.Tween then pcall(function() Navigation.Tween:Cancel() end);Navigation.Tween=nil end
    local humanoid,root=Navigation.Humanoid,Navigation.Root
    Navigation.Humanoid=nil;Navigation.Root=nil;Navigation.Owner=nil
    halt(humanoid,root)
    return true
end
function Navigation.Approach(player,target,reach,active,positionOf,mode,owner)
    local character=player.Character
    local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root or not humanoid then return false,"Waiting for your character" end
    local generation=Navigation.Generation
    local function valid()
        return active() and Navigation.Generation==generation and player.Character==character
            and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")==root
            and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")==humanoid
            and target.Parent~=nil and humanoid.Health>0
    end
    local function near()
        local point=positionOf(target)
        return point and (point-root.Position).Magnitude<=reach
    end
    if not valid() then return false,"Travel stopped" end
    if near() then return true end
    if mode=="Normal" then return false,"Normal mode: move closer to the target" end
    Navigation.Stop()
    generation=Navigation.Generation
    Navigation.Humanoid=humanoid;Navigation.Root=root;Navigation.Owner=owner
    local ok,result=pcall(function()
        local destination=positionOf(target)
        if not destination then return false end
        -- Stand beside dense bushes rather than pathfinding into their collision geometry.
        destination=destination+(root.Position-destination).Unit*(reach*.6)
        if mode=="Teleport" or mode=="Tween" then
            local destinationFrame=root.CFrame+(destination-root.Position)
            if not valid() then return false end
            if mode=="Teleport" then
                root.CFrame=destinationFrame
                task.wait(.1)
                return valid() and near()
            end
            local duration=math.clamp((destination-root.Position).Magnitude/24,.15,5)
            local tween=game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(root,TweenInfo.new(duration,Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),{CFrame=destinationFrame})
            Navigation.Tween=tween
            tween:Play()
            local deadline=os.clock()+duration+.5
            while valid() and not near() and os.clock()<deadline do task.wait(.05) end
            tween:Cancel()
            if Navigation.Tween==tween then Navigation.Tween=nil end
            return valid() and near()
        end
        local path=game:GetService("PathfindingService"):CreatePath({AgentRadius=2,AgentHeight=5,AgentCanJump=true,WaypointSpacing=4})
        path:ComputeAsync(root.Position,destination)
        if not valid() or path.Status~=Enum.PathStatus.Success then return false end
        local deadline=os.clock()+10
        for _,waypoint in ipairs(path:GetWaypoints()) do
            if not valid() then return false end
            if near() then return true end
            local function distanceToWaypoint()
                local offset=waypoint.Position-root.Position
                return Vector3.new(offset.X,0,offset.Z).Magnitude
            end
            -- Waypoint Y is at the feet; HumanoidRootPart stays above it.
            if waypoint.Action==Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then humanoid.Jump=true end
            humanoid:MoveTo(waypoint.Position)
            local best=distanceToWaypoint()
            local progressed=os.clock()
            while distanceToWaypoint()>2 or math.abs(waypoint.Position.Y-root.Position.Y)>6 do
                if not valid() then return false end
                if near() then return true end
                local distance=distanceToWaypoint()
                if distance<best-.3 then best=distance;progressed=os.clock() end
                if os.clock()>deadline or os.clock()-progressed>1.5 then return false end
                task.wait(.1)
            end
        end
        return near()
    end)
    -- A cancelled old path must not stop a newer worker's movement.
    if Navigation.Generation==generation and Navigation.Humanoid==humanoid then
        if Navigation.Tween then pcall(function() Navigation.Tween:Cancel() end);Navigation.Tween=nil end
        halt(humanoid,root)
        Navigation.Humanoid=nil;Navigation.Root=nil;Navigation.Owner=nil
    end
    return ok and result==true and valid(),ok and "Target not reached; skipping this target" or "Travel unavailable; skipping this target"
end
return Navigation
end)()
-- FARM NAVIGATION END

-- RESOURCE INPUT BEGIN
-- The equipped tool supplies live request names and session data through its own client code.
local function resourceInputHit(target)
    local myCycle = harvestCycleId
    local function active()
        return gui.Parent ~= nil and harvestCycleId == myCycle and target.Parent ~= nil
            and modeAllows(target.Name) and (woodEnabled or mineEnabled or harvestEnabled) and not enabled
    end
    if enabled then return false, "Pause Fishing before using resource input" end
    if not active() then return false, "Resource action stopped" end
    local char = player.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local tool = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    local category = targetCategory(target.Name)
    local toolName = tool and string.lower(tool.Name) or ""
    local pickaxe = string.find(toolName, "pickaxe", 1, true) ~= nil
    local axe = string.find(toolName, "axe", 1, true) ~= nil and not pickaxe
    local choice=type(mineToolPreference)=="string" and string.lower(mineToolPreference) or ""
    local miningAxe=string.find(choice,"axe",1,true) and not string.find(choice,"pickaxe",1,true)
    if not root or category == "mine" and not (miningAxe and axe or not miningAxe and pickaxe) or category == "wood" and not axe then
        return false, "Wait for the matching axe/pickaxe to equip"
    end
    local position = getInteractionPoint(target, root.Position)
    if not position then return false, "Resource has no position" end
    local focused, textBox = pcall(function() return UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() end)
    if focused and textBox then return false, "Close chat or finish editing before resource input" end
    local reach = RemoteCatalog.GameModule("ReplicatedStorage.Shared.SharedConfig", "CLIENT_REACH")
    if type(reach) ~= "number" then reach = 20 end
    if (position - root.Position).Magnitude > reach then
        return false, "Move closer to " .. target.Name .. " (tool reach " .. reach .. " studs)"
    end
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return false, "Camera unavailable" end
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
    position=getInteractionPoint(target,root.Position,function(candidate)
        local screen,visible=camera:WorldToScreenPoint(candidate)
        if not visible or screen.Z<=0 then return false end
        local hit=workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position,candidate-camera.CFrame.Position,params)
        -- No hit means an unobstructed line, including custom non-queryable tree geometry.
        return not hit or hit.Instance==target or hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(target)
    end,reach)
    if not position then return false,"No clear point within reach; face "..target.Name.." or move around the obstacle" end
    local point, visible = camera:WorldToScreenPoint(position)
    if not visible or point.Z <= 0 then return false, "Face " .. target.Name .. " so it is on screen" end
    local x, y = point.X, point.Y
    if hub.Root.Visible then
        local p, size = hub.Root.AbsolutePosition, hub.Root.AbsoluteSize
        if x >= p.X and x <= p.X + size.X and y >= p.Y and y <= p.Y + size.Y then
            return false, "Move the hub away from " .. target.Name
        end
    end
    local checked, objects = pcall(function() return playerGui:GetGuiObjectsAtPosition(x, y) end)
    if checked then
        for _, object in ipairs(objects) do
            if object:IsA("GuiButton") or object:IsA("TextBox") then
                return false, "Move the resource clear of on-screen controls"
            end
        end
    end
    local hostMouse=category~="crop" and type(mousemoveabs)=="function" and type(mouse1press)=="function" and type(mouse1release)=="function"
    local moved = pcall(function()
        if hostMouse then mousemoveabs(x,y) else VirtualInputManager:SendMouseMoveEvent(x,y,game) end
    end)
    if not moved then return false, "Tool input unavailable; record one manual hit to learn this session's request" end
    task.wait(category == "crop" and 0.1 or 0.03)
    if not active() or category ~= "crop" and tool.Parent ~= char then return false, "Resource action stopped or tool changed" end
    local function input(down)
        if category == "crop" then VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(down, Enum.KeyCode.F, false, game)
        elseif hostMouse then if down then mouse1press() else mouse1release() end
        else VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(x, y, 0, down, game, 0) end
    end
    local pressed = pcall(input, true)
    local held = pcall(function() if pressed then task.wait(category == "crop" and 0.1 or 0.06) end end)
    -- Always release even if Stop, UI cleanup or an input error happened during the press.
    local released = pcall(input, false)
    if not pressed or not held or not released then return false, "Tool input unavailable in this environment" end
    return active(), hostMouse and "Host mouse tool input" or "Normal tool input"
end
RemoteCatalog.InputMine = resourceInputHit
RemoteCatalog.InputHarvest = resourceInputHit
function RemoteCatalog.WoodReach(target,origin)
    local reach=RemoteCatalog.GameModule("ReplicatedStorage.Shared.SharedConfig","CLIENT_REACH")
    if type(reach)~="number" or reach<=0 then reach=20 end
    local point=getInteractionPoint(target,origin,nil,reach)
    if not point or (point-origin).Magnitude>reach then
        return false,"Move closer to "..target.Name.." (axe reach "..reach.." studs; scan range is separate)"
    end
    return true
end
-- RESOURCE INPUT END

local function fireBlockMine(block)
    return RemoteCatalog.CallMine(block, targetCategory(block.Name))
end

local function fireModelHarvest(model)
    return RemoteCatalog.CallHarvest(model)
end

local function harvestLoop(myCycle)
    FarmState.LastFailure=FarmState.LastFailure or {}
    local function active()
        return harvestCycleId == myCycle and (harvestEnabled or woodEnabled or mineEnabled) and gui.Parent ~= nil
    end
    while active() do
        if baitBusy then task.wait(0.05);continue end
        local targets = getMineTargets()
        if #targets == 0 then
            local category=harvestEnabled and "crop" or woodEnabled and "wood" or "mine"
            local failure=FarmState.LastFailure[category]
            local scan=category=="crop" and "Scanning your island for selected ready crops." or category=="wood" and "Scanning nearby trees; recent targets may be cooling down." or "Scanning nearby rocks and ores."
            setStatus(failure and (category.." waiting: "..failure) or scan,failure and COLORS.warn or COLORS.muted)
            task.wait(CONFIG.MineScanInterval)
            continue
        end
        for _, target in ipairs(targets) do
            if not active() then break end
            local obj = target.instance
            if not obj.Parent then continue end
            if target.category=="crop" and not FarmLogic.IsReady(obj) then continue end
            if target.kind=="fruit" then
                if not harvestEnabled or not CONFIG.FruitEnabled or not target.model.Parent or not obj:IsDescendantOf(target.model) then continue end
            elseif not modeAllows(obj.Name) then continue end
            local char = player.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local position = getPartPosition(obj)
            if not hrp or not position or (position - hrp.Position).Magnitude > FarmLogic.Range(target.category, CONFIG) then continue end
            if target.category=="crop" then
                local reach=RemoteCatalog.GameModule("ReplicatedStorage.Shared.SharedConfig","CLIENT_REACH")
                reach=type(reach)=="number" and math.min(reach,8) or 8
                if (position-hrp.Position).Magnitude>reach then
                    local reached,reason=false,"Move closer to "..target.name.." (scan range is not interaction range)"
                    if CONFIG.HarvestWalk then
                        setStatus((CONFIG.FarmTravelMode or "Walk").." to "..target.name,COLORS.muted)
                        reached,reason=RemoteCatalog.Navigation.Approach(player,obj,reach,function() return active() and CONFIG.HarvestWalk and harvestEnabled and not enabled end,getPartPosition,CONFIG.FarmTravelMode)
                    end
                    if not active() then break end
                    if not reached then
                        FarmState.Cooldowns[obj]=os.clock()+3
                        FarmState.LastFailure.crop=reason
                        setStatus(reason,COLORS.warn)
                        continue
                    end
                    hrp=player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not hrp then break end
                    position=getPartPosition(obj)
                    if not obj.Parent or not position then continue end
                    if (position-hrp.Position).Magnitude>reach then
                        FarmState.Cooldowns[obj]=os.clock()+3
                        FarmState.LastFailure.crop="Still outside crop interaction reach; trying another target"
                        continue
                    end
                end
            end
            if target.category=="wood" then
                local reachable,reason=RemoteCatalog.WoodReach(obj,hrp.Position)
                if not reachable and CONFIG.WoodTravelMode~="Normal" then
                    setStatus((CONFIG.WoodTravelMode or "Normal").." to "..target.name,COLORS.muted)
                    reachable,reason=RemoteCatalog.Navigation.Approach(player,obj,8,function() return active() and woodEnabled and not enabled end,RemoteCatalog.ResourcePosition,CONFIG.WoodTravelMode)
                    if not active() then break end
                    if reachable then reachable,reason=RemoteCatalog.WoodReach(obj,hrp.Position) end
                end
                if not reachable then
                    FarmState.LastFailure.wood=reason
                    woodStatus.Text=reason
                    setStatus(reason,COLORS.warn)
                    FarmState.Cooldowns[obj]=os.clock()+1
                    continue
                end
            end
            if target.category == "wood" or target.category == "mine" then
                local previous=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                if not equipBestResourceTool(target.category) then FarmState.Cooldowns[obj] = os.clock() + 2; continue end
                if char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")~=previous then task.wait(0.1) end
            end
            if not active() then break end
            -- Tool equip and travel yield; another player may remove this resource meanwhile.
            if not obj.Parent or player.Character~=char or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")~=hrp then continue end
            if target.kind=="fruit" and (not target.model.Parent or not obj:IsDescendantOf(target.model)) then continue end
            position = getPartPosition(obj)
            if not position or (position - hrp.Position).Magnitude > FarmLogic.Range(target.category, CONFIG) then continue end
            local before = FarmLogic.Snapshot(obj)
            if target.category == "crop" and not FarmLogic.IsReady(obj) then continue end
            local recorder = RemoteCatalog.Recorder
            local binding = recorder and recorder.HarvestBinding
            local learned = binding and binding.Remote.Parent and (binding.Target:IsA("Model") == obj:IsA("Model"))
            local nativeHarvest = target.category == "crop" and RemoteCatalog.HarvestEndpoint()
            local prompt = target.kind ~= "fruit" and target.category == "crop" and not learned and not nativeHarvest and FarmLogic.HarvestPrompt(obj) or nil
            local plantedType=target.category=="crop" and target.kind~="fruit" and FarmLogic.ReplantType(target.name,CONFIG.HarvestCrops,CONFIG.PlantType) or nil
            local plantedFrame
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") then plantedFrame = obj.CFrame
            elseif obj:IsA("Model") then plantedFrame = obj:GetPivot() end
            local ok, failure
            if prompt then
                local promptPosition = getPartPosition(prompt.Parent) or position
                if (promptPosition - hrp.Position).Magnitude > prompt.MaxActivationDistance then continue end
                if stats.resourceAttempts then stats.resourceAttempts[target.category]+=1 end
                ok = FarmLogic.HoldPrompt(prompt, active, task.wait, os.clock)
                if not active() then break end
            elseif target.kind == "fruit" then
                if stats.resourceAttempts then stats.resourceAttempts.crop+=1 end
                ok,failure=RemoteCatalog.CallFruit(target.model,obj)
                if not ok then setStatus("Fruit picking waiting: "..tostring(failure),COLORS.warn) end
            elseif target.kind == "model" then
                if stats.resourceAttempts then stats.resourceAttempts[target.category]+=1 end
                ok, failure = fireModelHarvest(obj)
                if not ok then setStatus("Harvest waiting: " .. tostring(failure), COLORS.warn) end
            else
                if stats.resourceAttempts then stats.resourceAttempts[target.category]+=1 end
                ok, failure = fireBlockMine(obj)
                if not ok then setStatus("Mining waiting: " .. tostring(failure), COLORS.warn) end
            end
            FarmState.LastFailure[target.category]=not ok and tostring(failure or "input not accepted") or nil
            if target.category=="wood" and woodStatus then
                woodStatus.Text=ok and ("Request sent to "..target.name.."; damage not yet confirmed.") or ("Wood waiting: "..tostring(failure))
            end
            local persistentBerry=target.category=="crop" and target.kind~="fruit" and string.find(string.lower(target.name),"berry",1,true)~=nil
            local confirmed = false
            if ok then
                local deadline = os.clock() + (persistentBerry and 0.15 or 0.8)
                repeat
                    if not active() then break end
                    confirmed = FarmLogic.Confirmed(obj, before, prompt)
                    if confirmed then break end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                until os.clock() >= deadline
            end
            if not active() then break end
            if ok and not confirmed then setStatus(persistentBerry and "Berry request sent; checking the next bush (collection unconfirmed)" or ("No target change confirmed; retrying " .. target.name), COLORS.muted) end
            if confirmed then
                registerHarvest(target.name, target.category)
                setStatus("Confirmed: " .. target.name .. " ×" .. stats.harvests, COLORS.good)
                -- A plant that regrows in place must never be planted over.
                if target.category == "crop" and target.kind ~= "fruit" and not obj.Parent and plantedFrame and CONFIG.PlantEnabled and CONFIG.AutoReplant and not (RemoteCatalog.ServerReplantEnabled()) then
                    if RemoteCatalog.PlacementAvailable() then
                        if active() then firePlant(plantedFrame, plantedType) end
                    else
                        setStatus("Harvest confirmed. Place one crop manually to enable replanting.", COLORS.warn)
                    end
                end
            end
            if confirmed then RemoteCatalog.CompleteModeGoal(target.category) end
            if not active() then break end
            local quickCrop=target.category=="crop" and (confirmed or persistentBerry and ok)
            FarmState.Cooldowns[obj] = os.clock() + (persistentBerry and ok and 15 or quickCrop and 0.08 or ok and 0.25 or 2)
            task.wait(quickCrop and math.min(CONFIG.MineDelay,0.08) or CONFIG.MineDelay)
        end
        -- All candidates may be out of prompt range; always yield before rescanning.
        task.wait(CONFIG.MineScanInterval)
    end
end

--------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Main fishing loop
--------------------------------------------------------------------
local setHarvestEnabled

local function run(myCycle)
	local failedCasts = 0
    local countedBites = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    local countedMisses = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    local reelAttempts = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

	while running(myCycle) do
        if baitBusy then task.wait(0.05); continue end
		local bobber = getBobber()

		if bobber then
            if (reelAttempts[bobber] or 0)>=3 then
                RemoteCatalog.RecoverFishingCast(myCycle,bobber)
                reelAttempts[bobber]=0
                task.wait(1)
                continue
            end
			failedCasts = 0
			setStatus("Waiting for fish...")
			local tugged, timedOut = waitForTug(bobber, myCycle)
			if not running(myCycle) then break end

			if timedOut then
                setStatus("No bite yet — resetting the cast and continuing without requiring bait.", COLORS.warn)
                RemoteCatalog.RecoverFishingCast(myCycle,bobber)
                task.wait(1)
                continue
            elseif not tugged then
				setStatus("Bobber disappeared")
				task.wait(0.1)
			elseif os.clock() - lastAction >= CONFIG.ActionCooldown then
				lastAction = os.clock()
				local reelTime = os.clock()
				if not countedBites[bobber] then stats.bites += 1; countedBites[bobber] = true end
				updateBoard()
				setStatus("Tug! Reeling in...", COLORS.gold)
                reelAttempts[bobber]=(reelAttempts[bobber] or 0)+1
                if not clickOnce("reel", myCycle) then
                    if not running(myCycle) then break end
                    setStatus("Reel input blocked; retrying.", COLORS.warn)
                    task.wait(0.25)
                    continue
                end
				task.wait(CONFIG.ReelSettleDelay)
                if not running(myCycle) then break end
				setStatus("Waiting for catch...")
				local gone = waitUntil(myCycle, CONFIG.ReelTimeout, function()
					return getBobber() == nil
				end)
				if not running(myCycle) then break end
				if gone then
					task.wait(0.05)
					local fishName
					if lastNewItem and lastNewItem.at >= reelTime then fishName = lastNewItem.name end
					registerCatch(fishName)
                    RemoteCatalog.CompleteModeGoal("fishing")
                    if not running(myCycle) then break end
					setStatus("Caught " .. (fishName or "a fish") .. "! 🐟", COLORS.good)
				else
                    if not countedMisses[bobber] then
                        countedMisses[bobber]=true
                        registerMiss()
                    end
					setStatus("Reel not confirmed - retrying", COLORS.warn)
					task.wait(0.05)
				end
			end
		elseif baitEnabled and RemoteCatalog.BaitPending then
            setStatus("Applying queued bait before the next cast...",COLORS.muted)
            task.wait(.05)
        elseif not hasRodEquipped() then
            local equipped,reason=RemoteCatalog.EquipFishingRod(myCycle)
            if not running(myCycle) then break end
            setStatus(equipped and "Fishing rod ready" or reason,equipped and COLORS.good or COLORS.warn)
            task.wait(equipped and .05 or .5)
		else
			if os.clock() - lastAction >= CONFIG.ActionCooldown then
				lastAction = os.clock()
				setStatus("Casting...")
				if clickOnce("cast", myCycle) then
					stats.casts += 1
					updateBoard()
					-- Only wait for a bobber after an actual cast input; this avoids
					-- burning the full timeout on frames where the cooldown is active.
					local found = waitUntil(myCycle, CONFIG.CastTimeout, function()
						return getBobber() ~= nil
					end)
					if not running(myCycle) then break end
					if not found then
						failedCasts += 1
						if failedCasts >= CONFIG.MaxFailedCasts then
                            setStatus("No bobber detected — pausing before retrying. Fishing stays ON.", COLORS.warn)
                            task.wait(2)
                            failedCasts=0
                            continue
						end
						setStatus(string.format("No bobber (%d/%d) - retrying", failedCasts, CONFIG.MaxFailedCasts), COLORS.warn)
						 task.wait(0.03)
					end
				else
                    if not running(myCycle) then break end
					setStatus("Input blocked - run in a supported client", COLORS.bad)
					task.wait(0.25)
				end
			else
				task.wait(0.02)
			end
		end
	end

	if cycleId == myCycle and not enabled then
		if not (harvestEnabled or woodEnabled or mineEnabled) then
			setStatus("Idle")
		end
	end
end

--------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Controls
--------------------------------------------------------------------
setEnabled = function(state)
    RemoteCatalog.StartFishingOnBait=false
	if state == enabled then
		return
	end

	enabled = state
	cycleId += 1

	if enabled then
        RemoteCatalog.ResetModeGoal("fishing")
		if not stats.onSince then
			stats.onSince = os.clock()
		end
		toggle.Text = "FISH ON  [" .. CONFIG.ToggleKey.Name .. "]"
		toggle.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.on
		RemoteCatalog.Navigation.Stop()
		setStatus("Fishing started...")
        task.spawn(function(token)
            local ok, failure = pcall(run, token)
            if not ok and token == cycleId then
                setEnabled(false)
                setStatus("Fishing stopped: " .. tostring(failure), COLORS.bad)
            end
        end, cycleId)
	else
		toggle.Text = "FISH OFF  [" .. CONFIG.ToggleKey.Name .. "]"
		toggle.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.off
		if not (harvestEnabled or woodEnabled or mineEnabled) and stats.onSince then
			stats.activeTime += os.clock() - stats.onSince
			stats.onSince = nil
		end
		if not (harvestEnabled or woodEnabled or mineEnabled) then
			setStatus("Idle")
		end
	end

	updateBoard()
end

local function refreshFarmButtons()
    harvestToggle.Text = harvestEnabled and "CROP FARM ON" or "CROP FARM OFF"
    harvestToggle.BackgroundColor3 = harvestEnabled and COLORS.on or COLORS.off
    woodToggle.Text = woodEnabled and "WOOD FARM ON" or "WOOD FARM OFF"
    woodToggle.BackgroundColor3 = woodEnabled and COLORS.on or COLORS.off
    mineToggle.Text = mineEnabled and "MINING ON" or "MINING OFF"
    mineToggle.BackgroundColor3 = mineEnabled and COLORS.on or COLORS.off
end

local function setFarmMode(mode, state)
    -- Resolve the requested mode explicitly. The old chained `and/or`
    -- expression could read another mode's state when this mode was off.
    local current
    if mode == "crop" then
        current = harvestEnabled
    elseif mode == "wood" then
        current = woodEnabled
    elseif mode == "mine" then
        current = mineEnabled
    else
        return
    end
    if current == state then return end
    RemoteCatalog.Navigation.Stop()
    if state then
        RemoteCatalog.ResetModeGoal(mode)
        harvestEnabled,woodEnabled,mineEnabled=false,false,false
        if FarmState and FarmState.LastFailure then FarmState.LastFailure[mode]=nil end
    end
    if mode == "crop" then harvestEnabled = state
    elseif mode == "wood" then woodEnabled = state
    else mineEnabled = state end
    if woodEnabled then RemoteCatalog.PrepareWoodLearning() else RemoteCatalog.StopWoodLearning() end
    harvestCycleId += 1
    refreshFarmButtons()
    refreshModeStatus()
    updateBoard()
    if harvestEnabled or woodEnabled or mineEnabled then
        if not stats.onSince then stats.onSince = os.clock() end
        setStatus(mode == "crop" and "Crop farming started" or mode == "wood" and "Wood farming started" or "Mining started", COLORS.good)
        task.spawn(function(token)
            local ok, failure = pcall(function()
                if mode=="wood" and state and woodEnabled and token==harvestCycleId then
                    local layout=RemoteCatalog.WoodHotbarLayout()
                    if layout then
                        local applied,reason=RemoteCatalog.CallHotbar(layout)
                        if token~=harvestCycleId or not gui.Parent then return end
                        woodStatus.Text=applied and "Wood hotbar ready. Equipping the selected axe for tree hits."
                            or "Hotbar update failed: "..tostring(reason)..". Using owned axe directly."
                    end
                end
                if token==harvestCycleId and gui.Parent then harvestLoop(token) end
            end)
            if not ok and token == harvestCycleId then
                harvestEnabled, woodEnabled, mineEnabled = false, false, false
                refreshFarmButtons()
                refreshModeStatus()
                setStatus("Farm stopped: " .. tostring(failure), COLORS.bad)
            end
        end, harvestCycleId)
    else
        if not enabled and stats.onSince then
            stats.activeTime += os.clock() - stats.onSince
            stats.onSince = nil
        end
        if not enabled then setStatus("Idle") end
    end
    refreshFarmButtons()
    refreshModeStatus()
    updateBoard()
    savePreferences()
end

setHarvestEnabled = function(state) setFarmMode("crop", state) end
function RemoteCatalog.StopGoalMode(mode)
    if mode=="fishing" then setEnabled(false) else setFarmMode(mode,false) end
end

function RemoteCatalog.StopChestLearning()
    local recorder=RemoteCatalog.Recorder
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestLearnButton then RemoteCatalog.ChestLearnButton.Text="Start learning" end
    if recorder and recorder.LearnChestOnly then
        recorder.LearnChestOnly=false;recorder.LearnChestMany=false
        if not recorder.LearnWoodOnly and not recorder.LearnBaitOnly then recorder:Stop() end
    end
end
track(RemoteCatalog.ChestLearnButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    if RemoteCatalog.SetChestAuto then RemoteCatalog.SetChestAuto(false) end
    RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration or 0)+1
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestBusy then setStatus("Wait for the current chest request to finish",COLORS.warn);return end
    local recorder=RemoteCatalog.Recorder
    if recorder and recorder.LearnChestOnly then
        RemoteCatalog.StopChestLearning()
        setStatus("Chest learning stopped",COLORS.warn)
        return
    end
    local ok,result=pcall(RemoteCatalog.StartCapture)
    if not ok or not result then setStatus("This host cannot learn chest calls",COLORS.warn);return end
    RemoteCatalog.Recorder=result
    result.LearnChestOnly=true;result.LearnChestMany=true;result.Active=false
    setStatus("Withdraw each item you want to collect, then press Stop learning",COLORS.good)
    RemoteCatalog.ChestLearnButton.Text="Stop learning"
    RemoteCatalog.UpdateCaptureUI()
end))
-- CHEST TARGETS BEGIN
function RemoteCatalog.ChestTargets()
    local recorders={}
    if RemoteCatalog.Recorder then table.insert(recorders,RemoteCatalog.Recorder) end
    local ok,other=pcall(function() return (type(getgenv)=="function" and getgenv() or _G).RebornCapture end)
    if ok and other and other~=RemoteCatalog.Recorder then table.insert(recorders,other) end
    if #recorders==0 then
        local fallback=RemoteCatalog.LiveRecorder("ChestBinding")
        if fallback then table.insert(recorders,fallback) end
    end
    local targets={}
    for _,recorder in ipairs(recorders) do
        local bindings=recorder.GetChestBindings and recorder:GetChestBindings() or (recorder.ChestBinding and {recorder.ChestBinding} or {})
