        for _,binding in ipairs(bindings) do
            if recorder.CanUseChestBinding and recorder:CanUseChestBinding(binding) then
                local duplicate=false
                for i,entry in ipairs(targets) do
                    if entry.Binding.Chest==binding.Chest and entry.Binding.Slot==binding.Slot then
                        duplicate=true
                        if (binding.ObservedAt or 0)>(entry.Binding.ObservedAt or 0) then targets[i]={Recorder=recorder,Binding=binding} end
                        break
                    end
                end
                if not duplicate then table.insert(targets,{Recorder=recorder,Binding=binding}) end
            end
        end
    end
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestDiscover then
        local island=getPlayerIsland()
        local blocks=island and island:FindFirstChild("Blocks")
        local char=player.Character
        local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if blocks and root then
            local templates=table.clone(targets)
            for _,chest in ipairs(blocks:GetChildren()) do
                for _,entry in ipairs(templates) do
                    local binding=entry.Binding
                    if entry.Recorder.MakeChestTarget and chest.Name==binding.Chest.Name then
                        local exists=false
                        for _,known in ipairs(targets) do if known.Binding.Chest==chest and (known.Binding.Item or "carrotHarvested")== (binding.Item or "carrotHarvested") then exists=true;break end end
                        local position=getPartPosition(chest)
                        if not exists and position and (position-root.Position).Magnitude<=RemoteCatalog.ChestScanRange then
                            local generated=entry.Recorder:MakeChestTarget(binding,chest,binding.Slot)
                            if generated then table.insert(targets,{Recorder=entry.Recorder,Binding=generated}) end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return targets
end
function RemoteCatalog.SelectedChestTargets()
    local targets,seen={},{}
    for _,entry in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.ChestTargets()) do
        local binding=entry.Binding
        local selected=RemoteCatalog.ChestSelection
        local item=binding.Item or "carrotHarvested"
        if (not RemoteCatalog.ChestItems or RemoteCatalog.ChestItems[item]) and (not RemoteCatalog.ChestItem or RemoteCatalog.ChestItem==item) and (not selected or selected.Chest==binding.Chest and (RemoteCatalog.ChestWhole or selected.Slot==binding.Slot)) then
            if RemoteCatalog.ChestWhole and entry.Recorder.MakeChestTarget and string.lower(binding.Chest.Name)=="chestmediumindustrialio" then
                seen[binding.Chest]=seen[binding.Chest] or {}
                if not seen[binding.Chest][item] then
                    seen[binding.Chest][item]=true
                    for slot=0,12 do
                        local generated=entry.Recorder:MakeChestTarget(binding,binding.Chest,slot)
                        if generated then generated.Probe=true;table.insert(targets,{Recorder=entry.Recorder,Binding=generated}) end
                    end
                end
            else
                table.insert(targets,entry)
            end
        end
    end
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestTravel=="Walk" then
        local char=player.Character
        local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then
            local order={}
            for index,entry in ipairs(targets) do order[entry]=index end
            table.sort(targets,function(a,b)
                local ap,bp=getPartPosition(a.Binding.Chest),getPartPosition(b.Binding.Chest)
                local ad=ap and (ap-root.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
                local bd=bp and (bp-root.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
                return ad<bd or ad==bd and order[a]<order[b]
            end)
        end
    end
    return targets
end
-- CHEST TARGETS END
function RemoteCatalog.ChestItemSelectionText()
    if not RemoteCatalog.ChestItems then return "All learned" end
    local items={}
    for item,selected in pairs(RemoteCatalog.ChestItems) do if selected then table.insert(items,item) end end
    table.sort(items)
    return #items==0 and "None selected" or #items==1 and items[1] or #items.." selected"
end
function RemoteCatalog.ToggleChestItems()
    local menu=RemoteCatalog.ChestItemMenu
    if menu.Visible then RemoteCatalog.CloseMenus();return end
    RemoteCatalog.CloseMenus()
    for _,connection in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.ChestItemConnections) do connection:Disconnect() end
    table.clear(RemoteCatalog.ChestItemConnections)
    for _,child in ipairs(menu:GetChildren()) do child:Destroy() end
    local items,seen,options={},{},{}
    for _,entry in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.ChestTargets()) do
        local item=entry.Binding.Item or "carrotHarvested"
        if not seen[item] then seen[item]=true;table.insert(items,item) end
    end
    table.sort(items)
    local search=Instance.new("TextBox")
    search.Name="ItemSearch";search.Text="";search.PlaceholderText="Search learned items…"
    search.ClearTextOnFocus=false;search.TextSize=14;search.TextColor3=COLORS.text
    search.BackgroundColor3=COLORS.panel;search.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,30);search.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(2,2)
    search.ZIndex=41;search.Parent=menu;RemoteCatalog.ChestItemSearch=search
    local empty=label({Parent=menu,Text="No matching items. Your selection is unchanged.",TextSize=12,Color=COLORS.muted,
        Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,44),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(2,68)})
    empty.ZIndex=41;empty.TextWrapped=true
    local none,done
    local function refresh()
        local query=string.lower(search.Text):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        local row,shown=36,0
        for index=0,#items do
            local option,item=options[index],items[index]
            local selected=index==0 and not RemoteCatalog.ChestItems or index>0 and RemoteCatalog.ChestItems and RemoteCatalog.ChestItems[item]
            option.Text=(selected and "✓ " or "")..(item or "All learned items")
            option.BackgroundColor3=selected and COLORS.on or COLORS.off
            option.Visible=index==0 or string.find(string.lower(item),query,1,true)~=nil
            if option.Visible then
                option.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(2,row);row+=32
                if item then shown+=1 end
            end
        end
        empty.Visible=shown==0
        if empty.Visible then empty.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(2,row);row+=46 end
        none.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(2,row);done.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(2,row+32)
        menu.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,math.min(256,row+64))
        RemoteCatalog.ChestItemButton.Text="Items: "..RemoteCatalog.ChestItemSelectionText().."  ▾"
        RemoteCatalog.LayoutChestControls()
    end
    for index=0,#items do
        local item=items[index]
        local option=button({Parent=menu,Text="",TextSize=12,Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,30),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(2,36+index*32)})
        option.ZIndex=41;options[index]=option
        table.insert(RemoteCatalog.ChestItemConnections,option.Activated:Connect(function()
            RemoteCatalog.SetChestAuto(false)
            RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration or 0)+1
            RemoteCatalog.ChestItem=nil
            if not item then RemoteCatalog.ChestItems=nil
            else
                RemoteCatalog.ChestItems=RemoteCatalog.ChestItems or {}
                if RemoteCatalog.ChestItems[item] then RemoteCatalog.ChestItems[item]=nil else RemoteCatalog.ChestItems[item]=true end
            end
            refresh();RemoteCatalog.UpdateChestSelectionUI()
        end))
    end
    none=button({Parent=menu,Text="Select none",TextSize=12,Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,30)})
    none.ZIndex=41
    table.insert(RemoteCatalog.ChestItemConnections,none.Activated:Connect(function()
        RemoteCatalog.SetChestAuto(false)
        RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration or 0)+1
        RemoteCatalog.ChestItem=nil;RemoteCatalog.ChestItems={}
        refresh();RemoteCatalog.UpdateChestSelectionUI()
    end))
    done=button({Parent=menu,Text="Done",TextSize=12,Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,30)})
    done.ZIndex=41
    table.insert(RemoteCatalog.ChestItemConnections,done.Activated:Connect(RemoteCatalog.CloseMenus))
    table.insert(RemoteCatalog.ChestItemConnections,search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        refresh();menu.CanvasPosition=Vector2.zero
    end))
    menu.CanvasPosition=Vector2.zero;menu.Visible=true;refresh()
end
function RemoteCatalog.PreviewChestSelection()
    local targets=RemoteCatalog.SelectedChestTargets()
    local chests,items={},{}
    local count,ready=0,0
    local reasons,reasonOrder={},{}
    for _,entry in ipairs(targets) do
        local binding=entry.Binding
        if not chests[binding.Chest] then chests[binding.Chest]=true;count+=1 end
        items[binding.Item or "carrotHarvested"]=true
        local available,reason=RemoteCatalog.ChestRequestReady(binding)
        if available then ready+=1
        else
            reason=tostring(reason or "Unavailable")
            if not reasons[reason] then reasons[reason]=0;table.insert(reasonOrder,reason) end
            reasons[reason]+=1
        end
    end
    local names={}
    for name in pairs(items) do table.insert(names,name) end
    table.sort(names)
    local itemCount=#names
    while #names>4 do table.remove(names) end
    local text=#targets==0 and "No targets selected. Learn a withdrawal and choose items first." or
        (count.." chests · "..#targets.." slot checks · "..itemCount.." item types\n"..
        ready.." checks in reach; "..(#targets-ready).." need approach or attention.\n"..
        table.concat(names,", ")..(itemCount>4 and " …" or "").."\nInventory contents remain unknown until withdrawal.")
    for index,reason in ipairs(reasonOrder) do
        if index>3 then text..="\nMore blocked checks: refresh after resolving the above.";break end
        text..="\n"..reasons[reason].." checks: "..reason
    end
    RemoteCatalog.ChestPlanLabel.Text=text
    RemoteCatalog.ChestPlanView.CanvasPosition=Vector2.zero
    return text
end
function RemoteCatalog.ChestRequestReady(binding)
    local chest=binding and binding.Chest
    local island=getPlayerIsland()
    local cursor=chest
    while cursor and cursor~=island do cursor=cursor.Parent end
    if not island or cursor~=island then return false,"The learned chest is not on your island" end
    local char=player.Character
    local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local position=getPartPosition(chest)
    if not root or not position or (position-root.Position).Magnitude>20 then
        return false,"Stand within 20 studs of the learned chest"
    end
    return true
end
function RemoteCatalog.WithdrawCarrots(amount,recorder,binding)
    if not recorder then
        local target=RemoteCatalog.SelectedChestTargets()[1]
        recorder=target and target.Recorder
        binding=target and target.Binding
    end
    if not recorder or not recorder.CanUseChestBinding or not recorder:CanUseChestBinding(binding) then
        return false,"Learn an item withdrawal on this character first"
    end
    binding=binding or recorder.ChestBinding
    local ready,reason=RemoteCatalog.ChestRequestReady(binding)
    if not ready then return false,reason end
    return (recorder.WithdrawItems or recorder.WithdrawCarrots)(recorder,amount or (RemoteCatalog.ChestAmount=="Learned" and binding.Amount or RemoteCatalog.ChestAmount),binding)
end
function RemoteCatalog.ChestResultText(value)
    local seen={}
    local function describe(v,depth)
        if type(v)~="table" then return tostring(v):sub(1,200) end
        if seen[v] then return "[cycle]" end
        if depth>3 then return "[nested response]" end
        seen[v]=true
        local parts={}
        for _,key in ipairs({"message","error","reason","code","success","value"}) do
            if v[key]~=nil then table.insert(parts,tostring(key).."="..describe(v[key],depth+1)) end
        end
        if #parts==0 then
            for key,item in pairs(v) do
                table.insert(parts,tostring(key):sub(1,60).."="..describe(item,depth+1))
                if #parts>=6 then break end
            end
        end
        return "{"..table.concat(parts,", ").."}"
    end
    return describe(value,0):sub(1,600)
end
track(RemoteCatalog.ChestCopyButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    local detail=RemoteCatalog.ChestLastDetail or "No chest request sent yet"
    local copy=setclipboard or toclipboard
    if type(copy)=="function" and pcall(copy,detail) then setStatus("Chest result copied",COLORS.good)
    else print(detail);setStatus("Chest result printed to console",COLORS.warn) end
end))
function RemoteCatalog.ApproachChest(recorder,active,binding)
    if not recorder:CanUseChestBinding(binding) then return false,"Learn this chest again" end
    binding=binding or recorder.ChestBinding
    local chest=binding.Chest
    local island=getPlayerIsland()
    local cursor=chest
    while cursor and cursor~=island do cursor=cursor.Parent end
    if not island or cursor~=island then return false,"The learned chest is not on your island" end
    local char=player.Character
    local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local position=getPartPosition(chest)
    if not root or not position then return false,"Waiting for your character and chest" end
    local distance=(position-root.Position).Magnitude
    if distance<=20 then return true end
    if distance>RemoteCatalog.ChestScanRange then return false,"Chest is outside approach range; move closer or increase the range" end
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestTravel~="Walk" then return false,"Stay mode: stand within 20 studs. Approach range controls walking only." end
    if harvestEnabled and CONFIG.HarvestWalk or woodEnabled and CONFIG.WoodTravelMode~="Normal" or mineEnabled then
        return false,"Pause other farming movement before walking to the chest"
    end
    return RemoteCatalog.Navigation.Approach(player,chest,12,function()
        return active() and RemoteCatalog.ChestTravel=="Walk" and recorder:CanUseChestBinding(binding)
    end,getPartPosition,"Walk","Chest")
end
function RemoteCatalog.ChestTargetLabel(binding,all)
    local seen,count={},0
    for _,entry in ipairs(all) do
        local chest=entry.Binding.Chest
        if not seen[chest] then count+=1;seen[chest]=count end
        if chest==binding.Chest then return "Chest #"..seen[chest]..(RemoteCatalog.ChestWhole and " · whole chest" or " · slot "..tostring(binding.Slot or "?")) end
    end
    return "Unavailable chest"
end
function RemoteCatalog.UpdateChestSelectionUI()
    local all=RemoteCatalog.ChestTargets()
    local seen,count={},0
    for _,entry in ipairs(all) do if not seen[entry.Binding.Chest] then seen[entry.Binding.Chest]=true;count+=1 end end
    local selected=RemoteCatalog.ChestSelection
    local text=(RemoteCatalog.ChestDiscover and "All matching chests" or "All learned chests").." ("..count..")"
    if selected then
        text="Choose a chest again"
        for _,entry in ipairs(all) do
            if entry.Binding.Chest==selected.Chest and entry.Binding.Slot==selected.Slot then text=RemoteCatalog.ChestTargetLabel(entry.Binding,all);break end
        end
    end
    RemoteCatalog.ChestSelectButton.Text=text.."  ▾"
    RemoteCatalog.ChestStats.CHESTS.Text=tostring(count)
    local learned=0
    for _,entry in ipairs(all) do if not entry.Binding.Generated then learned+=1 end end
    RemoteCatalog.ChestStats.SLOTS.Text=tostring(learned)
    RemoteCatalog.ChestStats["AUTO COLLECTED"].Text=tostring(RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoTotal or 0)
    if not RemoteCatalog.ChestBusy then RemoteCatalog.ChestWithdrawButton.Text=#all>0 and "Withdraw once" or "Learn chests first" end
    local learning=RemoteCatalog.Recorder and RemoteCatalog.Recorder.LearnChestOnly
    RemoteCatalog.ChestIntro.Text=learning and "Learning · withdraw once from each chest" or RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoEnabled and "Collecting · use OFF or Stop All to stop" or "Ready · learn chests, choose targets, then collect"
    RemoteCatalog.ChestLearnButton.Text=learning and "Stop learning" or "Start learning"
    RemoteCatalog.ChestLearnButton.BackgroundColor3=learning and COLORS.on or COLORS.off
end
track(RemoteCatalog.ChestSelectButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    local menu=RemoteCatalog.ChestSelectMenu
    if menu.Visible then menu.Visible=false;RemoteCatalog.LayoutChestControls();return end
    RemoteCatalog.CloseMenus()
    for _,connection in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.ChestMenuConnections) do connection:Disconnect() end
    table.clear(RemoteCatalog.ChestMenuConnections)
    for _,child in ipairs(menu:GetChildren()) do child:Destroy() end
    local list=Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    list.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1);list.BackgroundTransparency=1;list.BorderSizePixel=0
    list.ScrollBarThickness=5;list.CanvasSize=UDim2.new();list.AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    list.ZIndex=41;list.Parent=menu
    local targets=RemoteCatalog.ChestTargets()
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestWhole then
        local unique,seen={},{}
        for _,entry in ipairs(targets) do
            if not seen[entry.Binding.Chest] then seen[entry.Binding.Chest]=true;table.insert(unique,entry) end
        end
        targets=unique
    end
    menu.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,math.min(224,(#targets+1)*32))
    for i=0,#targets do
        local entry=targets[i]
        local option=button({Parent=list,Text=i==0 and (RemoteCatalog.ChestDiscover and "All matching chests" or "All learned chests") or RemoteCatalog.ChestTargetLabel(entry.Binding,targets),TextSize=12,
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,0,30),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(2,i*32)})
        option.ZIndex=42
        table.insert(RemoteCatalog.ChestMenuConnections,option.Activated:Connect(function()
            RemoteCatalog.SetChestAuto(false)
            RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration or 0)+1
            RemoteCatalog.ChestSelection=entry and {Chest=entry.Binding.Chest,Slot=entry.Binding.Slot} or nil
            menu.Visible=false;RemoteCatalog.LayoutChestControls();RemoteCatalog.UpdateChestSelectionUI()
        end))
    end
    menu.Visible=true
    RemoteCatalog.LayoutChestControls()
end))
function RemoteCatalog.WithdrawSelectedCarrots(generation)
    local targets=RemoteCatalog.SelectedChestTargets()
    local details,checked={},{}
    local sent,skipped=0,0
    for i,entry in ipairs(targets) do
        if not gui.Parent or generation and RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration~=generation then break end
        local chest=entry.Binding.Chest
        if not checked[chest] then
            local ready,reason=RemoteCatalog.ChestRequestReady(entry.Binding)
            checked[chest]={Ready=ready,Reason=reason}
        end
        local ready=checked[chest]
        local ran,ok,result=true,false,ready.Reason
        if ready.Ready then
            ran,ok,result=pcall(RemoteCatalog.WithdrawCarrots,nil,entry.Recorder,entry.Binding)
            if not ran then ok,result=false,ok end
        end
        if ok then sent+=1 else skipped+=1 end
        if #details<4 and (ready.Ready or not ready.Reported) then
            table.insert(details,"Check "..i..": "..(ok and "request sent" or RemoteCatalog.ChestResultText(result)))
        end
        ready.Reported=true
        RemoteCatalog.ChestWithdrawButton.Text="Stop · "..i.."/"..#targets
        if ready.Ready and i<#targets then task.wait(.6) end
    end
    if #targets==0 then return false,"Learn at least one chest first" end
    return sent>0,"Sent "..sent.."/"..#targets.." requests; "..skipped.." skipped or rejected. Check inventory. "..table.concat(details,"; ")
end
-- CHEST AUTO BEGIN
function RemoteCatalog.CarrotCount(item)
    local total=0
    for _,tool in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.OwnedTools()) do
        if tool.Name==(item or "carrotHarvested") then
            local count=RemoteCatalog.BaitCount(tool)
            -- Inventory confirmation needs exact whole-item totals.
            if type(count)~="number" or count<0 or count%1~=0 or count>9007199254740991-total then return nil end
            total+=count
        end
    end
    return total
end
function RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoMessage(text,color)
    if not gui.Parent then return end
    RemoteCatalog.ChestLastDetail="Reborn build-layers-48\n"..text
    RemoteCatalog.ChestResultLabel.Text=text
    RemoteCatalog.ChestResultLabel.TextColor3=color or COLORS.muted
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestResultView then RemoteCatalog.ChestResultView.CanvasPosition=Vector2.zero end
end
function RemoteCatalog.RecordChestRun(outcome,total,items,goal)
    local snapshot,names={},{}
    for item,count in pairs(items or {}) do snapshot[item]=count;table.insert(names,item) end
    table.sort(names)
    local text=outcome..": "..total.." confirmed items"..(goal and " · goal "..goal or " · unlimited")
    for _,item in ipairs(names) do text..="\n"..item..": "..snapshot[item] end
    local history=RemoteCatalog.ChestHistory or {}
    RemoteCatalog.ChestHistory=history
    table.insert(history,1,{Outcome=outcome,Total=total,Items=snapshot,Goal=goal,Text=text})
    while #history>5 do table.remove(history) end
    RemoteCatalog.ChestHistoryIndex=0
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestHistoryButton then RemoteCatalog.ChestHistoryButton.Text="Previous runs ("..#history..")" end
end
function RemoteCatalog.SetChestAuto(state)
    if not state then
        RemoteCatalog.Navigation.Stop("Chest")
        RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoEnabled=false
        RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoGeneration or 0)+1
        if gui.Parent then
            RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoButton.Text="AUTO WITHDRAW: OFF"
            RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoButton.BackgroundColor3=COLORS.off
        end
        return true
    end
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoEnabled then return true end
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoWorker or RemoteCatalog.ChestBusy then
        RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoMessage("Wait for the current chest request to finish")
        return false
    end
    local targets=RemoteCatalog.SelectedChestTargets()
    local states={}
    for _,entry in ipairs(targets) do
        local maximum=entry.Binding.Amount
        if type(maximum)=="number" and maximum>=1 and maximum%1==0 and maximum<=1000000 then
            table.insert(states,{Recorder=entry.Recorder,Binding=entry.Binding,Maximum=maximum,Amount=entry.Binding.Probe and 1 or maximum,Probe=entry.Binding.Probe,NoProgress=0})
        end
    end
    if #states==0 then
        RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoMessage(RemoteCatalog.ChestItems and next(RemoteCatalog.ChestItems)==nil and "No items selected. Choose items or All learned items." or "Learn and select at least one valid item withdrawal first")
        return false
    end
    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoGeneration or 0)+1
    local generation=RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoGeneration
    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoWorker=generation
    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoEnabled=true
    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoTotal=0
    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoItems={}
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestStats then RemoteCatalog.ChestStats["AUTO COLLECTED"].Text="0" end
    local goal=RemoteCatalog.ChestGoal
    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoButton.Text="AUTO WITHDRAW: ON"
    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoButton.BackgroundColor3=COLORS.on
    task.spawn(function()
        local ownsBusy=false
        local completed=false
        local goalReached=false
        local function active()
            return gui.Parent~=nil and RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoEnabled and RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoGeneration==generation
        end
        local ok,failure=pcall(function()
            local index=0
            local currentChest=nil
            local chestNumbers,chestCount={},0
            for _,state in ipairs(states) do
                local chest=state.Binding.Chest
                if chest and not chestNumbers[chest] then chestCount+=1;chestNumbers[chest]=chestCount end
            end
            while active() do
                if goal and RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoTotal>=goal then goalReached=true;break end
                local finished=0
                for _,state in ipairs(states) do if state.Done then finished+=1 end end
                RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoButton.Text="AUTO WITHDRAW: ON · "..finished.."/"..#states.." checks"
                if finished==#states then completed=true;break end
                index=index%#states+1
                if RemoteCatalog.ChestTravel=="Walk" and currentChest then
                    for candidate,state in ipairs(states) do
                        if not state.Done and state.Binding.Chest==currentChest then index=candidate;break end
                    end
                end
                local state=states[index]
                if state.Done then continue end
                local recorder,binding=state.Recorder,state.Binding
                local target="Chest #"..tostring(chestNumbers[binding.Chest] or index).." · slot "..tostring(binding.Slot or "?").." · "..tostring(binding.Item or "carrotHarvested")
                if not recorder:CanUseChestBinding(binding) then
                    state.Done=true
                    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoMessage("Skipping "..target..": its session or chest is no longer valid")
                    continue
                end
                local reached,reason=RemoteCatalog.ApproachChest(recorder,active,binding)
                if not active() then break end
                if not reached then
                    currentChest=nil
                    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoMessage(target.." waiting: "..tostring(reason))
                    task.wait(1)
                    continue
                end
                currentChest=binding.Chest
                local before=RemoteCatalog.CarrotCount(binding.Item)
                if before==nil then
                    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoMessage("Cannot read item inventory count. Use manual withdrawal.")
                    break
                end
                RemoteCatalog.ChestBusy=true;ownsBusy=true
                local amount=goal and math.min(state.Amount,goal-RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoTotal) or state.Amount
                state.Amount=amount
                local ran,sent,response=pcall(RemoteCatalog.WithdrawCarrots,amount,recorder,binding)
                if not active() then break end
                if not ran then sent,response=false,sent end
                local after=RemoteCatalog.CarrotCount(binding.Item)
                local elapsed=0
                while sent and active() and after~=nil and after<=before and elapsed<2 do
                    task.wait(.1);elapsed+=.1
                    if not active() then break end
                    after=RemoteCatalog.CarrotCount(binding.Item)
                end
                RemoteCatalog.ChestBusy=false;ownsBusy=false
                if not active() then break end
                if after==nil then
                    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoMessage("Item count became unreadable; automatic withdrawal stopped.")
                    break
                end
                local gained=math.max(0,after-before)
                if sent and gained>0 then
                    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoTotal+=gained
                    local item=binding.Item or "carrotHarvested"
                    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoItems[item]=(RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoItems[item] or 0)+gained
                    if RemoteCatalog.ChestStats then RemoteCatalog.ChestStats["AUTO COLLECTED"].Text=tostring(RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoTotal) end
                    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoMessage("Collected "..RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoTotal.." items · "..finished.."/"..#states.." checks finished\n"..target,COLORS.good)
                    if not state.Probe then state.Maximum=math.min(state.Maximum,state.Amount) end
                    state.Amount=state.Maximum;state.NoProgress=0;state.Probe=false
                    task.wait(.6)
                else
                    RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoMessage(target..": no inventory gain. "..RemoteCatalog.ChestResultText(response))
                    if state.Probe then
                        state.Done=true
                        task.wait(.2)
                    elseif state.Amount>1 then
                        state.Amount=math.max(1,math.floor(state.Amount/2))
                        task.wait(.6)
                    else
                        state.NoProgress+=1
                        if state.NoProgress>=3 then state.Done=true else task.wait(3) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        if ownsBusy then RemoteCatalog.ChestBusy=false end
        RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoWorker=nil
        if gui.Parent then
            local outcome=not ok and "Failed" or goalReached and "Goal reached" or completed and "Pass finished" or "Stopped"
            RemoteCatalog.RecordChestRun(outcome,RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoTotal,RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoItems,goal)
        end
        if RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoGeneration==generation then
            RemoteCatalog.SetChestAuto(false)
            if not ok then RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoMessage("Auto withdraw stopped: "..RemoteCatalog.ChestResultText(failure))
            elseif goalReached then RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoMessage("Goal reached: "..RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoTotal.." items collected. Remaining slots were not checked.",COLORS.good)
            elseif completed then RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoMessage("Pass finished: "..RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoTotal.." items collected. All "..#states.." selected slot checks finished; empty or unconfirmed slots were skipped.\nLast result: "..tostring(RemoteCatalog.ChestResultLabel.Text),COLORS.good) end
            local names,totals={},{}
            for item in pairs(RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoItems) do table.insert(names,item) end
            table.sort(names)
            for _,item in ipairs(names) do table.insert(totals,item..": "..RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoItems[item]) end
            if #totals>0 then RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoMessage(RemoteCatalog.ChestResultLabel.Text.."\nBy item: "..table.concat(totals,", "),RemoteCatalog.ChestResultLabel.TextColor3) end
        end
    end)
    return true
end
-- CHEST AUTO END
track(RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    RemoteCatalog.SetChestAuto(not RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoEnabled)
end))

track(RemoteCatalog.ChestWithdrawButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestAutoEnabled then return end
    if RemoteCatalog.ChestBusy then
        RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration or 0)+1
        RemoteCatalog.ChestWithdrawButton.Text="Stopping…"
        return
    end
    RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration or 0)+1
    local generation=RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration
    RemoteCatalog.ChestBusy=true
    RemoteCatalog.ChestWithdrawButton.Text="Stop collection"
    task.spawn(function()
        local ran,ok,reason=pcall(RemoteCatalog.WithdrawSelectedCarrots,generation)
        RemoteCatalog.ChestBusy=false
        if not gui.Parent then return end
        RemoteCatalog.ChestWithdrawButton.Text="Withdraw once"
        if RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration~=generation then return end
        local detail=ran and ok and "Withdrawal request sent. Check inventory; server response: "..RemoteCatalog.ChestResultText(reason)
            or "Withdrawal failed: "..RemoteCatalog.ChestResultText(ran and reason or ok)
        RemoteCatalog.ChestLastDetail="Reborn build-layers-48\nAmount: "..tostring(RemoteCatalog.ChestAmount).."\n"..detail
        RemoteCatalog.ChestResultLabel.Text=detail
        RemoteCatalog.ChestResultView.CanvasPosition=Vector2.zero
        RemoteCatalog.ChestResultLabel.TextColor3=ran and ok and COLORS.good or COLORS.warn
        setStatus(ran and ok and "Withdrawal requests sent; check inventory" or "Chest rejected the request. See the result below or copy it.",ran and ok and COLORS.good or COLORS.warn)
    end)
end))

function RemoteCatalog.TeleportSpotValid(spot)
    return spot~=nil and spot.PlaceId==game.PlaceId and (spot.Preset==true or spot.OwnerUserId==player.UserId and spot.JobId==game.JobId)
end
function RemoteCatalog.GetTeleportSpot(name)
    local preset=RemoteCatalog.TeleportPresets[name]
    if preset then
        if preset.PlaceId==game.PlaceId and not preset.CFrame then preset.CFrame=CFrame.new(table.unpack(preset.Values)) end
        return preset
    end
    if name=="Water hole" then return RemoteCatalog.WaterSpot end
    if name=="Previous" then return RemoteCatalog.TeleportPrevious end
    return RemoteCatalog.TeleportSpots and RemoteCatalog.TeleportSpots[name]
end
function RemoteCatalog.SetTeleportSpot(name,point)
    if RemoteCatalog.TeleportPresets[name] then return end
    if name=="Water hole" then RemoteCatalog.WaterSpot=point
    elseif name=="Previous" then RemoteCatalog.TeleportPrevious=point
    else
        RemoteCatalog.TeleportSpots=RemoteCatalog.TeleportSpots or {}
        RemoteCatalog.TeleportSpots[name]=point
    end
end
function RemoteCatalog.SaveTeleportSpot(name)
    if name~="Water hole" and name~="Island / home" and name~="Custom spot" then return false,"Choose a saved destination" end
    local character=player.Character
    local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not root or not root.Parent or not humanoid or humanoid.Health<=0 then return false,"Wait for your character before saving a position" end
    if baitBusy or fishingInputBusy then return false,"Wait for the current tool input to finish" end
    RemoteCatalog.SetTeleportSpot(name,{CFrame=root.CFrame,OwnerUserId=player.UserId,PlaceId=game.PlaceId,JobId=game.JobId})
    RemoteCatalog.TeleportGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.TeleportGeneration or 0)+1
    return true,name.." saved for this server"
end
function RemoteCatalog.SaveWaterSpot() return RemoteCatalog.SaveTeleportSpot("Water hole") end
function RemoteCatalog.TeleportSavedSpot(name)
    local spot=RemoteCatalog.GetTeleportSpot(name)
    if not spot then return false,name=="Previous" and "No previous teleport position yet" or "Save "..name.." first" end
    if not RemoteCatalog.TeleportSpotValid(spot) then
        if spot.Preset then return false,name.." preset is only available in PlaceId "..tostring(spot.PlaceId) end
        RemoteCatalog.SetTeleportSpot(name,nil)
        return false,"Saved spot belongs to another account or server; save it again"
    end
    local character=player.Character
    local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not root or not root.Parent or not humanoid or humanoid.Health<=0 then return false,"Wait for your character before teleporting" end
    if baitBusy or fishingInputBusy then return false,"Wait for the current tool input to finish before teleporting" end
    if root.Anchored then return false,"Your character cannot move right now" end
    local previous={CFrame=root.CFrame,OwnerUserId=player.UserId,PlaceId=game.PlaceId,JobId=game.JobId}
    RemoteCatalog.SetChestAuto(false)
    RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration or 0)+1
    setEnabled(false);setBaitEnabled(false)
    setFarmMode("crop",false);setFarmMode("wood",false);setFarmMode("mine",false)
    buildController:Stop()
    RemoteCatalog.Navigation.Stop()
    local ok,reason=pcall(function()
        root.CFrame=spot.CFrame
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity*0
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=root.AssemblyAngularVelocity*0
    end)
    if not ok then return false,"Teleport failed: "..tostring(reason) end
    RemoteCatalog.TeleportPrevious=previous
    RemoteCatalog.TeleportGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.TeleportGeneration or 0)+1
    local generation=RemoteCatalog.TeleportGeneration
    RemoteCatalog.TeleportLastResult="Teleport to "..name.." requested; checking arrival. Automation is paused."
    task.spawn(function()
        local function current()
            return gui.Parent~=nil and RemoteCatalog.TeleportGeneration==generation and player.Character==character
                and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")==root and root.Parent~=nil
        end
        local checked,distance
        for _,delay in ipairs({.25,.75,1}) do
            task.wait(delay)
            if not current() then return end
            checked,distance=pcall(function() return (root.Position-spot.CFrame.Position).Magnitude end)
            if not checked or distance>8 then break end
        end
        if not checked then
            RemoteCatalog.TeleportLastResult="Teleport requested; position check unavailable. Automation is paused."
        elseif distance>8 then
            RemoteCatalog.TeleportLastResult="Teleport corrected or target not reached. Try walking closer; no automatic retry."
        else
            RemoteCatalog.TeleportLastResult="Arrived at "..name..". Automation is paused."
        end
        if RemoteCatalog.TeleportStatus then RemoteCatalog.TeleportStatus.Text=RemoteCatalog.TeleportLastResult end
        setStatus(RemoteCatalog.TeleportLastResult,checked and distance<=8 and COLORS.good or COLORS.warn)
    end)
    return true,RemoteCatalog.TeleportLastResult
end
function RemoteCatalog.TeleportWaterSpot() return RemoteCatalog.TeleportSavedSpot("Water hole") end
-- WATER SPOT END
function RemoteCatalog.UpdateTeleportLabels()
    local char=player.Character
    local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    for name,card in pairs(RemoteCatalog.TeleportCards) do
        local spot=RemoteCatalog.GetTeleportSpot(name)
        local valid=RemoteCatalog.TeleportSpotValid(spot)
        local text=card.Preset and (valid and "Built-in destination" or "Available in PlaceId "..tostring(spot.PlaceId))
            or (valid and "Saved for this server" or "Not saved")
        if valid and root then
            local ok,distance=pcall(function() return (root.Position-spot.CFrame.Position).Magnitude end)
            if ok then text=text.." · "..math.floor(distance).." studs away" end
        end
        card.Label.Text=text
        card.Teleport.Text=card.Preset and name or valid and "Teleport" or "Save a position first"
        card.Teleport.BackgroundColor3=valid and COLORS.on or COLORS.off
    end
end


function RemoteCatalog.StopAllAutomation()
    RemoteCatalog.StopTimerDeadline=nil
    RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration or 0)+1
    RemoteCatalog.SetChestAuto(false)
    RemoteCatalog.StopChestLearning()
    RemoteCatalog.AntiAFK:SetEnabled(false)
    RemoteCatalog.PlantGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.PlantGeneration or 0)+1
    RemoteCatalog.Planting=false
    plantBtn.Text="Plant selected crop"
    buildController:Stop()
    movementController:Reset()
    cycleId += 1
    harvestCycleId += 1
    setEnabled(false)
    setBaitEnabled(false)
    setFarmMode("crop", false)
    setFarmMode("wood", false)
    setFarmMode("mine", false)
    setStatus("All automation stopped — all cycle tokens invalidated", COLORS.warn)
    refreshModeStatus()
    if stopAllButton then stopAllButton.Text = "ALL STOPPED ✓"; task.delay(1.5, function() if stopAllButton and stopAllButton.Parent then stopAllButton.Text = "STOP ALL AUTOMATION" end end) end
    RemoteCatalog.UpdateDashboard()
end
track(stopAllButton.Activated:Connect(RemoteCatalog.StopAllAutomation))

    if RemoteCatalog.BaitMouseInput or RemoteCatalog.FishingMouseInput then return end
    setEnabled(not enabled)
    refreshModeStatus()
end))

RemoteCatalog.CropControlPositions={}
function RemoteCatalog.SetCropDropdown(open)
    if open then RemoteCatalog.CloseMenus() end
    cropDropdownOpen=open
    cropDropdown.Visible=open
    local offset=open and cropDropdown.Size.Y.Offset+8 or 0
    for _,child in ipairs(harvestPage:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("GuiObject") and child~=cropDropdown then
            local base=RemoteCatalog.CropControlPositions[child]
            if not base and child.Position.Y.Offset>=160 then
                base=child.Position
                RemoteCatalog.CropControlPositions[child]=base
            end
            if base then child.Position=UDim2.new(base.X.Scale,base.X.Offset,base.Y.Scale,base.Y.Offset+offset) end
        end
    end
    refreshCropSelectionUI()
end
table.insert(RemoteCatalog.MenuClosers,function() RemoteCatalog.SetCropDropdown(false) end)

    RemoteCatalog.SetCropDropdown(not cropDropdownOpen)
end))

        if CONFIG.HarvestCrops[name] and #FarmLogic.SelectedCrops(CONFIG.HarvestCrops,CONFIG.HarvestCrop)==1 then
            setStatus("Keep at least one crop selected.",COLORS.warn);return

        CONFIG.HarvestCrops[name]=not CONFIG.HarvestCrops[name] or nil
        local selected=FarmLogic.SelectedCrops(CONFIG.HarvestCrops,CONFIG.HarvestCrop)
        CONFIG.HarvestCrop=selected[1];CONFIG.PlantType=selected[1]
        cropIndex=table.find(cropOptions,selected[1]) or 1
        savePreferences()
        refreshCropSelectionUI()
        settingsText.Text = string.format("Fish toggle: %s\nHarvest toggle: %s\nMine range: %d studs\nPlant type: %s\nHarvest crop: %s\nPlant radius: %d studs", CONFIG.ToggleKey.Name, CONFIG.HarvestKey.Name, CONFIG.MineRange, CONFIG.PlantType, CONFIG.HarvestCrop, CONFIG.PlantRadius)
    end))
end

    local busy=false
    track(replantToggle.Activated:Connect(function()
        if busy then return end
        busy=true
        local desired=not CONFIG.AutoReplant
        task.spawn(function()

            if not gui.Parent then return end

                CONFIG.AutoReplant=desired
                savePreferences()

            else setStatus("Auto-replant unchanged: "..tostring(reason),COLORS.warn) end
            replantToggle.Text=CONFIG.AutoReplant and "AUTO-REPLANT: ON" or "AUTO-REPLANT: OFF"
            replantToggle.BackgroundColor3=CONFIG.AutoReplant and COLORS.on or COLORS.off
            busy=false
        end)
    end))
end

-- Real expanding dropdowns matching the crop selector's visual style.
local function makeToolDropdown(page, anchor, options, kind, yPosition, onChoose)
    local menu = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    menu.Name = kind .. "Dropdown"
    menu.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, math.min(#options * 36, 180))
    menu.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0,#options * 36)
    menu.ScrollBarThickness = 4
    menu.ScrollBarImageColor3 = COLORS.accent
    menu.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    menu.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, yPosition)
    menu.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.panel
    menu.BorderSizePixel = 0
    menu.Visible = false
    menu.ClipsDescendants = true
    menu.ZIndex = 25
    menu.Parent = page
    corner(menu, 6)
    local list = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    list.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
    list.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    list.Parent = menu
    local buttons={}
    local function refresh()
        local selected=kind=="Axe" and woodToolPreference or mineToolPreference
        anchor.Text=kind..": "..selected..(menu.Visible and "  ▲" or "  ▼")
        for i,opt in ipairs(buttons) do
            local chosen=options[i]==selected
            opt.Text=(chosen and "✓  " or "    ")..options[i]
            opt.BackgroundColor3=chosen and COLORS.off or COLORS.panel
            opt.TextColor3=chosen and COLORS.accent or COLORS.text
        end
    end
    for i, optionName in ipairs(options) do
        local opt = button({Parent = menu, Text = optionName, TextSize = 12,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 34), Position = UDim2.new(), Bg = COLORS.panel})
        opt.LayoutOrder = i
        opt.ZIndex = 26
        buttons[i]=opt
        track(opt.Activated:Connect(function()
            onChoose(i)
            menu.Visible = false
            refresh()
        end))
    end
    table.insert(RemoteCatalog.MenuClosers,function() menu.Visible=false;refresh() end)
    track(anchor.Activated:Connect(function()
        local show=not menu.Visible
        RemoteCatalog.CloseMenus()
        menu.Visible = show
        refresh()
    end))
    RemoteCatalog.ToolMenuRefresh=RemoteCatalog.ToolMenuRefresh or {}
    RemoteCatalog.ToolMenuRefresh[menu]=refresh
    track(menu:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(refresh))
    refresh()
end

makeToolDropdown(woodPage, woodToolSelect, woodToolOptions, "Axe", 266, function(i)
    woodToolIndex = i
    woodToolPreference = woodToolOptions[i]
    savePreferences()
    woodStatus.Text = "Axe preference: " .. woodToolPreference .. ". Matching axe will be equipped before tree actions."
end)
makeToolDropdown(minePage, mineToolSelect, mineToolOptions, "Tool", 200, function(i)
    mineToolIndex = i
    mineToolPreference = mineToolOptions[i]
    savePreferences()
    mineInfo.Text = "Mining tool: " .. mineToolPreference .. ". Selected tool is equipped before mining."
end)
track(mineRangeBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
    CONFIG.MineRange = CONFIG.MineRange >= 120 and 30 or CONFIG.MineRange + 15
    mineRangeBtn.Text = "Mining range: " .. CONFIG.MineRange .. " studs  (+ / −)"
    savePreferences()
end))

track(delayButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    CONFIG.MineDelay = CONFIG.MineDelay >= 0.40 and 0.08 or math.min(0.40, CONFIG.MineDelay + 0.04)
    delayButton.Text = "Mine delay: " .. string.format("%.2fs", CONFIG.MineDelay) .. "  (click to adjust)"
    savePreferences()
end))
track(scanButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    CONFIG.MineScanInterval = CONFIG.MineScanInterval >= 1.0 and 0.15 or math.min(1.0, CONFIG.MineScanInterval + 0.15)
    scanButton.Text = "Scan interval: " .. string.format("%.2fs", CONFIG.MineScanInterval) .. "  (click to adjust)"
    savePreferences()
end))
track(saveButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    local saved = savePreferences()
    saveButton.Text = saved and "Preferences saved ✓" or "Save unavailable in this environment"
    saveButton.BackgroundColor3 = saved and COLORS.on or COLORS.warn
end))

track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
	if gameProcessed then
		return
	end
	if input.KeyCode == CONFIG.ToggleKey then
		setEnabled(not enabled)
	elseif input.KeyCode == CONFIG.HarvestKey then
		setHarvestEnabled(not harvestEnabled)
	end
end))

track(reset.Activated:Connect(function()
	local wasOn = stats.onSince ~= nil
	stats = newStats()
	if wasOn then
		stats.onSince = os.clock()
	end
	updateBoard()
end))

track(layoutBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
    local recorder=RemoteCatalog.LiveRecorder("HotbarBinding")
    local layout=recorder and recorder.HotbarLayout or "stonePickaxe,stoneAxe,fishingRod,coal,stone,fertilizerBasic,bait,sapling"
    local ok = setHotbarLayout(layout)
	if ok then
		setStatus("Hotbar layout request sent", COLORS.good)
	else
		setStatus("Change hotbar manually once with capture active, then retry", COLORS.bad)
	end
end))

track(plantBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
    if RemoteCatalog.Planting then return end
    RemoteCatalog.PlantGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.PlantGeneration or 0)+1
    local token=RemoteCatalog.PlantGeneration
    RemoteCatalog.Planting=true
    plantBtn.Text="Planting… use Stop All to cancel"
    setStatus("Planting "..CONFIG.PlantType.."...",COLORS.good)
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok,n=pcall(plantAroundPlayer,CONFIG.PlantType,token)
        if not gui.Parent or RemoteCatalog.PlantGeneration~=token then return end
        RemoteCatalog.Planting=false
        plantBtn.Text="Plant selected crop"
        if not ok then setStatus("Planting stopped: "..tostring(n),COLORS.warn)
        elseif n>0 then setStatus("Placement requests sent: "..n.." × "..CONFIG.PlantType,COLORS.good)
        else setStatus("No placement requests sent. Place a block manually first; check tool and range.",COLORS.warn) end
    end)
end))

--------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Live refresh + cleanup
--------------------------------------------------------------------
for mode,page in pairs({fishing=fishingPage,crop=harvestPage,wood=woodPage,mine=minePage}) do
    local bottom=0
    for _,child in ipairs(page:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("GuiObject") then bottom=math.max(bottom,child.Position.Y.Offset+child.Size.Y.Offset) end
    end
    local goal=RemoteCatalog.ModeGoals[mode]
    goal.PageButton=button({Parent=page,Text=goal.Button.Text,TextSize=12,
        Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,34),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,bottom+12)})
    track(goal.PageButton.Activated:Connect(function() RemoteCatalog.CycleModeGoal(mode) end))
end
updateBoard()

task.spawn(function()
	while gui.Parent do
		updateBoard()
		task.wait(1)
	end
end)

gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
    RemoteCatalog.StopTimerDeadline=nil
    for _,connection in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.ChestItemConnections) do connection:Disconnect() end
    table.clear(RemoteCatalog.ChestItemConnections)
    for _,connection in ipairs(RemoteCatalog.ChestMenuConnections) do connection:Disconnect() end
    table.clear(RemoteCatalog.ChestMenuConnections)
    RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.ChestManualGeneration or 0)+1
    RemoteCatalog.SetChestAuto(false)
    hub._destroyed=true
    hub._transitionGeneration=(hub._transitionGeneration or 0)+1
    hub._inPage=nil;hub._outPage=nil
    RemoteCatalog.PlantGeneration=(RemoteCatalog.PlantGeneration or 0)+1
    RemoteCatalog.Planting=false
    woodEnabled=false;mineEnabled=false
    RemoteCatalog.AntiAFK:Destroy()
    RemoteCatalog.Navigation.Stop()
    buildController:Destroy()
    movementController:Reset()
    if RemoteCatalog.Recorder then RemoteCatalog.Recorder:Stop() end
    for _, conn in ipairs(hub._connections) do conn:Disconnect() end
    hub._connections = {}
    if statsOverlayGui then statsOverlayGui:Destroy() end
    baitEnabled = false
    baitCycleId += 1
	enabled = false
	harvestEnabled = false
	cycleId += 1
	harvestCycleId += 1
	for _, conn in ipairs(connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(connections)
end)
